_G.__Skyr0Gen = (_G.__Skyr0Gen or 0) + 1

do
    local _flingJob = tostring(game.JobId)
    if _G.__EliteFlingJob ~= _flingJob then

        _G.__EliteFlingJob    = _flingJob
        _G.EliteFlingTP       = nil
        _G.EliteFlingAmp      = nil
        _G.EliteFlingCalm     = nil
        _G.EliteFlingMinRange = nil
        _G.__EliteFlashHolding  = nil
        _G.__EliteFlingCalm     = nil
        _G.__EliteFlingSettled  = nil
    end

    if _G.EliteFlingTP       == nil then _G.EliteFlingTP       = false end
    if _G.EliteFlingAmp      == nil then _G.EliteFlingAmp      = 1     end
    if _G.EliteFlingCalm     == nil then _G.EliteFlingCalm     = 45    end
    if _G.EliteFlingMinRange == nil then _G.EliteFlingMinRange = 200   end
    if _G.__EliteFlashHolding  == nil then _G.__EliteFlashHolding  = false end
    if _G.__EliteFlingCalm     == nil then _G.__EliteFlingCalm     = false end
    if _G.__EliteFlingSettled  == nil then _G.__EliteFlingSettled  = false end
end

local _gcSnap = {}
_G.__Skyr0GcEach = function(deep, visit)
    if type(getgc) ~= "function" then return false end
    local key = deep and "deep" or "shallow"
    local snap = _gcSnap[key]
    local objs
    if snap and os.clock() - snap.t < 10 then
        objs = snap.objs
    else
        local okGc, res = pcall(getgc, deep)
        if not okGc or type(res) ~= "table" then return false end
        objs = res
        local entry = { t = os.clock(), objs = objs }
        _gcSnap[key] = entry
        task.delay(10, function()
            if _gcSnap[key] == entry then _gcSnap[key] = nil end
        end)
    end
    local slice = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.Skyr0HeapSliceMs) or 2, 1, 16) / 1000
    local canYield = coroutine.isyieldable()
    local last, n = os.clock(), 0
    for _, v in pairs(objs) do
        if visit(v) then return true end
        n = n + 1
        if canYield and n % 64 == 0 and os.clock() - last > slice then
            task.wait()
            last = os.clock()
        end
    end
    return false
end

do

    local _syncJob = tostring(game.JobId)
    if _G.__SKYR0_SYNC_JOB ~= _syncJob then
        _G.__SKYR0_SYNC_JOB = _syncJob
        _G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer = nil
        _G.Skyr0SynchronizerReady = nil
    end

    local function looksLikeSync(t)
        if type(t) ~= "table" then return false end
        if typeof(t) ~= "table" then return false end
        local getCh = rawget(t, "GetTableFromChannel")
        local getAll = rawget(t, "GetAllChannels")
        return type(getCh) == "function" and type(getAll) == "function"
    end
    _G.Skyr0LooksLikeSync = looksLikeSync

    local function steal(deep)
        if type(getgc) ~= "function" then return false end
        local found
        _G.__Skyr0GcEach(deep, function(v)
            if type(v) == "table" and looksLikeSync(v) then found = v; return true end
        end)
        if not found then return false end
        _G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer = found
        _G.Skyr0SynchronizerReady = true
        return true
    end
    _G.Skyr0StealSync = steal

    local function stealFast()
        if type(filtergc) ~= "function" then return false end
        local ok, res = pcall(filtergc, "table", { Keys = { "GetTableFromChannel", "GetAllChannels" } }, true)
        if ok and type(res) == "table" and not looksLikeSync(res) then

            local hit
            for _, v in pairs(res) do if looksLikeSync(v) then hit = v; break end end
            res = hit
        end
        if not (ok and looksLikeSync(res)) then return false end
        _G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer = res
        _G.Skyr0SynchronizerReady = true
        _G.Skyr0SyncFromRequire = nil
        return true
    end
    _G.Skyr0StealSyncFast = stealFast

    local function haveRealSync()
        return looksLikeSync(_G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer) and not _G.Skyr0SyncFromRequire
    end

    if looksLikeSync(_G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer) then
        _G.Skyr0SynchronizerReady = true
    elseif not stealFast() then

        task.spawn(function()
            if looksLikeSync(_G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer) then return end
            local ok, mod = pcall(function()
                local pkgs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Packages", 10)
                local m = pkgs and pkgs:WaitForChild("Synchronizer", 10)
                return m and require(m) or nil
            end)
            if ok and looksLikeSync(mod) and not looksLikeSync(_G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer) then
                _G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer = mod
                _G.Skyr0SynchronizerReady = true
                _G.Skyr0SyncFromRequire = true
            end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()

            if haveRealSync() then return end
            local function deep()
                if haveRealSync() then return end
                if stealFast() then return end
                if steal(true) then _G.Skyr0SyncFromRequire = nil end
            end
            if type(_G.__Skyr0HeapSweep) == "function" then
                _G.__Skyr0HeapSweep("synchronizer-steal", deep, 6)
            else
                task.wait(1.5); deep()
            end
        end)
    end

    local _reqInFlight = false
    local function requireFallback()
        if _reqInFlight then return end
        _reqInFlight = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, mod = pcall(function()
                local pkgs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Packages")
                local m = pkgs and pkgs:FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")
                return m and require(m) or nil
            end)
            if ok and type(mod) == "table" then

                _G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer = mod
                _G.Skyr0SynchronizerReady = true
            end
            _reqInFlight = false
        end)
    end

    local _lastSteal, _stealInFlight = 0, false
    function _G.Skyr0SyncModule()
        local pre = _G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer
        if looksLikeSync(pre) then return pre end
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - _lastSteal < 0.5 then return nil end
        _lastSteal = now
        if type(getgc) == "function" and not _stealInFlight then
            _stealInFlight = true
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(steal, false)
                _stealInFlight = false
            end)
        end
        requireFallback()
        return nil
    end

    function _G.Skyr0SyncReady()
        return looksLikeSync(_G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer)
    end

    function _G.Skyr0SyncModuleBlocking()
        local pre = _G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer
        if looksLikeSync(pre) then return pre end
        if stealFast() then return _G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer end

        if type(getgc) == "function" and coroutine.isyieldable() then
            _G.Skyr0SyncModule()
            local w0 = os.clock()
            while os.clock() - w0 < 2 do
                task.wait(0.05)
                if looksLikeSync(_G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer) then return _G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer end
            end
        end
        local ok, mod = pcall(function()
            local pkgs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Packages")
            local m = pkgs and pkgs:WaitForChild("Synchronizer", 5)
            return m and require(m) or nil
        end)
        if ok and type(mod) == "table" then
            if not looksLikeSync(_G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer) then
                _G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer = mod
                _G.Skyr0SynchronizerReady = true
                _G.Skyr0SyncFromRequire = true
            end
            return _G.Skyr0PreloadedSynchronizer
        end
        return nil
    end

    function _G.Skyr0GetSyncChannel(name)
        if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then return nil end
        local mod = _G.Skyr0SyncModule()
        if not looksLikeSync(mod) then return nil end

        local canSwap = type(getthreadidentity) == "function"
            and type(setthreadidentity) == "function"
        local old
        if canSwap then
            local okId, id = pcall(getthreadidentity)
            if okId and type(id) == "number" then old = id else canSwap = false end
        end
        if canSwap then pcall(setthreadidentity, 8) end
        local ok, t = pcall(function() return mod:GetTableFromChannel(name) end)
        if canSwap then pcall(setthreadidentity, old) end
        if ok and type(t) == "table" then return t end
        return nil
    end
end

do
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local pkgs = RS:WaitForChild("Packages", 120)

            pkgs:WaitForChild("Synchronizer", 120)
            local datas = RS:WaitForChild("Datas", 60)
            require(datas:WaitForChild("Animals", 60))
            require(datas:WaitForChild("Mutations", 30))
            require(datas:WaitForChild("Traits", 30))
            local utils = RS:WaitForChild("Utils", 30)
            require(utils:WaitForChild("NumberUtils", 30))
            pkgs:WaitForChild("Net", 30)
        end)
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
        for _ = 1, 20 do
            pcall(function()
                local c = lp and lp.Character
                local h = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if h and h.Anchored then h.Anchored = false end
            end)
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)
end

if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end

_G.__LT = os.clock()

do
    local _lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local _TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local _showing = false
    local function _moggedPopup()
        if _showing then return end
        _showing = true
        local ok = pcall(function()
            local parent
            pcall(function() if type(gethui) == "function" then parent = gethui() end end)
            parent = parent or _lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
            local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            sg.Name = "Skyr0Mogged"
            sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
            sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            sg.DisplayOrder = 2000

            lbl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.32)

            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Text = "MOGGED"
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            lbl.TextSize = 64
            lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0

            sc.Scale = 0.6
            _TS:Create(sc, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = 1 }):Play()
            task.delay(0.7, function()
                pcall(function()
                    _TS:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                end)
            end)
            task.delay(0.9, function()
                pcall(function() sg:Destroy() end)
                _showing = false
            end)
        end)
        if not ok then _showing = false end
    end
    if _G.__Skyr0MoggedConn then pcall(function() _G.__Skyr0MoggedConn:Disconnect() end) end
    _G.__Skyr0MoggedConn = _lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        if _lp:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then _moggedPopup() end
    end)
end

_G.__Skyr0Stage = "preamble"
_G.__Skyr0Ready = false

_G.__Skyr0LPHOk = false
do
    local obfuscated
    local okRead = pcall(function() obfuscated = LPH_OBFUSCATED end)

    if okRead and obfuscated ~= nil then

        _G.__Skyr0LPHOk = true
    else
        local passthrough = function(...) return ... end
        local targets = {}
        local function collect(f)
            local ok, t = pcall(f)
            if ok and type(t) == "table" then targets[#targets + 1] = t end
        end
        collect(function() return getfenv(1) end)
        collect(function() return getfenv(0) end)
        collect(function() return (type(getgenv) == "function") and getgenv() or nil end)

        for _, name in ipairs({ "LPH_NO_" .. "VIRTUALIZE", "LPH_JIT_" .. "MAX" }) do
            for _, t in ipairs(targets) do
                pcall(function() t[name] = passthrough end)
                pcall(function() rawset(t, name, passthrough) end)
            end
        end

        local resolved
        pcall(function() resolved = getfenv(1)["LPH_NO_" .. "VIRTUALIZE"] end)
        _G.__Skyr0LPHOk = (type(resolved) == "function")
    end
end
_G.__Skyr0Stage = "lph stubs"

do

    local function chunkEnv()
        local ok, e = pcall(function() return getfenv(0) end)
        if ok and type(e) == "table" then return e end
        ok, e = pcall(function() return getfenv(1) end)
        if ok and type(e) == "table" then return e end
        return nil
    end

    local ENV
    do
        local ok, e = pcall(function()
            return (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or nil
        end)
        if ok and type(e) == "table" then ENV = e end
        if not ENV then ENV = chunkEnv() end
        if not ENV then ENV = _G end
    end

    local function get(name)
        local v
        pcall(function() v = ENV[name] end)
        if v == nil then
            local ce = chunkEnv()
            if ce then pcall(function() v = ce[name] end) end
        end
        return v
    end
    local function put(name, v)
        if v == nil then return end
        pcall(function() ENV[name] = v end)

        local ce = chunkEnv()
        if ce then
            local seen
            pcall(function() seen = ce[name] end)
            if seen == nil then
                pcall(function() ce[name] = v end)
                pcall(function() rawset(ce, name, v) end)
            end
        end
    end

    local function alias(name, ...)
        if type(get(name)) == "function" then return end
        local alts = table.pack(...)
        for i = 1, alts.n do
            local alt = alts[i]
            local v = (type(alt) == "string") and get(alt) or alt
            if type(v) == "function" then put(name, v); return end
        end
    end

    local function fromNamespace(ns, field)
        local t = get(ns)
        if type(t) == "table" then
            local v
            pcall(function() v = t[field] end)
            if type(v) == "function" then return v end
        end
        return nil
    end

    alias("identifyexecutor", "getexecutorname", "get_executor_name",
          function() return "Unknown", "0" end)
    local execName = "Unknown"
    pcall(function() execName = tostring((get("identifyexecutor"))()) end)
    _G.SKYR0_EXECUTOR = execName
    _G.SKYR0_IS_DELTA = (execName:lower():find("delta", 1, true) ~= nil)

    alias("getthreadidentity", "get_thread_identity", "getidentity", "getthreadcontext")
    alias("setthreadidentity", "set_thread_identity", "setidentity", "setthreadcontext")

    alias("request", "http_request",
          fromNamespace("http", "request"),
          fromNamespace("syn", "request"),
          fromNamespace("fluxus", "request"))
    alias("http_request", "request")

    alias("getcustomasset", "getsynasset", "getcustomasseturl",
          fromNamespace("syn", "getcustomasset"))

    alias("setfpscap", "set_fps_cap", "setfpsmax")
    alias("getfpscap", "get_fps_cap")

    alias("cloneref", function(o) return o end)
    alias("clonefunction", function(f) return f end)
    alias("compareinstances", function(a, b) return a == b end)

    if type(get("gethui")) ~= "function" then
        local cached
        put("gethui", function()
            if cached and cached.Parent ~= nil then return cached end
            local target
            pcall(function()
                local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
                local probe = Instance.new("Folder")
                probe.Parent = cg
                probe:Destroy()
                target = cg
            end)
            if not target then
                pcall(function()
                    target = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
                        :WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
                end)
            end
            cached = target
            return target
        end)
    end

    local _getupvalues
    pcall(function()
        if type(debug) == "table" and type(debug.getupvalues) == "function" then
            _getupvalues = debug.getupvalues
        end
    end)
    if not _getupvalues then
        local gu
        pcall(function()
            if type(debug) == "table" and type(debug.getupvalue) == "function" then
                gu = debug.getupvalue
            end
        end)
        if gu then
            _getupvalues = function(f)
                local t = {}
                for i = 1, 255 do
                    local ok, v = pcall(gu, f, i)
                    if not ok then break end
                    t[i] = v
                end
                return t
            end

            pcall(function() debug.getupvalues = _getupvalues end)
        end
    end
    alias("getupvalues",  _getupvalues)
    alias("getupvalue",   (type(debug) == "table") and debug.getupvalue  or nil)
    alias("setupvalue",   (type(debug) == "table") and debug.setupvalue  or nil)
    alias("getinfo",      (type(debug) == "table") and debug.getinfo     or nil)

    do
        local raw = get("getgc")
        if type(raw) == "function" then
            put("getgc", function(includeTables)
                local ok, res = pcall(raw, includeTables)
                if ok and type(res) == "table" then return res end
                if includeTables then
                    local ok2, res2 = pcall(raw)
                    if ok2 and type(res2) == "table" then return res2 end
                end
                return {}
            end)
        end
    end

    alias("setclipboard", "toclipboard", "writeclipboard", "set_clipboard",
          fromNamespace("syn", "write_clipboard"))
    alias("toclipboard", "setclipboard")
    alias("writeclipboard", "setclipboard")

    do
        local missing = {}
        for _, n in ipairs({ "getgc", "getconnections", "firesignal", "fireproximityprompt",
                             "getsenv", "getrenv", "loadstring", "setfenv", "hookfunction",
                             "writefile", "readfile", "isfile", "getcustomasset",
                             "request", "setclipboard", "setfpscap", "gethui" }) do
            if type(get(n)) ~= "function" then missing[#missing + 1] = n end
        end
    end

end
_G.__Skyr0Stage = "executor compat"

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

while not LocalPlayer do task.wait(); LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer end
UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")

pcall(function() CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
pcall(function() VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager") end)
_G.__Skyr0Stage = "services"

_G.SXEBuscaGui = function(nome)
    local alvo = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local r
    pcall(function() r = alvo and alvo:FindFirstChild(nome) end)
    if r then return r end
    pcall(function()
        local pg = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
            :FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        r = pg and pg:FindFirstChild(nome)
    end)
    return r
end

do
local _xchan
local _lastTry, _attempts = 0, 0
local _deepScans, _lastDeep = 0, 0
local _mod
local MAX_ATTEMPTS, RETRY_GAP = 60, 0.25

local SLOW_GAP = 2.0
local BOOT_T0, BOOT_BURST, BOOT_GAP = os.clock(), 6.0, 0.10
local MAX_DEEP, DEEP_GAP = 3, 1.5
local function _retryGap()
    if (os.clock() - BOOT_T0) < BOOT_BURST then return BOOT_GAP end
    if _attempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS then return SLOW_GAP end
    return RETRY_GAP
end
local function _channelCount(t)
    if type(t) ~= "table" then return 0 end
    local ok, hits = pcall(function()
        local h, n = 0, 0
        for _, v in next, t do
            n = n + 1
            if type(v) == "table" and type(rawget(v, "CacheTable")) == "table" then
                h = h + 1
            end
            if n >= 200 then break end
        end
        return h
    end)
    return (ok and hits) or 0
end

local function _module()
    if _mod and _G.Skyr0LooksLikeSync and _G.Skyr0LooksLikeSync(_mod) then return _mod end
    local m = _G.Skyr0SyncModule and _G.Skyr0SyncModule() or nil
    if type(m) == "table" then _mod = m end
    return _mod
end
local function _probe(mod)
    local gu = (debug and debug.getupvalue) or getupvalue
    if type(gu) ~= "function" then return nil, 0, nil end
    local best, bestN, where = nil, 0, nil
    local function consider(t, tag)
        local n = _channelCount(t)
        if n > bestN then best, bestN, where = t, n, tag end
    end
    local ok, up = pcall(gu, mod.Get, 9)
    if ok and type(up) == "table" then
        consider(rawget(up, 3), "Get/9/3")
        if bestN > 0 then return best, bestN, where end
        consider(up, "Get/9")
        if bestN > 0 then return best, bestN, where end
    end
    return nil, 0, nil
end
local function _deepScan(mod)
    local gu = (debug and debug.getupvalue) or getupvalue
    if type(gu) ~= "function" then return nil, 0, nil end
    local best, bestN, where = nil, 0, nil
    local function consider(t, tag)
        local n = _channelCount(t)
        if n > bestN then best, bestN, where = t, n, tag end
    end
    for fname, fn in next, mod do
        if type(fn) == "function" then
            for i = 1, 24 do
                local o, u = pcall(gu, fn, i)
                if not o then break end
                if type(u) == "table" then
                    consider(u, tostring(fname) .. "/" .. i)
                    for j = 1, 4 do
                        consider(rawget(u, j), tostring(fname) .. "/" .. i .. "/" .. j)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best, bestN, where
end
local function _chans()
    if _xchan then return _xchan end
    if (os.clock() - _lastTry) <= _retryGap() then return nil end
    _lastTry = os.clock()
    local mod = _module()
    if not mod then return nil end

    _attempts = _attempts + 1
    local found, n, where = _probe(mod)

    local deepBudget = (_attempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) and (MAX_DEEP + 6) or MAX_DEEP
    if not found and _deepScans < deepBudget and (os.clock() - _lastDeep) > DEEP_GAP then
        _lastDeep = os.clock()
        _deepScans = _deepScans + 1
        found, n, where = _deepScan(mod)
    end
    if found then
        _xchan = found
        _G.XenSyncDiag = string.format("bypassed via %s - %d channels (attempt %d)",
            tostring(where), n, _attempts)
    elseif _attempts == MAX_ATTEMPTS then
        _G.XenSyncDiag = "channels not found after " .. MAX_ATTEMPTS
            .. " fast attempts - still retrying every " .. tostring(SLOW_GAP) .. "s"
    end
    return _xchan
end

_G.XenSyncAll=function()return _chans()end
local _gtfcHit, _gtfcNext = {}, {}

_G.XenSyncGet=function(idx)
local t=_chans()
if t and idx~=nil then
local ok,cd=pcall(rawget,t,idx)
if ok and type(cd)=="table" then return cd end
local ok2,cd2=pcall(function() return t[idx] end)
if ok2 and type(cd2)=="table" then return cd2 end
end

if idx~=nil and type(_G.Skyr0GetSyncChannel)=="function" then
local key=tostring(idx)
local hit=_gtfcHit[key]
if hit~=nil then return hit end
local now=os.clock()
if now>=(_gtfcNext[key] or 0) then
_gtfcNext[key]=now+0.15
local ok3,cd3=pcall(_G.Skyr0GetSyncChannel,key)
if ok3 and type(cd3)=="table" then _gtfcHit[key]=cd3; return cd3 end
end
end
return nil
end

_G.sProp=function(ch,key)
if type(ch)~="table" or key==nil then return nil end
local ct=rawget(ch,"CacheTable")
if type(ct)~="table" then
local okC,c2=pcall(function() return ch.CacheTable end)
if okC and type(c2)=="table" then ct=c2 end
end
if type(ct)~="table" then return nil end
local v=rawget(ct,key)
if v~=nil then return v end
local okV,v2=pcall(function() return ct[key] end)
if okV then return v2 end
return nil
end

_G.Skyr0CachedSlotsForPlot=function(plotName)
    local c = (_G.SharedState and _G.SharedState.AllAnimalsCache)
           or (SharedState and SharedState.AllAnimalsCache)
    if type(c) ~= "table" then return nil end
    local out, n = {}, 0
    for _, a in ipairs(c) do
        if a.plot == plotName and a.slot and a.index then

            out[a.slot] = { Index = a.index, Mutation = a.mutation,
                            Traits = a.Traits or a.traitList }
            n = n + 1
        end
    end
    if n == 0 then return nil end
    return out
end

local _missAt = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
_G.Skyr0ChanHasData=function(ch)
    if type(ch)~="table" then return false end
    local ct=rawget(ch,"CacheTable")
    if type(ct)~="table" then
        local ok,c2=pcall(function() return ch.CacheTable end)
        if ok and type(c2)=="table" then ct=c2 end
    end
    return type(ct)=="table" and next(ct)~=nil
end
_G.Skyr0ChanMayGet=function(ch,key)
    if not _G.Skyr0ChanHasData(ch) then return true end
    local per=_missAt[ch]
    if not per then per={}; _missAt[ch]=per end
    local now=os.clock()
    if now<(per[key] or 0) then return false end
    per[key]=now+2
    return true
end
_G.Skyr0ChanGet=function(ch,key)
    if type(ch)~="table" or key==nil then return nil end
    local v=_G.sProp and _G.sProp(ch,key)
    if v~=nil then return v end
    if not _G.Skyr0ChanMayGet(ch,key) then return nil end
    local ok,v2=pcall(function() return ch.Get and ch:Get(key) end)
    if ok and v2~=nil then return v2 end
    return nil
end
_G._xenRawCT=function(plotName)
local c=_G.XenSyncGet(plotName)
if not c then return nil end
return rawget(c,"CacheTable")
end
local _AD,_MD,_TD
local function _data()
if _AD then return true end
local ok=pcall(function()
local d=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Datas")
_AD=require(d:WaitForChild("Animals"))
_MD=require(d:WaitForChild("Mutations"))
_TD=require(d:WaitForChild("Traits"))
end)
return ok and _AD~=nil
end

_G.Skyr0TraitData=function()
if not _data() then return nil end
return _TD
end

_G.Skyr0NormTraits=function(traits)
if traits==nil then return nil end
if type(traits)=="string" then return traits~="" and {traits} or nil end
if type(traits)~="table" then return nil end
local out={}

for _,v in ipairs(traits) do
if type(v)=="string" then
if v~="" then out[#out+1]=v end
elseif type(v)=="table" then
local n=v.Name or v.Id or v.Trait or v.TraitName or v.Type
if type(n)=="string" and n~="" then out[#out+1]=n end
end
end

if #out==0 then
for k,v in pairs(traits) do
if type(k)=="string" and k~="" and v~=false and v~=nil then out[#out+1]=k end
end
end
if #out==0 then return nil end
return out
end

_G._xenGen=function(index,mutation,traits)
if not _data() then return 0 end
local info=_AD[index]
if not info or not info.Generation then return 0 end
local mult=1
if mutation and mutation~="None" and mutation~="" then
local m=_MD[mutation]
if m and m.Modifier then mult=mult+m.Modifier end
end

local tl=_G.Skyr0NormTraits and _G.Skyr0NormTraits(traits) or traits
if type(tl)=="table" then
for _,tr in ipairs(tl)do
local t=_TD[tr]
if t and t.MultiplierModifier then mult=mult+t.MultiplierModifier end
end
end
return info.Generation*mult
end

_G._xenAnimReal = _G._xenAnimReal or nil
_G._xenAnimShim=setmetatable({GetGeneration=function(_,index,mutation,traits)return _G._xenGen(index,mutation,traits)end},{
__index=function(self,k)
local real=_G._xenAnimReal
if type(real)~="table" then
local ok,r=pcall(function()return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared"):WaitForChild("Animals"))end)
if not ok or type(r)~="table" then return nil end
real=r; _G._xenAnimReal=r
end
local v=rawget(real,k)
if v==nil then local ok2,v2=pcall(function()return real[k] end); if ok2 then v=v2 end end

if v~=nil then rawset(self,k,v) end
return v
end})

_G.Skyr0_GetPlotChannel=function(plotName)return _G.XenSyncGet(plotName)end
_G.Skyr0_GetAllPlots=function()return _G.XenSyncAll() or {} end
_G.Skyr0_GetPlotAnimalList=function(plotName)
local ct=_G._xenRawCT(plotName)
local al=ct and ct.AnimalList
return type(al)=="table" and al or nil
end

_G.SXE_GetPlotChannel=_G.Skyr0_GetPlotChannel
_G.SXE_GetAllPlots=_G.Skyr0_GetAllPlots
_G.SXE_GetPlotAnimalList=_G.Skyr0_GetPlotAnimalList

end

do
if _G.Skyr0SkipInFusePets == nil then _G.Skyr0SkipInFusePets = true end

_G.Skyr0IsInFuse = function(ad)
    if _G.Skyr0SkipInFusePets == false then return false end
    if type(ad) ~= "table" then return false end
    local m = ad.Machine
    return type(m) == "table" and m.Type == "Fuse"
end

_G.Skyr0SlotInFuse = function(plotName, slot)
    if _G.Skyr0SkipInFusePets == false or plotName == nil or slot == nil then return false end
    local al = _G.Skyr0_GetPlotAnimalList and _G.Skyr0_GetPlotAnimalList(tostring(plotName))
    if type(al) ~= "table" and _G.Skyr0ReadPlotData then
        local ok, d = pcall(_G.Skyr0ReadPlotData, tostring(plotName))
        al = ok and type(d) == "table" and d.AnimalList or nil
    end
    if type(al) ~= "table" then return false end
    local e = al[slot]
    if e == nil then e = al[tostring(slot)] end
    if e == nil then local n = tonumber(slot); if n ~= nil then e = al[n] end end
    return _G.Skyr0IsInFuse(e)
end
end

do
    if type(_G.Skyr0RemodelCleanup) == "function" then pcall(_G.Skyr0RemodelCleanup) end
    if _G.Skyr0RemoveMapModels ~= false then
        local FIXED = {}
        for _, n in ipairs({
            "Beehive", "CraftingMachine", "CrystalSpinWheel", "EclipseSpinWheel", "EventBoard", "FuseMachine",
            "IncubatorMachine", "LosTraders", "RobuxShop",
            "SoccerScoreBoard", "StPatricksPot", "TradePlazaPortal",
        }) do FIXED[n] = true end

        local KW    = { "fuse", "craft", "incubator", "shop", "spinwheel", "wheel", "roulette",
                        "trader", "plaza", "scoreboard", "cupid", "truck", "soccerball",

                        "jump", "egg", "easter", "bunny", "ltm", "obby", "parkour", "hunt", "event" }
        local AVOID = { "plot", "base", "portal", "admin", "sentry", "laser", "spawn", "terrain", "camera" }
        local EXTRA = { { "Events", "Bubblegum" } }

        local KEEP    = { ShopNPCRobuxIdle = true, Shop = true }
        local PARTIAL = { Shop = { ShopNPCCash = true } }
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")

        local stash = Instance.new("Folder"); stash.Name = "Skyr0RemodelStash"
        local removed, conns, alive = {}, {}, true
        local function isFake(i) return i:GetAttribute("Skyr0Fake") == true end
        local function wanted(c)
            if isFake(c) or Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(c) then return false end
            if c:IsA("Terrain") or c:IsA("Camera") then return false end
            local name = c.Name
            if KEEP[name] then return false end
            if FIXED[name] then return true end
            if name == "Events" then return false end
            if not (c:IsA("Model") or c:IsA("Folder")) then return false end
            local n = name:lower()
            for _, k in ipairs(AVOID) do if n:find(k, 1, true) then return false end end
            for _, k in ipairs(KW) do if n:find(k, 1, true) then return true end end
            return false
        end
        local function copyAttrs(src, dst)
            pcall(function() for k, v in pairs(src:GetAttributes()) do dst:SetAttribute(k, v) end end)
        end
        local function skeleton(src, dst)
            for _, c in ipairs(src:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("Folder") or c:IsA("Model") then
                    local n = Instance.new(c:IsA("Folder") and "Folder" or "Model")
                    n.Name = c.Name
                    copyAttrs(c, n)
                    if c:IsA("Model") then pcall(function() n.WorldPivot = c:GetPivot() end) end
                    skeleton(c, n)
                    n.Parent = dst
                end
            end
        end
        local function makeFake(real)
            local f
            if real:IsA("Folder") then
                f = Instance.new("Folder")
            elseif real:IsA("BasePart") then
                f = Instance.new("Part")
                f.Anchored, f.CanCollide, f.CanQuery, f.CanTouch = true, false, false, false
                f.Transparency, f.Size, f.CFrame = 1, Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), real.CFrame
            else
                f = Instance.new("Model")
                pcall(function() f.WorldPivot = real:GetPivot() end)
            end
            f.Name = real.Name
            copyAttrs(real, f)
            f:SetAttribute("Skyr0Fake", true)
            skeleton(real, f)
            return f
        end
        local function removeReal(real)
            if not alive or removed[real] or isFake(real) then return end
            local parent = real.Parent
            if not parent then return end
            local ok, fake = pcall(makeFake, real)
            if not ok then return end
            local rec = { fake = fake, parent = parent }
            removed[real] = rec
            local okMove = pcall(function() real.Parent = stash end)
            if not okMove then removed[real] = nil; pcall(function() fake:Destroy() end); return end
            fake.Parent = parent
            rec.anc = real.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
                if not alive or rec.restoring or not removed[real] then return end
                if real.Parent ~= stash and real.Parent ~= nil then
                    task.defer(function()
                        if alive and removed[real] and not rec.restoring and real.Parent ~= stash and real.Parent ~= nil then
                            pcall(function() real.Parent = stash end)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            rec.dead = real.Destroying:Connect(function()
                if rec.anc then rec.anc:Disconnect() end
                pcall(function() fake:Destroy() end)
                removed[real] = nil
            end)
        end
        local function realChild(container, name)
            for _, c in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if c.Name == name and not isFake(c) then return c end
            end
        end
        local function avoided(c)
            local n = c.Name:lower()
            for _, k in ipairs(AVOID) do if n:find(k, 1, true) then return true end end
            return false
        end
        local function sweepExtra()
            for _, path in ipairs(EXTRA) do
                local node = workspace
                for i = 1, #path - 1 do node = node and realChild(node, path[i]) end
                local leaf = node and realChild(node, path[#path])
                if leaf then removeReal(leaf) end
            end

            for cname, keepKids in pairs(PARTIAL) do
                local box = realChild(workspace, cname)
                if box then
                    for _, c in ipairs(box:GetChildren()) do
                        if not keepKids[c.Name] and not isFake(c)
                           and (c:IsA("Model") or c:IsA("Folder") or c:IsA("BasePart")) then
                            removeReal(c)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local ev = realChild(workspace, "Events")
            if ev then
                for _, c in ipairs(ev:GetChildren()) do
                    if not isFake(c) and not avoided(c) and (c:IsA("Model") or c:IsA("Folder") or c:IsA("BasePart")) then
                        removeReal(c)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        for _, c in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if wanted(c) then removeReal(c) end
        end
        sweepExtra()

        conns[#conns + 1] = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
            if not alive then return end
            if wanted(c) then removeReal(c) end
            task.defer(sweepExtra)
        end)
        local ev = workspace:FindFirstChild("Events")
        local function hookEvents(e)
            conns[#conns + 1] = e.ChildAdded:Connect(function() if alive then task.defer(sweepExtra) end end)
        end
        if ev then hookEvents(ev) end
        conns[#conns + 1] = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
            if alive and c.Name == "Events" and not isFake(c) then hookEvents(c) end
        end)

        for cname in pairs(PARTIAL) do
            local box = workspace:FindFirstChild(cname)
            if box then hookEvents(box) end
        end
        conns[#conns + 1] = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
            if alive and PARTIAL[c.Name] and not isFake(c) then hookEvents(c) end
        end)

        _G.Skyr0MapNames = function()
            local out = {}
            for _, c in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if not isFake(c) then out[#out + 1] = c.ClassName .. ":" .. c.Name end
            end
            local ev = realChild(workspace, "Events")
            if ev then
                local e = {}
                for _, c in ipairs(ev:GetChildren()) do e[#e + 1] = c.Name .. (isFake(c) and "(removed)" or "") end
            end
        end

        _G.Skyr0RemodelCleanup = function()
            alive = false
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            for real, rec in pairs(removed) do
                rec.restoring = true
                if rec.anc then rec.anc:Disconnect() end
                if rec.dead then rec.dead:Disconnect() end
                pcall(function() rec.fake:Destroy() end)
                if rec.parent and (rec.parent == workspace or rec.parent:IsDescendantOf(game)) then
                    pcall(function() real.Parent = rec.parent end)
                end
                removed[real] = nil
            end
        end
    end
end

do

if _G.Skyr0NoclipMachines == nil then _G.Skyr0NoclipMachines = (_G.Skyr0RemoveMapModels == false) end
if _G.Skyr0MachineFade == nil then _G.Skyr0MachineFade = 0.45 end

task.spawn(function()
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local NOCLIP_KW = _G.Skyr0NoclipKW or { "fuse", "craft", "incubator", "cupid", "truck", "soccerball",
                                            "shop", "wheel", "spin", "roulette",
                                            "trade", "plaza", "jump", "egg" }

    local AVOID_KW  = _G.Skyr0NoclipAvoidKW or { "plot", "base", "portal", "admin", "sentry", "laser", "board" }

    local FORCE_KW  = { "summerfuse", "summer base", "fuse", "wheel", "trade", "plaza", "jump", "egg" }

    local memo, memoN = {}, 0
    local function classify(name)
        local raw = name or ""
        local hit = memo[raw]
        if hit ~= nil then return hit or nil end
        local n = raw:lower()
        local res
        for _, k in ipairs(FORCE_KW) do if n:find(k, 1, true) then res = "noclip"; break end end
        if not res then for _, k in ipairs(AVOID_KW) do if n:find(k, 1, true) then res = "avoid"; break end end end
        if not res then for _, k in ipairs(NOCLIP_KW) do if n:find(k, 1, true) then res = "noclip"; break end end end
        if memoN >= 8000 then memo, memoN = {}, 0 end
        memo[raw] = res or false
        memoN = memoN + 1
        return res
    end

    local function isNpc(inst)
        if not inst:IsA("Model") then return false end
        if inst:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return true end
        return inst:FindFirstChild("Head") ~= nil
            and (inst:FindFirstChild("Torso") or inst:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                 or inst:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) ~= nil
    end

    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local function isPlayerChar(inst)
        return inst:IsA("Model") and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(inst) ~= nil
    end

    local function pinnedSet()
        local set = {}
        for _, n in ipairs(_G.Skyr0NoclipPaths or { "FuseMachine", "EclipseSpinWheel" }) do
            set[n] = true
        end
        return set
    end

    local function chainClassify(p)
        local chain, a = {}, p
        while a and a ~= workspace do table.insert(chain, 1, a); a = a.Parent end
        if not a then return nil end
        for _, node in ipairs(chain) do
            if isPlayerChar(node) then return nil end
        end
        if pinnedSet()[chain[1] and chain[1].Name] then return "noclip" end
        for i, node in ipairs(chain) do
            local cls = classify(node.Name)
            if cls then
                if cls == "noclip" and node.Name:lower():find("shop", 1, true) then
                    for j = i, #chain do if isNpc(chain[j]) then return nil end end
                end
                return cls
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local orig = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

    local function apply(obj, alpha)
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            local o = orig[obj]
            if not o then
                o = { t = obj.Transparency, c = obj.CanCollide, q = obj.CanQuery }
                if o.t == alpha then o.t = 0 end
                orig[obj] = o
            end
            if obj.CanCollide then obj.CanCollide = false end
            if obj.CanQuery then obj.CanQuery = false end
            if alpha > 0 and o.t < 1 then
                local target = o.t + (1 - o.t) * alpha
                if math.abs(obj.Transparency - target) > 0.01 then obj.Transparency = target end
            end
        elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
            local o = orig[obj]
            if not o then
                o = { t = obj.Transparency }
                if o.t == alpha then o.t = 0 end
                orig[obj] = o
            end
            if alpha > 0 and o.t < 1 then
                local target = o.t + (1 - o.t) * alpha
                if math.abs(obj.Transparency - target) > 0.01 then obj.Transparency = target end
            end
        end
    end

    local function applyTree(inst, alpha, spareNpcs)
        pcall(apply, inst, alpha)
        for _, c in ipairs(inst:GetChildren()) do
            if not (spareNpcs and isNpc(c)) and not isPlayerChar(c) then applyTree(c, alpha, spareNpcs) end
        end
    end

    local function alphaNow()
        return math.clamp(tonumber(_G.Skyr0MachineFade) or 0, 0, 0.95)
    end

    local function restoreAll()
        local snap = orig
        orig = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        for obj, o in pairs(snap) do
            pcall(function()
                obj.Transparency = o.t
                if o.c ~= nil then obj.CanCollide = o.c; obj.CanQuery = o.q end
            end)
        end
    end

    local pending, scheduled = {}, false
    local function drain()
        scheduled = false
        local batch = pending
        pending = {}
        if _G.Skyr0NoclipMachines == false then return end
        local alpha, n = alphaNow(), 0
        for obj in pairs(batch) do
            if obj.Parent and chainClassify(obj) == "noclip" then pcall(apply, obj, alpha) end
            n = n + 1
            if n % 150 == 0 then RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
        end
    end
    workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if _G.Skyr0NoclipMachines == false then return end
        if not (obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture")) then return end
        pending[obj] = true
        if not scheduled then scheduled = true; task.delay(0.15, drain) end
    end)

    local targets, lastDiscover = {}, 0
    local function discover()
        local out, walked = {}, 0
        local pins = pinnedSet()
        local function walk(container, depth)
            for _, c in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if (c:IsA("Model") or c:IsA("Folder") or c:IsA("BasePart"))
                   and not isPlayerChar(c) then
                    local cls = (depth == 0 and pins[c.Name]) and "noclip" or classify(c.Name)
                    if cls == "noclip" then
                        out[#out + 1] = c
                    elseif cls ~= "avoid" and depth < 5 then
                        walk(c, depth + 1)
                    end
                end
                walked = walked + 1
                if walked % 200 == 0 then RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
            end
        end
        pcall(walk, workspace, 0)
        targets = out
    end

    local boot, wasOn = os.clock(), false
    while true do
        if _G.Skyr0NoclipMachines ~= false then
            wasOn = true
            local every = (os.clock() - boot < 12) and 1 or 3
            if os.clock() - lastDiscover > every then lastDiscover = os.clock(); discover() end
            local alpha = alphaNow()
            for _, m in ipairs(targets) do
                if m.Parent then applyTree(m, alpha, m.Name:lower():find("shop", 1, true) ~= nil) end
            end
        elseif wasOn then
            wasOn = false
            targets = {}
            restoreAll()
        end
        task.wait(0.3)
    end
end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local WS = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local plots
    local t0 = os.clock()
    repeat
        plots = WS:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then task.wait(0.05) end
    until plots or (os.clock() - t0) > 25
    if not plots then return end
    local done, warmT0 = {}, os.clock()
    local _warmGap = 0.04
    while (os.clock() - warmT0) < 8 do
        local kids = plots:GetChildren()
        local pending = false
        for _, plot in ipairs(kids) do
            if not done[plot.Name] then

                local ch
                if _G.XenSyncGet then
                    local ord; pcall(function() ord = plot:GetAttribute("Order") end)
                    if ord ~= nil then ch = _G.XenSyncGet("Plot" .. tostring(ord)) end
                    if not ch then ch = _G.XenSyncGet(plot.Name) end
                end
                if ch then

                    if _G.Skyr0ChanHasData and _G.Skyr0ChanHasData(ch) then
                        done[plot.Name] = true
                    else
                        pcall(function() if ch.Get then ch:Get("AnimalList"); ch:Get("Owner") end end)
                        local al = _G.Skyr0ChanGet and _G.Skyr0ChanGet(ch, "AnimalList")
                        if al ~= nil then done[plot.Name] = true else pending = true end
                    end
                else
                    pending = true
                end
            end
        end
        if not pending and #kids > 0 then break end

        _warmGap = math.min(_warmGap * 1.3, 0.5)

        task.wait(_warmGap)
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local WS = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not WS.StreamingEnabled then
        return
    end
    local plots
    local t0 = os.clock()
    repeat plots = WS:FindFirstChild("Plots"); if not plots then task.wait(0.1) end
    until plots or (os.clock() - t0) > 25
    if not plots then return end
    local function plotPos(plot)
        local okp, pv = pcall(function() return plot:GetPivot().Position end)
        if okp and pv and pv.Magnitude > 1 then return pv end
        if plot.PrimaryPart then return plot.PrimaryPart.Position end
        local bp = plot:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        return bp and bp.Position or nil
    end
    local pending = 0
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        local pos = plotPos(plot)
        if pos then
            pending = pending + 1
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function() lp:RequestStreamAroundAsync(pos) end)
                pending = pending - 1
            end)
        end
    end
    local sw = os.clock()
    while pending > 0 and os.clock() - sw < 10 do task.wait(0.05) end
end)

do
  if not (_G.__Skyr0NetBuilt or _G.__SXENetBuilt) then

    _G.__Skyr0NetBuilt = true
    _G.__SXENetBuilt = true
    _G.__Skyr0Stage = "net folder wait"
    local netFolder = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages"):WaitForChild("Net")
    _G.__Skyr0Stage = "net builder"
    local getupvalues = debug.getupvalues or getupvalues
    local getinfo = debug.getinfo or getinfo

    local F1, F2, secret

    local function isGuid(s)
        return type(s) == "string" and #s == 36
            and s:match("^%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x$") ~= nil
    end

    local _bufFromString = buffer.fromstring or function(s)
        local b = buffer.create(#s)
        buffer.writestring(b, 0, s)
        return b
    end

    local _lastBuild, _buildFails = 0, 0
    local function build()
        if F1 and secret then return true end

        if _buildFails >= 4 then return false end
        if (os.clock() - _lastBuild) < 15 then return false end
        _lastBuild = os.clock()
        _buildFails = _buildFails + 1
        local jobId = game.JobId
        local a, b
        local cands, seen = {}, {}
        local _srcMatches = 0
        local _srcSamples = {}
        local _cloneFn = clonefunction or function(f) return f end
        if not getgc then
            return false
        end

        _G.__Skyr0GcEach(true, function(v)
            if typeof(v) == "function" then
                local ok, info = pcall(getinfo, v)
                if ok and info and info.source then
                    local src = info.source
                    local matched = (src == "=ReplicatedStorage.Packages.Net.Net")
                        or (src:find("ReplicatedStorage", 1, true) and src:find("Net", 1, true))
                    if matched then
                        _srcMatches = _srcMatches + 1
                        if #_srcSamples < 5 then _srcSamples[#_srcSamples + 1] = src end
                        local ok2, ups = pcall(getupvalues, v)
                        if ok2 and ups then
                            for _, up in pairs(ups) do
                                if type(up) == "table" then
                                    local x, y, z = rawget(up, 1), rawget(up, 2), rawget(up, 3)
                                    if not a and type(x) == "function" and type(y) == "function" and type(z) == "string" then
                                        a, b = _cloneFn(x), _cloneFn(y)
                                    end
                                    for _, e in pairs(up) do
                                        if isGuid(e) and e ~= jobId and not seen[e] then seen[e] = true; cands[#cands + 1] = e end
                                    end
                                elseif isGuid(up) and up ~= jobId and not seen[up] then
                                    seen[up] = true; cands[#cands + 1] = up
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        if not a then
            pcall(function()
                local children = netFolder:GetChildren()
                for i = 1, math.min(5, #children) do
                end
            end)
            return false
        end
        F1, F2 = a, b
        local cipher = F2("UseItem", jobId)
        if #cands == 1 then secret = cands[1];  return true end
        for _, sec in ipairs(cands) do
            local ok, h = pcall(function() return F1(_bufFromString(cipher), _bufFromString(sec .. jobId)) end)
            if ok and type(h) == "string" and netFolder:FindFirstChild("RE/" .. h) then secret = sec;  return true end
        end
        pcall(function()
            local children = netFolder:GetChildren()
            for i = 1, math.min(5, #children) do
            end
        end)
        return false
    end

    local _resolveWarned = {}
    local function resolve(prefix, name)

        local direct = netFolder:FindFirstChild(prefix .. "/" .. name)
        if direct then return direct end

        if not (F1 and secret) then
            if not _G.__Skyr0NetAllowBuild then
                if not _resolveWarned[name] then
                    _resolveWarned[name] = true
                end
                return nil
            end
            if not build() then
                if not _resolveWarned[name] then
                    _resolveWarned[name] = true
                end
                return nil
            end
        end
        local jobId = game.JobId
        local ok, h = pcall(function()
            local c = F2(name, jobId)
            return F1(_bufFromString(c), _bufFromString(secret .. jobId))
        end)
        if not ok or type(h) ~= "string" then
            if not _resolveWarned[name] then
                _resolveWarned[name] = true
            end
            return nil
        end
        local remote = netFolder:FindFirstChild(prefix .. "/" .. h)
        if not remote then
            if not _resolveWarned[name] then
                _resolveWarned[name] = true
            end
            return nil
        end
        return remote
    end

    local function getRemoteAny(name)
        for _, pfx in ipairs({"RE", "RF", "URE"}) do
            local r = resolve(pfx, name)
            if r then return r end
        end
        return nil
    end

_G.Net = {
        RemoteEvent           = function(_, name) return resolve("RE", name) end,
        RemoteFunction        = function(_, name) return resolve("RF", name) end,
        UnreliableRemoteEvent = function(_, name) return resolve("URE", name) end,
        GetRemote             = function(_, name) return getRemoteAny(name) end,
    }

    _G.Net.FireServer = function(_, name, ...)
        local r = getRemoteAny(name)
        if not r then return false end
        local args = {...}
        if r:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
            return pcall(function() return r:InvokeServer(table.unpack(args)) end)
        else
            return pcall(function() r:FireServer(table.unpack(args)) end)
        end
    end
  end
end

_G.__Skyr0HeapBusy = false
_G.__Skyr0HeapSweep = function(name, fn, maxWait)
    task.spawn(function()

        local cap = tonumber(maxWait) or 60
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while not game:IsLoaded() and os.clock() - t0 < cap do task.wait(0.25) end

        local delay = tonumber(_G.Skyr0HeapDelay) or 5
        local since = tonumber(_G.__LT) or t0
        while os.clock() - since < delay do task.wait(0.25) end

        local tp0 = os.clock()
        local function _tpBusy()
            if _G.__Skyr0CicloRodando then return true end
            local pend = _G.__Skyr0TPPending
            return type(pend) == "number" and (os.clock() - pend) < 45
        end
        while _tpBusy() and os.clock() - tp0 < 45 do task.wait(0.1) end

        local w0 = os.clock()
        while _G.__Skyr0HeapBusy and os.clock() - w0 < 60 do task.wait(0.1) end
        _G.__Skyr0HeapBusy = true

        local RunSvc = game:GetService("RunService")
        local tries = 0
        while tries < 5 do
            tries = tries + 1
            local clear = true
            for _ = 1, 20 do
                RunSvc.Heartbeat:Wait()
                if _tpBusy() then clear = false; break end
            end
            if clear then break end
            _G.__Skyr0HeapBusy = false
            local r0 = os.clock()
            while _tpBusy() and os.clock() - r0 < 45 do task.wait(0.1) end
            _G.__Skyr0HeapBusy = true
        end

        local ok, err = pcall(fn)
        _G.__Skyr0HeapBusy = false
    end)
end

_G.__Skyr0HeapSweep("net-hasher", function()

    if type(_G.Skyr0FindUseItem) == "function" then
        local okD, d = pcall(_G.Skyr0FindUseItem)
        if okD and d then return end
    end

    _G.__Skyr0NetAllowBuild = true
    pcall(function()
        if _G.Net and _G.Net.GetRemote then _G.Net:GetRemote("UseItem") end
    end)
    _G.__Skyr0NetAllowBuild = false
end)

do
    local _netFolder = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages"):WaitForChild("Net")
    local function _resolveAny(prefix, name)
        local direct = _netFolder:FindFirstChild(prefix .. "/" .. name)
        if direct then return direct end
        return nil
    end
    local function _getRemoteAny(name)
        for _, pfx in ipairs({"RE", "RF", "URE"}) do
            local r = _resolveAny(pfx, name)
            if r then return r end
        end

        if _G.Net and _G.Net.RemoteEvent then
            for _, pfx in ipairs({"RE", "RF", "URE"}) do
                local r = _G.Net[pfx == "RE" and "RemoteEvent" or (pfx == "RF" and "RemoteFunction" or "UnreliableRemoteEvent")]
                if r then
                    local ok, remote = pcall(r, _G.Net, name)
                    if ok and remote then return remote end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    if _G.Net then
        _G.Net.GetRemote = function(_, name) return _getRemoteAny(name) end
        _G.Net.FireServer = function(_, name, ...)
            local r = _getRemoteAny(name)
            if not r then return false end
            local args = {...}
            if r:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                return pcall(function() return r:InvokeServer(table.unpack(args)) end)
            else
                return pcall(function() r:FireServer(table.unpack(args)) end)
            end
        end

        local _origRE = _G.Net.RemoteEvent
        _G.Net.RemoteEvent = function(_, name)
            local r = _origRE and _origRE(_, name)
            if r then return r end
            return _getRemoteAny(name)
        end
    end
end

_G.Skyr0_FPS_CAP = _G.Skyr0_FPS_CAP or _G.SXE_FPS_CAP or 999
do
    local function applyCap()
        if not setfpscap then return end
        pcall(setfpscap, tonumber(_G.Skyr0_FPS_CAP) or tonumber(_G.SXE_FPS_CAP) or 999)
    end
    applyCap()
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            applyCap()
            task.wait(2)
        end
    end)
end

local function makeOneWay(plat)
    if not plat then return end
    local rsConn
    local lastY = nil
    rsConn = game:GetService("RunService").Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not plat or not plat.Parent then
            if rsConn then rsConn:Disconnect() end
            return
        end
        local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            local currentY = hrp.Position.Y
            if not lastY then lastY = currentY end

            local deltaY = currentY - lastY
            local isMovingUp = (hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y > 1) or (deltaY > 0.01 and deltaY < 5)

            if isMovingUp then
                plat.CanCollide = false
            else
                if currentY > plat.Position.Y + 0.1 then
                    plat.CanCollide = true
                else
                    plat.CanCollide = false
                end
            end

            lastY = currentY
        end
    end)
end

do
local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local secure_call
do
    local LAYERS = 2
    local TEMPLATE do
        local lines = {
            "return function(__renv, __func, ...)",
            "    setfenv(0, __renv)",
            ("    local function l%d(...) return __func(...) end"):format(LAYERS),
        }
        for c = LAYERS - 1, 1, -1 do
            lines[#lines + 1] = ("    local function l%d(...) return l%d(...) end"):format(c, c + 1)
        end
        lines[#lines + 1] = "    return l1(...)"
        lines[#lines + 1] = "end"
        TEMPLATE = table.concat(lines, string.char(10))
    end
    local _gti = getthreadidentity or get_thread_identity or getidentity
    local _sti = setthreadidentity or set_thread_identity or setidentity
    local _sentinel, _senv
    secure_call = function(func, mask, ...)
        if type(func) ~= "function" or typeof(mask) ~= "Instance" then return nil end
        if not (_gti and _sti and getrenv and loadstring and setfenv) then return nil end
        local renv = getrenv()
        if not _sentinel then
            local ok, env = pcall(getsenv, mask)
            if not ok or type(env) ~= "table" then
                env = setmetatable({ script = mask, _G = {}, shared = {} }, { __index = renv, __newindex = renv })
            end
            local loader = loadstring(TEMPLATE, "=" .. mask:GetFullName())
            if not loader then return nil end
            setfenv(loader, env)
            _sentinel, _senv = loader(), env
        end
        local level = _gti()
        local changed = {}
        local okU, snap = pcall(debug.getupvalues, func)
        if okU and type(snap) == "table" then
            for i, v in pairs(snap) do
                if typeof(v) == "Instance" and v:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") then
                    if pcall(debug.setupvalue, func, i, mask) then changed[i] = v end
                end
            end
        end
        _sti(2)
        local co, args = coroutine.create(_sentinel), table.pack(renv, func, ...)
        local response, err
        while true do
            local r = table.pack(coroutine.resume(co, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)))
            if not r[1] then err = r[2] break end
            if coroutine.status(co) == "dead" then
                response = table.pack(table.unpack(r, 2, r.n)) break
            end
            args = table.pack(coroutine.yield(table.unpack(r, 2, r.n)))
        end
        _sti(level)
        for i, orig in pairs(changed) do pcall(debug.setupvalue, func, i, orig) end
        if err or not response then return nil end
        return table.unpack(response, 1, response.n)
    end
end
_G.secure_call = secure_call

local _sc_mask
local function _sc_getMask()
    if _sc_mask and _sc_mask.Parent then return _sc_mask end
    local c = RS:FindFirstChild("Controllers") or RS:WaitForChild("Controllers", 10)
    _sc_mask = c and (c:FindFirstChild("PlotController") or c:WaitForChild("PlotController", 10))
    return _sc_mask
end
_G.VanishGetMask = _sc_getMask
end
_G.__Skyr0Stage = "secure_call"

_G.Skyr0GrappleValue = _G.Skyr0GrappleValue or 0.33
_G.__Skyr0UseItemPayload = _G.__Skyr0UseItemPayload or nil
do
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local netFolder = RS:WaitForChild("Packages"):WaitForChild("Net")
    local PAYLOAD_FILE = "renzo_grapple_payload.txt"

    local function isHashName(nm)
        return type(nm) == "string"
            and (nm:match("^RE/%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x") or nm:match("^RF/%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x")) ~= nil
    end
    local function isHashRemote(ch, className)
        return ch and ch:IsA(className) and isHashName(ch.Name)
    end

    local _dualCache
    local function findUseItemByDualHash()
        if _dualCache and _dualCache.Parent then return _dualCache end
        local reH, rfH = {}, {}
        for _, ch in ipairs(netFolder:GetChildren()) do
            local nm = ch.Name
            if type(nm) == "string" then
                local pfx, rest = nm:match("^(R[EF])/(.+)$")
                if pfx and rest and #rest >= 32 and rest:match("^%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x") then
                    if pfx == "RE" then reH[rest] = ch else rfH[rest] = ch end
                end
            end
        end
        local found, count = nil, 0
        for h, ch in pairs(reH) do
            if rfH[h] then count = count + 1; found = ch end
        end
        if count == 1 then _dualCache = found; return found end
        return nil
    end

    local PlayersSvc = game:GetService("Players")
    local _hookCache, _hookLastTry, _hookAmbigWarned = nil, 0, false

    local function isNetRemote(c)
        return c and (c:IsA("RemoteEvent") or c:IsA("RemoteFunction")) and isHashName(c.Name)
    end
    local function asNetRemote(v)
        if typeof(v) == "Instance" then
            if v.Parent == netFolder and isNetRemote(v) then return v end
            return nil
        end
        if type(v) == "string" and #v >= 8 and #v < 200 then
            local c = netFolder:FindFirstChild(v) or netFolder:FindFirstChild("RE/" .. v)
                or netFolder:FindFirstChild("RF/" .. v)
            if isNetRemote(c) then return c end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function findUseItemRF()
        local c = netFolder:FindFirstChild("RF/UseItem")
        if c and c:IsA("RemoteFunction") then return c end
        return nil
    end
    _G.Skyr0FindUseItemRF = findUseItemRF

    local _getUps = (debug and debug.getupvalues) or getupvalues
    local _getConsts = (debug and debug.getconstants) or getconstants
    local function scanValue(v, found, seen, budget, depth)
        if budget.n <= 0 then return end
        budget.n = budget.n - 1
        local r = asNetRemote(v)
        if r then found[r] = true; return end
        local tv = type(v)
        if depth <= 0 or (tv ~= "table" and tv ~= "function") or seen[v] then return end
        seen[v] = true
        if tv == "table" then
            pcall(function()
                for _, x in next, v do
                    scanValue(x, found, seen, budget, depth - 1)
                    if budget.n <= 0 then break end
                end
            end)
        else
            if _getUps then
                local ok, ups = pcall(_getUps, v)
                if ok and type(ups) == "table" then
                    for _, u in next, ups do scanValue(u, found, seen, budget, depth - 1) end
                end
            end
            if _getConsts then
                local ok, cs = pcall(_getConsts, v)
                if ok and type(cs) == "table" then
                    for _, c in next, cs do
                        if type(c) == "string" then scanValue(c, found, seen, budget, 0) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function getHookScript()
        local plr = PlayersSvc.LocalPlayer
        local char = plr and plr.Character
        local tool = char and char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")
        if not tool then

            local bp = plr and plr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            tool = bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")
        end
        return tool and tool:FindFirstChild("HookScript"), tool
    end

    local function ownedBy(fn, hs)

        local ok, e = pcall(getfenv, fn)
        if not ok or type(e) ~= "table" then return true end
        local okS, s = pcall(function() return e.script end)
        return not (okS and typeof(s) == "Instance" and s ~= hs)
    end

    local function findUseItemViaHookScript(deep)
        if _hookCache and _hookCache.Parent then return _hookCache end
        _hookCache = nil

        local now = os.clock()
        if not deep and now - _hookLastTry < 1 then return nil end
        _hookLastTry = now

        local hs, tool = getHookScript()
        if not hs then return nil end
        local found, seen, budget = {}, { [_G] = true, [shared] = true }, { n = deep and 20000 or 3000 }

        if getsenv then
            local ok, env = pcall(getsenv, hs)
            if ok and type(env) == "table" then scanValue(env, found, seen, budget, 3) end
        end

        if getconnections and tool then
            for _, sig in ipairs({ "Activated", "Equipped", "Unequipped" }) do
                local ok, conns = pcall(function() return getconnections(tool[sig]) end)
                if ok and type(conns) == "table" then
                    for _, cn in ipairs(conns) do
                        local okF, fn = pcall(function() return cn.Function end)
                        if okF and type(fn) == "function" and ownedBy(fn, hs) then
                            scanValue(fn, found, seen, budget, 3)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if getscriptclosure then
            local ok, fn = pcall(getscriptclosure, hs)
            if ok and type(fn) == "function" then scanValue(fn, found, seen, budget, 3) end
        end

        if deep and getgc and next(found) == nil then
            pcall(function()
                _G.__Skyr0GcEach(false, function(f)
                    if type(f) == "function" then
                        local okF, e = pcall(getfenv, f)
                        if okF and type(e) == "table" then
                            local okS, s = pcall(function() return e.script end)
                            if okS and s == hs then scanValue(f, found, seen, budget, 2) end
                        end
                    end
                    if budget.n <= 0 then return true end
                end)
            end)
        end

        local list = {}
        for r in pairs(found) do list[#list + 1] = r end
        if #list == 1 then _hookCache = list[1]; return list[1] end
        if #list > 1 then

            local d = findUseItemByDualHash()
            if d and found[d] then _hookCache = d; return d end
            if not _hookAmbigWarned then
                _hookAmbigWarned = true
                local names = {}
                for _, r in ipairs(list) do names[#names + 1] = r.Name end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    _G.Skyr0FindGrappleRemote = findUseItemViaHookScript

    local function findUseItem()
        local rf = findUseItemRF()
        if rf then return rf end
        local ok, r = pcall(findUseItemViaHookScript, false)
        if ok and r then return r end
        return findUseItemByDualHash()
    end
    _G.Skyr0FindUseItem = findUseItem

    local function resolveHashed(name, className)
        className = className or "RemoteEvent"
        if name == "UseItem" then

            local d = findUseItem()
            if d then return d end
        end
        local prefix = (className == "RemoteFunction") and "RF/" or "RE/"

        local children = netFolder:GetChildren()
        local aliasIdx
        for i, ch in ipairs(children) do
            if ch.Name == prefix .. name and ch:IsA(className) then aliasIdx = i; break end
        end
        if aliasIdx then
            if isHashRemote(children[aliasIdx - 1], className) then return children[aliasIdx - 1] end
            if isHashRemote(children[aliasIdx + 1], className) then return children[aliasIdx + 1] end
        end

        return nil
    end
    _G.Skyr0ResolveHashed = resolveHashed

    _G.__Skyr0RemoteCache = _G.__Skyr0RemoteCache or {}
    _G.__Skyr0RemoteJob   = _G.__Skyr0RemoteJob or game.JobId

    _G.Skyr0GetRemote = function(name, className)
        if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then return nil, false end
        className = className or "RemoteEvent"

        if _G.__Skyr0RemoteJob ~= game.JobId then
            _G.__Skyr0RemoteCache = {}
            _G.__Skyr0RemoteJob = game.JobId
        end
        local ck = className .. "|" .. name
        local hit = _G.__Skyr0RemoteCache[ck]
        if hit and typeof(hit) == "Instance" and hit.Parent then return hit, true end

        local prefix = (className == "RemoteFunction") and "RF/" or "RE/"

        local found = netFolder:FindFirstChild(prefix .. name)
        if found and not found:IsA(className) then found = nil end
        if not found then
            local bare = netFolder:FindFirstChild(name)
            if bare and bare:IsA(className) then found = bare end
        end

        if name == "UseItem" then
            local okD, d = pcall(findUseItem)
            if okD and typeof(d) == "Instance" and d:IsA(className) then found = d end
        end

        if found then

            if isHashName(found.Name) or found.Name:find("-", 1, true) then
                _G.__Skyr0RemoteCache[ck] = found
                return found, true
            end
            return found, false
        end
        return nil, false
    end

    _G.Skyr0FireRemote = function(name, ...)
        local r, real = _G.Skyr0GetRemote(name, "RemoteEvent")
        if not r or not real then return false end
        local args = table.pack(...)
        return (pcall(function() r:FireServer(table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)) end))
    end

    _G.Skyr0InvokeRemote = function(name, ...)
        local r, real = _G.Skyr0GetRemote(name, "RemoteFunction")
        if not r or not real then return false, nil end
        local args = table.pack(...)
        local ok, res = pcall(function() return r:InvokeServer(table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)) end)
        return ok, res
    end

    local function loadPersistedPayload()
        if _G.Skyr0_GRAPPLE_PAYLOAD then return _G.Skyr0_GRAPPLE_PAYLOAD end
        if not (readfile and isfile) then return nil end
        local ok, res = pcall(function()
            if not isfile(PAYLOAD_FILE) then return nil end
            local raw = readfile(PAYLOAD_FILE)
            local lines = {}
            for line in raw:gmatch("[^\n]+") do lines[#lines + 1] = line end
            local n = tonumber(lines[1]); if not n then return nil end
            local p = { n = n }
            for i = 1, n do
                local ln = lines[i + 1] or ""
                local tag, rest = ln:sub(1, 2), ln:sub(3)
                if tag == "s:" then p[i] = rest
                elseif tag == "n:" then p[i] = tonumber(rest)
                elseif tag == "b:" then p[i] = (rest == "true")
                else p[i] = nil end
            end
            return p
        end)
        return ok and res or nil
    end
    _G.__Skyr0UseItemPayload = loadPersistedPayload()

    task.spawn(function()
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - t0 < 60 do
            local r = findUseItem()
            if r then
                return
            end
            task.wait(0.25)
        end

        if getgc and getHookScript() then
            _G.__Skyr0HeapSweep("hookscript-remote", function()
                local ok, r = pcall(findUseItemViaHookScript, true)
                if ok and r then  end
            end)
        end
    end)

    _G.Skyr0BuildPMRefs = function(deep)
        local refs = _G.__Skyr0PMRefs or {}
        local vistos = {}
        for _, t in ipairs(refs) do vistos[t] = true end
        local function juntar(t)
            if type(t) == "table" and not vistos[t] then
                vistos[t] = true; refs[#refs + 1] = t
            end
        end
        pcall(function()
            juntar(require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Packages.PlayerMouse))
        end)
        _G.__Skyr0PMRefs = refs
        if deep and not _G.__Skyr0PMRefsDeep and getgc then
            pcall(function()
                _G.__Skyr0GcEach(true, function(v)
                    if type(v) == "table" and typeof(rawget(v, "Hit")) == "CFrame" then
                        local n = 0
                        for _ in pairs(v) do n = n + 1 end
                        if n <= 3 then juntar(v) end
                    end
                end)
            end)
            _G.__Skyr0PMRefsDeep = true
        end
        return refs
    end

    _G.__Skyr0HeapSweep("playermouse-refs", function()
        _G.Skyr0BuildPMRefs(true)
    end, 4)

    _G.__Skyr0GrappleDisparar = function(tool, pos, alvo, char, RSv)

        local refs = _G.__Skyr0PMRefs
        if not refs or #refs == 0 then refs = _G.Skyr0BuildPMRefs(false) end

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local camAntes = cam and cam.CFrame
        local camNova, camRot
        if cam then
            pcall(function()
                local m = game:GetService("UserInputService"):GetMouseLocation()
                local raio = cam:ViewportPointToRay(m.X, m.Y)
                local L = cam.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(raio.Direction).Unit
                local d = pos - cam.CFrame.Position
                if d.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                    d = d.Unit

                    camRot = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, d)
                           * CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, L):Inverse()
                    camNova = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position) * camRot
                end
            end)
        end

        local mira = CFrame.new(pos)
        local antes = {}
        for i, t in ipairs(refs) do
            antes[i] = { rawget(t, "Hit"), rawget(t, "Target") }
            pcall(function() t.Hit = mira; t.Target = alvo end)
        end
        if camNova then cam.CFrame = camNova end

        local function cancelPull()
            if _G.Skyr0GrappleKeepPull then return end
            pcall(function()
                for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("Sound") then d.Volume = 0; d:Stop()
                    elseif d:IsA("Beam") then d.Enabled = false; d.Attachment0 = nil end
                end
                local hrpC = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local fp = hrpC and hrpC:FindFirstChild("FlightPower")
                if fp then fp:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
        cancelPull()

        local ok = pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
        if not ok then ok = pcall(function() firesignal(tool.Activated) end) end

        if ok then _G.Skyr0GrappleFiredAt = os.clock() end
        cancelPull()

        task.spawn(function()
            local RSvc = game:GetService("RunService")
            local tCancel = os.clock()
            for _ = 1, 4 do
                if camRot and cam then cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position) * camRot end
                for _, t in ipairs(refs) do
                    pcall(function() t.Hit = mira; t.Target = alvo end)
                end
                cancelPull()
                RSvc.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end

            local cancelConn
            cancelConn = RSvc.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                cancelPull()
                if os.clock() - tCancel > 0.6 then cancelConn:Disconnect() end
            end)
            if camRot and cam and camAntes then
                cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position) * (camAntes - camAntes.Position)
            end
            for i, t in ipairs(refs) do
                local a = antes[i]
                pcall(function() t.Hit = a[1]; t.Target = a[2] end)
            end
        end)

        return ok
    end

    _G.Skyr0FireGrappleTool = function(value, exato)

        local RSv = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local Plrs = game:GetService("Players")
        local eu = Plrs.LocalPlayer
        local char = eu and eu.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return false end

        if eu:GetAttribute("Stealing") then return false end

        local tool = char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")
        if not tool then
            local bp = eu:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            local t2 = bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not t2 or not hum then return false end
            pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(t2) end)

            local t0 = os.clock()
            repeat
                game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Wait()
                char = eu.Character
                tool = char and char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")
            until tool or os.clock() - t0 > 0.5
            if not tool then return false end
            hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then return false end
        end

        local par0 = RaycastParams.new()
        par0.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        par0.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }

        if exato and typeof(value) == "Vector3" then
            local pos = value
            local m = (pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude
            if m < 10 or m > 50 then return false end

            local alvo
