                   and p.Z > h.minZ - pad and p.Z < h.maxZ + pad
                   and p.Y < h.top + 2 and p.Y > h.bottom - 8
            end

            local function segHits(a, b, x0, x1, z0, z1)
                local t0, t1 = 0, 1
                local dx, dz = b.X - a.X, b.Z - a.Z
                local P = { -dx, dx, -dz, dz }
                local Q = { a.X - x0, x1 - a.X, a.Z - z0, z1 - a.Z }
                for i = 1, 4 do
                    local p, q = P[i], Q[i]
                    if p == 0 then
                        if q <= 0 then return false end
                    else
                        local t = q / p
                        if p < 0 then
                            if t > t1 then return false end
                            if t > t0 then t0 = t end
                        else
                            if t < t0 then return false end
                            if t < t1 then t1 = t end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return (t1 - t0) > 1e-4
            end

            local function allHulls()
                local list, seen = {}, {}
                local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                if plots then
                    for _, p in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                        seen[p] = true
                        local h = H.of(p)
                        if h and h.n and h.n > 0 and h.minX then list[#list + 1] = h end
                    end
                end
                if type(_G.Skyr0StashedPlots) == "function" then
                    local ok, st = pcall(_G.Skyr0StashedPlots)
                    if ok and type(st) == "table" then
                        for _, p in ipairs(st) do
                            if not seen[p] then
                                local h = H.of(p)
                                if h and h.n and h.n > 0 and h.minX then list[#list + 1] = h end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return list
            end

            local function doorPair(h, y, M)
                local n, d = h.nrm, h.door
                local inner, outer
                if n.X ~= 0 then
                    local face = n.X > 0 and h.maxX or h.minX
                    inner = Vector3.new(face - n.X * 8, y, d.Z)
                    outer = Vector3.new(face + n.X * (M + 4), y, d.Z)
                else
                    local face = n.Z > 0 and h.maxZ or h.minZ
                    inner = Vector3.new(d.X, y, face - n.Z * 8)
                    outer = Vector3.new(d.X, y, face + n.Z * (M + 4))
                end
                return inner, outer
            end

            function H.plan(fromPos, toPos, facingDir)
                if _G.Skyr0PlotWrap == false then return nil end
                local hulls = allHulls()
                if #hulls == 0 then return nil end
                local M = tonumber(_G.Skyr0HullMargin) or 6

                local startH, endH
                for _, h in ipairs(hulls) do
                    if not startH and H.contains(h, fromPos, 0) then startH = h end
                    if not endH and H.contains(h, toPos, 0) then endH = h end
                end
                if startH and startH == endH then return nil end

                local pre, post = {}, {}
                local S, E = fromPos, toPos

                if startH then
                    local inner, outer = doorPair(startH, fromPos.Y, M)
                    if not _clear(fromPos, outer) then pre[#pre + 1] = inner end
                    pre[#pre + 1] = outer
                    S = outer
                end

                if facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1 then

                    local f = Vector3.new(facingDir.X, 0, facingDir.Z).Unit
                    local q = toPos - f * 14
                    for k = 0, 60, 2 do
                        q = toPos - f * (14 + k)
                        local inside = false
                        for _, h in ipairs(hulls) do
                            if H.contains(h, q, M - 0.5) then inside = true; break end
                        end
                        if not inside then break end
                    end
                    E = q
                elseif endH then

                    local inner, outer = doorPair(endH, toPos.Y, M)
                    E = outer
                    post[#post + 1] = inner
                end

                local function blocked(a, b)
                    for _, h in ipairs(hulls) do
                        if segHits(a, b, h.minX - M + 0.5, h.maxX + M - 0.5, h.minZ - M + 0.5, h.maxZ + M - 0.5) then
                            return true
                        end
                    end
                    return false
                end
                local mid = {}
                if blocked(S, E) then
                    local nodes = { S }
                    for _, h in ipairs(hulls) do
                        for _, c in ipairs({
                            Vector3.new(h.minX - M, 0, h.minZ - M), Vector3.new(h.maxX + M, 0, h.minZ - M),
                            Vector3.new(h.maxX + M, 0, h.maxZ + M), Vector3.new(h.minX - M, 0, h.maxZ + M),
                        }) do
                            local free = true
                            for _, o in ipairs(hulls) do
                                if c.X > o.minX - M + 0.5 and c.X < o.maxX + M - 0.5
                                   and c.Z > o.minZ - M + 0.5 and c.Z < o.maxZ + M - 0.5 then
                                    free = false; break
                                end
                            end
                            if free then nodes[#nodes + 1] = c end
                        end
                    end
                    nodes[#nodes + 1] = E
                    local N = #nodes

                    local dist, prev, done = {}, {}, {}
                    for i = 1, N do dist[i] = huge end
                    dist[1] = 0
                    local flat = function(v) return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z) end
                    for _ = 1, N do
                        local u, best = nil, huge
                        for i = 1, N do
                            if not done[i] and dist[i] < best then u, best = i, dist[i] end
                        end
                        if not u or u == N then break end
                        done[u] = true
                        for v = 2, N do
                            if not done[v] then
                                local w = (flat(nodes[u]) - flat(nodes[v])).Magnitude
                                if dist[u] + w < dist[v] and not blocked(nodes[u], nodes[v]) then
                                    dist[v] = dist[u] + w
                                    prev[v] = u
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if dist[N] < huge then
                        local chain, k = {}, prev[N]
                        while k and k ~= 1 do table.insert(chain, 1, k); k = prev[k] end

                        local total = dist[N]
                        for _, idx in ipairs(chain) do
                            local t = total > 0 and (dist[idx] / total) or 1
                            local c = nodes[idx]
                            mid[#mid + 1] = Vector3.new(c.X, S.Y + (E.Y - S.Y) * t, c.Z)
                        end
                    else
                        if #pre == 0 and #post == 0 then return nil end
                    end
                elseif #pre == 0 and #post == 0 and (E - toPos).Magnitude < 0.5 then
                    return nil
                end

                local out = {}
                for _, p in ipairs(pre) do out[#out + 1] = p end
                for _, p in ipairs(mid) do out[#out + 1] = p end
                if (E - toPos).Magnitude > 0.5 then out[#out + 1] = E end
                for _, p in ipairs(post) do out[#out + 1] = p end
                out[#out + 1] = toPos
                return out
            end

            return H
        end)()
        MV.plotHull = function(plot, force) return _hull.of(plot, force) end
        _G.Skyr0PlotHulls = function()
            local out = {}
            local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            if plots then
                for _, p in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                    local h = _hull.of(p)
                    if h and h.minX then
                        out[#out + 1] = string.format("%s  x[%.0f..%.0f] z[%.0f..%.0f] y[%.0f..%.0f]  door %s  parts %d",
                            p.Name, h.minX, h.maxX, h.minZ, h.maxZ, h.bottom, h.top, tostring(h.nrm), h.n)
                    end
                end
            end
            for _, l in ipairs(out) do  end
            return out
        end

        local function _planLeg(fromPos, toPos, facingDir, maxLift, preferCrest)

            if _clearWide(fromPos, toPos) then return { toPos } end

            if _segmentCrossesCenter(fromPos, toPos) then
                local c0, c1 = _centerSpanT(fromPos, toPos)
                local h0, h1 = _hitSpanT(fromPos, toPos)
                if c0 and h0 and h1 and h1 >= c0 and h0 <= (c1 or 1) then
                    local t0 = math.max(h0, c0)
                    local t1 = math.min(h1, c1 or h1)
                    if (t1 - t0) > 0.02 then return _localHopOver(fromPos, toPos, t0, t1) end
                end
            end

            local rowPatch = _findRowDetour(fromPos, toPos, fromPos.Y)
            if rowPatch and #rowPatch > 0 then fromPos = rowPatch[#rowPatch] end
            local function _withPatch(route)
                if not rowPatch or #rowPatch == 0 then return route end
                local merged = {}
                for _, p in ipairs(rowPatch) do merged[#merged + 1] = p end
                for _, p in ipairs(route) do merged[#merged + 1] = p end
                return merged
            end
            if _clearWide(fromPos, toPos) then return _withPatch({ toPos }) end

            if preferCrest or _G.Skyr0CrestRoute == true then
                local cruiseY = math.max(fromPos.Y, toPos.Y, 26) + 12
                local up = Vector3.new(fromPos.X, cruiseY, fromPos.Z)
                local over = Vector3.new(toPos.X, cruiseY, toPos.Z)
                local crest = { fromPos, up, over, toPos }
                local ok = true
                for i = 1, #crest - 1 do
                    local a, b = crest[i], crest[i + 1]
                    if (a - b).Magnitude > 0.5 then
                        local sA = (i == 1) and _ENDPOINT_SLACK or 0
                        local sB = (i == #crest - 1) and _ENDPOINT_SLACK or 0
                        if not _clearWide(a, b, sA, sB) then ok = false; break end
                    end
                end
                if ok then return _withPatch(crest) end
            end

            do
                local vr = voxelRoute(fromPos, toPos)
                if vr and #vr > 0 then return _withPatch(vr) end
            end

            local entry = facingDir and (toPos - facingDir * 14) or toPos
            local best, bestLen = nil, math.huge
            local function consider(pts)
                if not pts or #pts < 2 then return end
                local n = #pts
                for i = 1, n - 1 do
                    local a, b = pts[i], pts[i + 1]
                    if (a - b).Magnitude > 0.5 then
                        local sA = (i == 1) and _ENDPOINT_SLACK or 0
                        local sB = (i == n - 1) and _ENDPOINT_SLACK or 0
                        if not _clearWide(a, b, sA, sB) then return end
                    end
                end
                local pulled = _pullWide(pts)
                local L = _lenOf(pulled)
                if L < bestLen then best, bestLen = pulled, L end
            end
            do
                local dirF = Vector3.new(entry.X - fromPos.X, 0, entry.Z - fromPos.Z)
                if dirF.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                    dirF = dirF.Unit
                    local perp = Vector3.new(-dirF.Z, 0, dirF.X)
                    local midBase = (fromPos + entry) * 0.5
                    for _, off in ipairs({ 14, -14, 24, -24, 38, -38, 56, -56, 76, -76 }) do
                        consider({ fromPos, midBase + perp * off, entry })
                        consider({ fromPos, fromPos + perp * off, entry + perp * off, entry })
                    end
                end
            end

            local navRaw
            if not best then
                local groundTo = Vector3.new(entry.X, fromPos.Y, entry.Z)
                local path = PathfindingService:CreatePath({
                    AgentRadius = 16, AgentHeight = 5, AgentCanJump = true, AgentJumpHeight = 10, AgentMaxSlope = 89,
                })
                local FLOAT = 5
                local nav = { fromPos }
                local ok = pcall(function()
                    path:ComputeAsync(Vector3.new(fromPos.X, fromPos.Y, fromPos.Z), groundTo)
                end)
                if ok and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
                    local last = fromPos
                    for _, wp in ipairs(path:GetWaypoints()) do
                        if (wp.Position - last).Magnitude >= 8 then
                            nav[#nav + 1] = wp.Position + Vector3.new(0, FLOAT, 0)
                            last = wp.Position
                        end
                    end
                end
                nav[#nav + 1] = entry + Vector3.new(0, FLOAT, 0)
                nav = _pushOffWalls(nav)
                navRaw = nav
                consider(nav)
            end

            local route = best
            if not route then
                local vr = voxelRoute(fromPos, toPos, true)
                if vr and #vr > 0 then route = vr end
            end
            if not route and navRaw then route = _pullWide(navRaw) end
            if not route and _clearWide(fromPos, toPos, _ENDPOINT_SLACK, _ENDPOINT_SLACK) then route = { toPos } end
            if not route then
                route = { toPos }
            end
            if (route[#route] - toPos).Magnitude > 0.5 then route[#route + 1] = toPos end
            return _withPatch(route)
        end

        return function(fromPos, toPos, facingDir, maxLift, preferCrest)

            if _G.Skyr0RouterLegacy == true then return MV.legacyRoute(fromPos, toPos, facingDir) end
            _rebuildSkip(toPos, fromPos)

            local okH, anchors = pcall(_hull.plan, fromPos, toPos, facingDir)
            if not okH then
                anchors = nil
            end
            if not anchors then return _planLeg(fromPos, toPos, facingDir, maxLift, preferCrest) end

            local out, cur = {}, fromPos
            for i, a in ipairs(anchors) do
                local last = (i == #anchors)
                local leg
                if (a - cur).Magnitude < 0.5 then
                    leg = {}
                elseif _clearWide(cur, a, (i == 1) and _ENDPOINT_SLACK or 0, last and _ENDPOINT_SLACK or 0) then

                    leg = { a }
                else
                    leg = _planLeg(cur, a, last and facingDir or nil, maxLift, preferCrest)
                    if not leg or #leg == 0 then leg = { a } end
                end
                for _, p in ipairs(leg) do out[#out + 1] = p end
                cur = a
            end
            if #out == 0 or (out[#out] - toPos).Magnitude > 0.5 then out[#out + 1] = toPos end
            return out
        end
    end)()

    local function doClone()
        local char = LP.Character or LP.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not char or not hum then return false end
        local cloner = (LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack") and LP.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner"))
                    or char:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")
        if not cloner then
            return false
        end

        if _G.Skyr0InstantClone then

            for _attempt = 1, 3 do
                if _G.Skyr0InstantClone() then return true end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
            return false
        end
        return false
    end

    local function carpetGlideTo(targetPos)
        if not targetPos then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
        pcall(equipCarpet)
        local FLOAT_OFFSET = (targetPos.Y > 20) and -4 or 0
        local goal = Vector3.new(targetPos.X, targetPos.Y + FLOAT_OFFSET, targetPos.Z)

        if hrp.Parent and not LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") and (goal - hrp.Position).Magnitude >= 3 then
            local _speed = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoSpeed) or 200
            local _cap = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoGlideMax) or 3
            local _g0 = os.clock()
            while os.clock() - _g0 < _cap do
                if not (hrp and hrp.Parent) then break end
                if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then break end
                local d = goal - hrp.Position
                if d.Magnitude <= 2 then break end
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = d.Unit * _speed
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end)
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
        end
        if hrp and hrp.Parent then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(goal) * (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position)
        end
    end

    local function goToBrainrot(petPos)
        if not petPos then return end
        local char, hrp, hum
        local _t0 = os.clock()
        repeat
            char = LP.Character
            hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hrp and hum then break end
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        until os.clock() - _t0 > 3
        if not hrp or not hum then return end
        pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
        local _equipped = false
        do
            local _e0 = os.clock()
            repeat
                char = LP.Character
                for _, _cn in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
                    if char and char:FindFirstChild(_cn) then _equipped = true; break end
                end
                if _equipped then break end
                equipCarpet()
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            until _equipped or os.clock() - _e0 > 1.5
            if _equipped then task.wait(0.2) end
        end
        char = LP.Character
        hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp then return end
        pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
        local _plotRad = (petPos.Y <= 8.9) and 26 or 25
        do
            local _t0b = os.clock()
            repeat
                local p = hrp.Position
                local inRad = false
                local plotsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                if plotsFolder then
                    for _, plot in ipairs(plotsFolder:GetChildren()) do
                        pcall(function()
                            local pp = plot:GetPivot().Position
                            if math.abs(p.X - pp.X) < _plotRad and math.abs(p.Z - pp.Z) < _plotRad then inRad = true end
                        end)
                        if inRad then break end
                    end
                end
                if inRad then break end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            until os.clock() - _t0b > 1.5
        end
        local h = petPos.Y

        local _isF2FromF1Case = _G.Skyr0IsF2FromF1 and _G.Skyr0IsF2FromF1(h) or false
        local targetY = hrp.Position.Y
        if h > 23.15 then targetY = 21
        elseif h >= 11 and h <= 23.15 then

            targetY = (_isF2FromF1Case and (h - 8)) or 14.5
        elseif h >= -6.9 and h <= 8.9 then targetY = -4 end
        local _to = Vector3.new(petPos.X, targetY, petPos.Z)

        if _isF2FromF1Case then

        elseif Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.BrainrotCarpet then

            carpetGlideTo(petPos)
        else

            if hrp and hrp.Parent and not LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                local _speed = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoSpeed) or 200
                local _cap = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoGlideMax) or 3
                local _g0 = os.clock()
                while os.clock() - _g0 < _cap do
                    if not (hrp and hrp.Parent) then break end
                    if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then break end
                    local d = _to - hrp.Position
                    if d.Magnitude <= 2 then break end
                    pcall(function()
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = d.Unit * _speed
                        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end)
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(_to)
                end)
            end
        end
        if hrp and hrp.Parent and not _isF2FromF1Case then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        if not _isF2FromF1Case then
            do
                local _platPos = (hrp and hrp.Parent and hrp.Position) or _to
                local _feetY = _platPos.Y - 3
                local _plat = Instance.new("Part")
                _plat.Name = "Skyr0TempPlatform"; _plat.Size = Vector3.new(8, 1, 8)
                _plat.Position = Vector3.new(petPos.X, _feetY - 1.5, petPos.Z)
                _plat.Anchored = true; _plat.CanCollide = false; pcall(makeOneWay, _plat); _plat.Transparency = 1
                _plat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic; _plat.Parent = workspace
                task.spawn(function()
                    local _s = tick()
                    while tick() - _s < 20 do
                        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then break end
                        task.wait(0.1)
                    end
                    if _plat and _plat.Parent then _plat:Destroy() end
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    local InternalStealCache = {}
    local STEAL_HOLD_DURATION = 1.3
    local _stealHoldStart = 0
    local _stealHoldActive = false

    local function buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
        local cached = InternalStealCache[prompt]
        if cached then

            if #cached.holdCallbacks > 0 or not cached.ready then return end
            if type(getconnections) ~= "function" then return end
            if os.clock() - (cached.builtAt or 0) < 0.2 then return end
            InternalStealCache[prompt] = nil
        end
        if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return end
        local data = { holdCallbacks = {}, triggerCallbacks = {}, holdEndCallbacks = {}, ready = true, builtAt = os.clock() }
        local function grab(sig, into)
            local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, sig)
            if ok and type(conns) == "table" then
                for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
                    if type(c.Function) == "function" then table.insert(into, c.Function) end
                end
            end
        end
        grab(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan, data.holdCallbacks)
        grab(prompt.Triggered, data.triggerCallbacks)
        grab(prompt.PromptButtonHoldEnded, data.holdEndCallbacks)

        data.direct = not (#data.holdCallbacks > 0 or #data.triggerCallbacks > 0
                           or #data.holdEndCallbacks > 0)
        if data.direct and type(fireproximityprompt) ~= "function" then

            if not _G.__Skyr0NoStealAPI then
                _G.__Skyr0NoStealAPI = true
            end
            return
        end
        InternalStealCache[prompt] = data
    end

    local function _apFromHold(data)
        if data.ap ~= nil then return data.ap or nil end
        if debug and debug.getupvalues then
            for _, f in ipairs(data.holdCallbacks) do
                local ok, ups = pcall(debug.getupvalues, f)
                if ok and type(ups) == "table" then
                    for _, v in pairs(ups) do
                        if type(v) == "table" and rawget(v, "State") ~= nil and rawget(v, "ProximityPrompt") ~= nil then
                            data.ap = v
                            return v
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        data.ap = false
        return nil
    end

    local function executeStealAsync(prompt)

        if _stealHoldActive then return false end
        local data = InternalStealCache[prompt]
        if not data or not data.ready then return false end
        data.ready = false
        _stealHoldStart = tick()
        _stealHoldActive = true
        _G.Skyr0_StealStatus = _G.Skyr0_StealStatus or {}
        _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.active = true
        _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.start = _stealHoldStart
        _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.duration = tonumber(_G.Skyr0StealHoldDuration) or STEAL_HOLD_DURATION

        task.spawn(function()

            if data.direct then
                local _dur = tonumber(_G.Skyr0StealHoldDuration) or STEAL_HOLD_DURATION
                local _t0 = tick()

                pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
                local _next = 0.25
                while prompt and prompt.Parent and (tick() - _t0) < _dur do
                    if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then break end
                    task.wait(_next)
                    if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then break end
                    if prompt and prompt.Parent then
                        pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt, 0)
                    end
                    _next = math.min(_next * 1.6, 0.5)
                end
                _stealHoldActive = false
                if _G.Skyr0_StealStatus then _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.active = false end
                task.wait(0.05)
                data.ready = true
                return
            end

            local _oldHoldDur
            pcall(function() _oldHoldDur = prompt.HoldDuration; prompt.HoldDuration = 0 end)
            local _epoch = tonumber(_G.__Skyr0HoldEpoch) or 0
            local _hAge = _epoch > 0 and (tick() - _epoch) or math.huge
            local _carried = _G.Skyr0StealHoldCarry ~= false and prompt.Enabled
                and _hAge <= (tonumber(_G.Skyr0StealCarryMax) or 1.25)
            local _ap = _apFromHold(data)
            if _carried then
                _stealHoldStart = _epoch
                if _G.Skyr0_StealStatus then _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.start = _epoch end
            elseif _ap and _ap.State ~= "Steal" then
                local _os = _ap.State
                _ap.State = "Steal"
                for _, fn in ipairs(data.holdCallbacks) do pcall(fn) end
                _ap.State = _os
                _G.__Skyr0HoldEpoch = tick()
            else
                for _, fn in ipairs(data.holdCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end
                if #data.holdCallbacks > 0 and prompt.Enabled then _G.__Skyr0HoldEpoch = tick() end
            end

            if not _ap then pcall(function()
                local _st = prompt:GetAttribute("State")
                if _st ~= nil and _st ~= "Steal" then

                    if not _G._xenStealRemote or not _G._xenStealRemote.Parent then
                        local _r, _real = nil, false
                        if _G.Skyr0GetRemote then
                            _r, _real = _G.Skyr0GetRemote("f40f7d9e-2f0d-4167-b250-899273f46874", "RemoteEvent")
                        end
                        if _r and _real then _G._xenStealRemote = _r end
                    end
                    local r = _G._xenStealRemote
                    if r then
                        local _t = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + 124
                        r:FireServer(_t, "68c86eb7-eb7e-4b4d-96ae-cf7cd847c5b0")
                        r:FireServer(_t, "07b9cc25-2a1f-4a26-a0ec-f2fab578d8bd")
                    end
                end
            end) end

            local _holdDur = tonumber(_G.Skyr0StealHoldDuration) or STEAL_HOLD_DURATION
            local remain = _holdDur - (tick() - _stealHoldStart)
            if remain > 0 then task.wait(remain) end

            if prompt and prompt.Parent then
                if type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" and _G.Skyr0StealUseFPP ~= false then
                    pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
                else
                    for _, fn in ipairs(data.triggerCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end
                    if #data.triggerCallbacks == 0 and type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
                        pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
                    end
                end
            end
            for _, fn in ipairs(data.holdEndCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end
            pcall(function() if _oldHoldDur ~= nil and prompt.Parent then prompt.HoldDuration = _oldHoldDur end end)
            _stealHoldActive = false
            if _G.Skyr0_StealStatus then _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.active = false end
            task.wait(0.05)
            data.ready = true
        end)
        return true
    end

    local function findStealPrompt(pet)
        if pet.plot and pet.slot then
            local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(pet.plot)
            local podiums = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            local podium = podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(pet.slot))
            if podium then
                local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
                local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                local attach = spawn and spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                if attach then
                    for _, p in ipairs(attach:GetChildren()) do
                        if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return p end
                    end
                end
                for _, d in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return d end
                end
            end
        end
        if pet.model and pet.model.Parent then
            for _, d in ipairs(pet.model:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return d end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local STEAL_PROXIMITY = 60

    local STEAL_PREARM    = 0.95
    local STEAL_ARM_TIMEOUT = 25
    local _stealTarget = nil
    local _stealArmedAt = 0

    local function timeUntilCanSteal()
        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") or LP:GetAttribute("IsTrading")
            or LP:GetAttribute("IsDuelSelecting") or LP:GetAttribute("Web") then
            return -1
        end
        return 0
    end

    local function _petKey(pet)
        if not pet then return nil end
        if pet.plot and pet.slot then return tostring(pet.plot) .. "|" .. tostring(pet.slot) end
        return "idx|" .. tostring(pet.index or pet.name)
    end
    local function _petStillExists(sel, pets)
        if not sel or not pets then return nil end
        local k = _petKey(sel)
        for _, p in ipairs(pets) do if _petKey(p) == k then return p end end
        return nil
    end

    local _petPorUID

    local function _pickPriorityFrom(pets, hrp)
        if not (priorityList and #priorityList > 0 and _G.Skyr0PickPriority) then return nil end
        local _rp
        if typeof(hrp) == "Vector3" then _rp = hrp
        elseif typeof(hrp) == "Instance" then _rp = hrp.Position end

        if pets then
            local hit = _G.Skyr0PickPriority(
                pets,
                function(p) return p.mps or 0 end,
                function(p) return p.position end,
                function(p) return not p.conveyor end,
                _rp)
            if hit then return hit end
        end

        local cache = (_G.SharedState and _G.SharedState.AllAnimalsCache)
                   or (SharedState and SharedState.AllAnimalsCache)
        if type(cache) == "table" and _petPorUID then
            local eu = LocalPlayer

            local cands = {}
            for _, a in ipairs(cache) do
                if a and a.uid and a.plot
                   and a.owner ~= eu.Name and a.owner ~= eu.DisplayName
                   and not (_G.Skyr0IsMyPlotCached and _G.Skyr0IsMyPlotCached(a.plot)) then
                    local rank = _G.Skyr0PrioRank(a.name, a.index)
                    if rank then cands[#cands + 1] = { a = a, rank = rank, gen = a.genValue or 0 } end
                end
            end
            table.sort(cands, function(x, y)
                if x.rank ~= y.rank then return x.rank < y.rank end
                return x.gen > y.gen
            end)
            for _, c in ipairs(cands) do
                local rebuilt = _petPorUID(c.a.uid)
                if rebuilt and rebuilt.position then return rebuilt end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function _pickByMode(pets, hrp, autoGrab)
        if not pets or #pets == 0 then return nil end
        local C = Config or {}

        local mode = _G.Skyr0ResolveMode and _G.Skyr0ResolveMode() or (C.StealMode or "Priority")

        if not (autoGrab and mode == "Nearest") then
            local hit = _pickPriorityFrom(pets, hrp)
            if hit then return hit end
        end

        if mode == "Nearest" then

            local rp
            if typeof(hrp) == "Vector3" then
                rp = hrp
            elseif typeof(hrp) == "Instance" then
                rp = hrp.Position
            else
                local c = LocalPlayer.Character
                local h = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                rp = h and h.Position
            end
            if rp then
                local best, bd = nil, math.huge
                for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
                    if p.position then
                        local d = (rp - p.position).Magnitude
                        if d < bd then bd, best = d, p end
                    end
                end
                if best then return best end
            end
            return pets[1]
        end

        if mode == "Highest" then
            local best, bv
            for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
                local v = p.mps or 0
                if not bv or v > bv then bv, best = v, p end
            end
            return best or pets[1]
        end

        local best, bv
        for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
            local v = p.mps or 0
            if not bv or v > bv then bv, best = v, p end
        end
        return best or pets[1]
    end
    local function _samePet(a, b)
        if not a or not b then return false end
        return a.plot == b.plot and tostring(a.slot) == tostring(b.slot)
    end

    function _petPorUID(uid)
        if type(uid) ~= "string" then return nil end

        local plotName, slot = uid:match("^(.+)_([^_]+)$")
        if not plotName or not slot then return nil end
        if _G.Skyr0SlotInFuse and _G.Skyr0SlotInFuse(plotName, slot) then return nil end

        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
        if not plot then return nil end

        local pos = getPetPosition(plot, slot)
        if not pos then return nil end

        local nome, mps, mut = "Target", 0, "None"
        for _, a in ipairs(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache or {}) do
            if a.uid == uid then
                nome = a.name or nome
                mps  = a.genValue or 0
                mut  = a.mutation or mut
                break
            end
        end
        return {
            name = nome, index = nome, mps = mps, mutation = mut,
            position = pos, plot = plotName, slot = tostring(slot),
        }
    end

    local _stealTarget2 = nil
    local function armSteal(pet)
        if not pet then return end
        _stealTarget = pet; _stealArmedAt = os.clock()
        _stealTarget2 = pet
        _G.Skyr0_StealStatus = _G.Skyr0_StealStatus or {}
        _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.target = pet
    end
    local function disarmSteal()
        _stealTarget = nil
        _G.Skyr0_StealStatus = _G.Skyr0_StealStatus or {}
        _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.target = nil

        if not _stealHoldActive then _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.active = false end
    end
    _G.Skyr0ArmSteal = armSteal
    _G.Skyr0DisarmSteal = disarmSteal

    local AUTO_STEAL = (Config and Config.AutoStealEnabled) and true or false
    _G.Skyr0AutoSteal = function(on) AUTO_STEAL = on ~= false end

    local _stealLastScan = 0
    local _autoLastScan = 0
    local isTeleporting = false
    local _tpStartedAt = 0
    local _cloneTP = false
    local _cloneFired = false
    local _started = false

    local _lastCacheVer = -1

    local _tpAnchor = nil

    local _tpCommit = { pet = nil, untilT = 0 }
    local function _commitTP(pet, landed)
        if not pet or pet.conveyor then return end
        _tpCommit.pet = pet
        _tpCommit.untilT = landed and (os.clock() + (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpCommitHold) or 4)) or math.huge
    end

    _G.Skyr0GrabFinisher = function()

        if Config and Config.AutoStealEnabled then AUTO_STEAL = true end

        if _stealHoldActive then return false end

        _G.Skyr0_StealStatus = _G.Skyr0_StealStatus or {}
        _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.active = false
        _autoLastScan = 0
        return true
    end

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local now = os.clock()

        local _ciclo = _G.__Skyr0CicloRodando
        local _cicloRunning = type(_ciclo) == "number" and (os.clock() - _ciclo) < 20
        local _tpInFlight = (isTeleporting and (now - _tpStartedAt) < 30) or _cicloRunning

        local _ver = SharedState and SharedState.CacheVersion or 0
        local _plotChanged = (_ver ~= _lastCacheVer)
        if _plotChanged then _lastCacheVer = _ver end

        local _camp = _G.Skyr0Camp
        local _camping = _camp and _camp.active == true
        if AUTO_STEAL and not _camping and _started and not LP:GetAttribute("Stealing")
            and (_plotChanged or not _tpInFlight)
            and (now - _autoLastScan) >= 0.1 then
            _autoLastScan = now
            local char = LP.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                local ok, pets = pcall(scanAllPets)
                if ok and pets then
                    local best

                    if manuallySelectedUID then
                        for _, pet in ipairs(pets) do
                            if not pet.conveyor and pet.plot and pet.slot then
                                local uid = pet.plot .. "_" .. tostring(pet.slot)
                                if uid == manuallySelectedUID then best = pet; break end
                            end
                        end

                        if not best then best = _petPorUID(manuallySelectedUID) end
                    end
                    if not best then

                        local nc = {}
                        for _, p in ipairs(pets) do if not p.conveyor then nc[#nc + 1] = p end end

                        local _nm = _G.Skyr0ResolveMode and _G.Skyr0ResolveMode() or "Priority"
                        if _nm == "Nearest" then
                            local base = _G.Skyr0NearBase
                            if not base then
                                if #nc > 0 then
                                    base = {}
                                    for _, p in ipairs(nc) do base[tostring(p.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot)] = true end
                                    _G.Skyr0NearBase = base
                                end
                            else
                                local kept = {}
                                for _, p in ipairs(nc) do
                                    if base[tostring(p.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot)] then kept[#kept + 1] = p end
                                end
                                nc = kept
                            end
                        else
                            _G.Skyr0NearBase = nil
                        end
                        best = _pickByMode(nc, (_tpInFlight and _tpAnchor) or hrp, true)
                    end

                    if _tpCommit.pet and os.clock() < _tpCommit.untilT and not manuallySelectedUID then
                        local kept
                        for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
                            if _samePet(p, _tpCommit.pet) then kept = p; break end
                        end
                        if kept then best = kept else _tpCommit.pet = nil end
                    end
                    if best then
                        if not _samePet(best, _stealTarget) then armSteal(best) end
                    elseif _stealTarget and not _stealHoldActive then
                        disarmSteal()
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if _camping then return end
        local pet = _stealTarget
        if not pet then return end
        local STEAL_MODE = (Config and Config.StealMode) or "Priority"
        if STEAL_MODE == "Nearest" and now - _stealArmedAt > STEAL_ARM_TIMEOUT then
            disarmSteal(); return
        end
        if now - _stealLastScan < 0.067 then return end
        _stealLastScan = now
        local t = timeUntilCanSteal()
        if t == -1 then
            if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then disarmSteal() end
            return
        end
        if t > 0 and t > STEAL_HOLD_DURATION then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        local prompt = findStealPrompt(pet)
        if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return end
        local pp = prompt.Parent
        local ppPos = (pp and pp:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Position)
            or (pp and pp.Parent and pp.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Parent.Position)
        local _inCloneTP = isTeleporting and _cloneTP and _cloneFired

        local _preArm = false

        if isTeleporting then _G.__Skyr0LastFlightAt = os.clock() end
        local _pend = _G.__Skyr0TPPending
        local _pendBlocks = _pend ~= nil
            and not (type(_pend) == "number" and (tonumber(_G.__Skyr0LastFlightAt) or 0) > _pend)
        if (isTeleporting or _pendBlocks) and not _inCloneTP then return end

        local _reach = (_G.Skyr0GetGrabRadius and _G.Skyr0GetGrabRadius()) or STEAL_PROXIMITY
        if _G.Skyr0Camp and _G.Skyr0Camp.active == true and pet.plot == _G.Skyr0Camp.plotName then
            _reach = math.max(_reach, tonumber(_G.Skyr0CampStealRadius) or 95)
        end
        if not _inCloneTP and not _preArm and ppPos
           and (hrp.Position - ppPos).Magnitude > _reach then return end
        local oldMax
        pcall(function() oldMax = prompt.MaxActivationDistance end)
        pcall(function() prompt.MaxActivationDistance = math.huge end)
        buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
        if InternalStealCache[prompt] then executeStealAsync(prompt) end
        pcall(function() if oldMax ~= nil then prompt.MaxActivationDistance = oldMax end end)
    end)

    MV.mk = (function()
        local SkyrTP = {}
        local LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE = function(f) return f end
        local _STRIP_OK = (type(getconnections) == "function")
        local CARPET_SPEED = Skyr0Speed.CARPET
        local SKYR0_TP_ARRIVE = ARRIVE
        local SKYR0_TP_TIMEOUT_SPEED = 125
        local function _spd() return tonumber(Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed) or 400 end
        local function _cloneDelay() return tonumber(Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal) or 0.12 end

        if _G.Skyr0TpNoZeroVel == nil then _G.Skyr0TpNoZeroVel = false end
        if _G.Skyr0TpClimb == nil then _G.Skyr0TpClimb = 100 end
        if _G.Skyr0TpCloseSpeed == nil then _G.Skyr0TpCloseSpeed = 300 end
        if _G.Skyr0TpGoSpeed == nil then _G.Skyr0TpGoSpeed = 220 end
        if _G.Skyr0TpCFrameSpeed == nil then _G.Skyr0TpCFrameSpeed = 450 end
        local function _vzOK()
            if _G.Skyr0TpNoZeroVel == true then return false end
            if _G.Skyr0TpZeroWhileStealing ~= true and LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return false end
            return true
        end
        local function _vzL(p) if p and _vzOK() then p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end end
        local function _vzA(p) if p and _vzOK() then p.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end end

        local function _fireGrapple()

            _G.Skyr0CycleHookAt = os.clock()
            local ok = false
            if _G.Skyr0FireGrapple2 then
                pcall(function() ok = _G.Skyr0FireGrapple2(SkyrTP.aim) and true or false end)
            end
            return ok
        end
        local function fireGrapple()
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then return end
            if not char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
                local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                local tool = bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if tool and hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end) end
            end
            if not char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then return end
            _fireGrapple()
        end

        local function _climbCap()
            local v = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpClimb) or 200, 100, 250)
            if not _STRIP_OK then v = 55 end
            return v
        end

        local function vZero(hrp)
            if hrp then _vzL(hrp); _vzA(hrp) end
        end

        local function _setFlightVel(hrp, vel)
            local char = hrp and hrp.Parent
            local part = (char and (char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))) or hrp
            if part then part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel end

            if _G.EliteFlingTP == true and Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.FlingTP == true
               and not _G.__EliteFlashHolding and not _G.__EliteFlingCalm
               and hrp and typeof(vel) == "Vector3" and vel.Magnitude > 5 then
                pcall(function()
                    local t = os.clock()
                    local amp = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.EliteFlingAmp) or 1, 0, 3)
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position)
                        * CFrame.Angles(0, (t * 18) % (math.pi * 2), 0)
                        * CFrame.Angles(math.sin(t * 20) * math.rad(45) * amp, 0, (t * 16) % (math.pi * 2))
                end)
            end
        end

        local function velMoveThrough(hrp, waypoints, speedOverride, allowJump, quickStart)
            if not hrp or not hrp.Parent or #waypoints == 0 then return end
            local _runSpeed = speedOverride or (_G.TPVelocity and math.clamp(_G.TPVelocity, 200, 1200)) or CARPET_SPEED
            vizPath(hrp.Position, waypoints)
            local wpIdx = 1
            local done = false
            local conn

            local _reason = "timeout"
            local function finish(why)
                if done then return end
                done = true
                if why then _reason = why end
                if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                    _vzL(hrp)
                    _vzA(hrp)
                    local _, y = hrp.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()

                    if _reason ~= "lagback"
                       and (hrp.Position - waypoints[#waypoints]).Magnitude <= (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpSnapMax) or 25) then
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(waypoints[#waypoints]) * CFrame.Angles(0, y, 0)
                    end
                end
                if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
            end
            local lastDist, stallStart = math.huge, 0

            local lastPos, lagRecover, lagUntil, unstick = hrp.Position, 0, 0, 0

            local _flingTripDist2 = 0
            do
                local _fp2 = hrp.Position
                for _, _wp2 in ipairs(waypoints) do
                    _flingTripDist2 = _flingTripDist2 + (_fp2 - _wp2).Magnitude
                    _fp2 = _wp2
                end
            end
            local _flingRangeOk2 = _flingTripDist2 >= (tonumber(_G.EliteFlingMinRange) or 200)

            local MAX_RECOVER = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpMaxRecover) or 5
            local _startPos = hrp.Position

            local _lagMul = 1
            do
                local m, at = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpLagMul), tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpLagMulAt)
                if m and at then _lagMul = math.min(1, m + (os.clock() - at) * 0.04) end
            end

            local _cmdDir, _prevCmd = nil, nil
            local _backSum, _backAt = 0, os.clock()

            local function _resumeIdx(pos)
                local best, bestD, bestT = wpIdx, math.huge, 1
                local prev = _startPos
                for k = 1, #waypoints do
                    local a, b = prev, waypoints[k]
                    local ab = b - a
                    local L2 = ab:Dot(ab)
                    local t = (L2 > 1e-6) and math.clamp((pos - a):Dot(ab) / L2, 0, 1) or 1
                    local d = (a + ab * t - pos).Magnitude
                    if d < bestD - 0.01 then best, bestD, bestT = k, d, t end
                    prev = b
                end

                if bestT < 0.02 and best > 1 then best = best - 1 end
                return best
            end

            local _stStart = os.clock()
            local _lastJump = 0

            local _routeLen = 0
            do
                local _p = hrp.Position
                for _, wp in ipairs(waypoints) do
                    _routeLen = _routeLen + (_p - wp).Magnitude
                    _p = wp
                end
            end
            local _mayJump = _routeLen >= (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpJumpMinDist) or 100)

            local _ = quickStart

            conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(dt)
                dt = dt or (1 / 60)
                if not hrp or not hrp.Parent or done then
                    if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                    return
                end
                if _G.Skyr0TPCancel then finish("cancel") return end
                equipCarpet()
                if _mayJump and _G.Skyr0TpJumpEachStep ~= false then
                    local _now = os.clock()
                    if _now - _lastJump >= (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpJumpGap) or 0.2) then
                        _lastJump = _now
                        local _jh = hrp.Parent and hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if _jh then
                            pcall(function() _jh:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
                            pcall(function() _jh.Jump = true end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                local target = waypoints[wpIdx]
                local diff = target - hrp.Position
                local mag = diff.Magnitude
                local _spd = math.max(_runSpeed * _lagMul, math.min(_runSpeed, 140))
                if wpIdx < #waypoints and mag < 26 then
                    local nxt = waypoints[wpIdx + 1]
                    local b = nxt - target
                    if mag > 0.1 and b.Magnitude > 0.1 and diff.Unit:Dot(b.Unit) < 0.9 then
                        _spd = math.min(_spd, 240)
                    end
                end
                local _arr = math.max(SKYR0_TP_ARRIVE, _spd / 60 * 1.25)
                if mag < _arr then
                    wpIdx = wpIdx + 1
                    if wpIdx > #waypoints then finish("arrived") return end
                    lastDist, stallStart = math.huge, 0
                    if _G.Skyr0TpZeroEachStep ~= false and _vzOK() then
                        pcall(function()
                            local _v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                            local _keepY = (_G.Skyr0TpZeroStepKeepY == false) and 0 or math.max(_v.Y, 0)
                            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, _keepY, 0)
                            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        end)
                    end
                    if _mayJump and _G.Skyr0TpJumpEachStep ~= false then
                        local _wh = hrp.Parent and hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if _wh then
                            _lastJump = os.clock()
                            pcall(function() _wh:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
                            pcall(function() _wh.Jump = true end)
                        end
                    end
                    target = waypoints[wpIdx]
                    diff = target - hrp.Position
                    mag = diff.Magnitude
                end

                local _delta = hrp.Position - lastPos
                local moved = _delta.Magnitude
                local _png = (_G.Skyr0Ping and _G.Skyr0Ping()) or 0.1
                local _budget = _spd * (math.max(dt, 1 / 240) * 2 + _png) + 6
                local _nowL = os.clock()
                if (_nowL - _backAt) > 0.5 then _backSum, _backAt = 0, _nowL end
                local _against = _cmdDir and -_delta:Dot(_cmdDir) or 0
                if _cmdDir and _prevCmd and _cmdDir:Dot(_prevCmd) > 0.7 and _against > 0.5
                   and _nowL >= lagUntil then
                    _backSum = _backSum + _against
                end

                local _snapped = moved > _budget
                    and ((lastDist < math.huge and mag > lastDist + 4) or _against > moved * 0.3)
                local _drifted = _backSum > math.max(24, _spd * 0.12)
                if (_snapped or _drifted) and _nowL >= lagUntil then
                    lastPos = hrp.Position
                    _backSum, _backAt = 0, _nowL
                    _cmdDir, _prevCmd = nil, nil
                    if lagRecover < MAX_RECOVER then
                        lagRecover = lagRecover + 1
                        _lagMul = math.max(_lagMul * 0.8, 0.35)
                        _G.Skyr0TpLagMul, _G.Skyr0TpLagMulAt = _lagMul, _nowL
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

                        lagUntil = _nowL + math.clamp(_png * 2 + 0.2, 0.35, 1.5)
                        wpIdx = _resumeIdx(hrp.Position)
                        lastDist, stallStart = math.huge, 0
                        return
                    end
                    finish("lagback") return
                end
                lastPos = hrp.Position
                if _nowL < lagUntil then
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    return
                end

                if mag > lastDist - 0.05 then
                    if stallStart == 0 then stallStart = os.clock() end
                else
                    stallStart = 0
                end
                lastDist = mag
                if stallStart ~= 0 and (os.clock() - stallStart) > (0.45 + _png * 2) then

                    if unstick < 2 and MV.clear then
                        unstick = unstick + 1
                        local flat = Vector3.new(diff.X, 0, diff.Z)
                        if flat.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                            local perp = Vector3.new(-flat.Z, 0, flat.X).Unit
                            local here = hrp.Position
                            local pick
                            for _, off in ipairs({ perp * 18, perp * -18, Vector3.new(0, 16, 0),
                                                   perp * 18 + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0),
                                                   perp * -18 + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0) }) do
                                if MV.clear(here, here + off) then pick = here + off; break end
                            end
                            if pick then
                                table.insert(waypoints, wpIdx, pick)
                                lastDist, stallStart = math.huge, 0
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    finish("stuck") return
                end

                if mag >= 0.1 then
                    local dir = diff.Unit
                    if (allowJump or diff.Y > 10) and diff.Y > 5 and wpIdx < #waypoints then
                        local hum = hrp.Parent and hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if hum then
                            local st = hum:GetState()
                            if st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
                                pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
                                pcall(function() hum.Jump = true end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    local _sp = _spd
                    local _mc = _climbCap()
                    if dir.Y > 0 and dir.Y * _sp > _mc then
                        _sp = _mc / dir.Y
                    end

                    local _vx, _vy, _vz = dir.X * _sp, dir.Y * _sp, dir.Z * _sp
                    local _calmD = tonumber(_G.EliteFlingCalm) or 45
                    _G.__EliteFlingCalm = (wpIdx == #waypoints and mag < _calmD)
                    if _G.EliteFlingTP == true and Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.FlingTP == true
                       and not _G.__EliteFlashHolding and not _G.__EliteFlingCalm
                       and _flingRangeOk2 then
                        _G.__EliteFlingSettled = false
                        local flat = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
                        if flat.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                            local u = flat.Unit
                            local perp = Vector3.new(-u.Z, 0, u.X)
                            local t = os.clock()
                            local amp = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.EliteFlingAmp) or 1, 0, 3)
                            local slide = ((math.sin(t * 16) * 0.8) + (math.cos(t * 8) * 0.55)) * math.min(125, _sp * 0.28) * amp
                            local waveY = ((math.sin(t * 20) * 0.75) + (math.cos(t * 10) * 0.5)) * math.min(75, _sp * 0.18) * amp
                            local pulse = 1 + (math.sin(t * 24) * 0.18 * amp)
                            _vx = dir.X * _sp * pulse + perp.X * slide
                            _vz = dir.Z * _sp * pulse + perp.Z * slide
                            _vy = math.clamp(_vy + waveY, math.min(_vy, -_mc), _mc)
                            local _m = math.sqrt(_vx * _vx + _vy * _vy + _vz * _vz)
                            if _m > _sp and _m > 0 then
                                local k = _sp / _m
                                _vx, _vy, _vz = _vx * k, _vy * k, _vz * k
                            end
                        end
                    elseif _G.EliteFlingTP == true and Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.FlingTP == true
                           and not _G.__EliteFlingSettled and _flingRangeOk2 then

                        _G.__EliteFlingSettled = true
                        local fd = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
                        if fd.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                            pcall(function()
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + fd.Unit)
                                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            end)
                        end
                    end

                    _prevCmd, _cmdDir = _cmdDir, dir
                    _setFlightVel(hrp, Vector3.new(_vx, _vy, _vz))
                end
            end))

            local totalDist = 0
            do
                local prev = hrp.Position
                for _, wp in ipairs(waypoints) do
                    totalDist = totalDist + (prev - wp).Magnitude
                    prev = wp
                end
            end

            local _settle = math.clamp(((_G.Skyr0Ping and _G.Skyr0Ping()) or 0.1) * 2 + 0.2, 0.35, 1.5)
            local timeout = totalDist / math.min(SKYR0_TP_TIMEOUT_SPEED, _runSpeed) + (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpFlightGrace) or 2)
            local _t0 = os.clock()
            while not done and (os.clock() - _t0) < timeout + lagRecover * (_settle + 1.5) do
                task.wait(0.05)
                if not (hrp and hrp.Parent) then break end
            end
            finish()
            vZero(hrp)

            pcall(function()
                local _h2 = hrp.Parent and hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if _h2 then _h2.AutoRotate = true end
            end)
            SkyrTP.lastLeg = { reason = _reason, lagbacks = lagRecover }
            return _reason, lagRecover
        end
        local _carpetEngaging = false
        local function carpetEngage(force)
            if not force then
                local c = LP.Character
                if c then
                    for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
                        local t = c:FindFirstChild(n)
                        if t and t:IsA("Tool") then
                            _G.TPEngage = "carpet=" .. tostring(n)
                            return n
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if _carpetEngaging then
                local _tw = os.clock()
                repeat RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() until (not _carpetEngaging) or os.clock() - _tw > 6
                local c = LP.Character
                if c then
                    for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
                        local t = c:FindFirstChild(n)
                        if t and t:IsA("Tool") then return n end
                    end
                end
            end
            _carpetEngaging = true
            local _t0 = os.clock()
            while not findTool("Grapple Hook") and os.clock() - _t0 < (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGrappleToolWait) or 5) do
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
            local char = LP.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not char or not hum then _carpetEngaging = false; return nil end

            if not char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
                local g = findTool("Grapple Hook")
                if g then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(g) end) end
            end
            local _te = os.clock()
            while not (LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")) and os.clock() - _te < 1.5 do
                local c = LP.Character
                local h2 = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local g = findTool("Grapple Hook")
                if g and h2 then pcall(function() h2:EquipTool(g) end) end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
            if LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
                _fireGrapple()
            end
            task.wait(0.06)
            local h = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then pcall(function() h:UnequipTools() end) end
            task.wait(0.04)
            local cn
            local _tc = os.clock()
            repeat
                cn = equipCarpet()
                local c = LP.Character
                if cn and c and c:FindFirstChild(cn) then break end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            until os.clock() - _tc > 0.5
            _G.TPEngage = "carpet=" .. tostring(cn)
            _carpetEngaging = false
            return cn
        end
        local function carpetEngageFast()
            local char = LP.Character
            local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not char or not hum then return nil end

            if not char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
                local g = findTool("Grapple Hook")
                if g then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(g) end) end
            end
            local _t = os.clock()
            while not (LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook"))
                and os.clock() - _t < (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoGrappleWait) or 0.35) do
                local c = LP.Character
                local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local g = findTool("Grapple Hook")
                if g and h then pcall(function() h:EquipTool(g) end) end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
            if LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
                _fireGrapple()
            end

            local h2 = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h2 then pcall(function() h2:UnequipTools() end) end
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

            local cn
            local _t2 = os.clock()
            repeat
                cn = equipCarpet()
                local c = LP.Character
                if cn and c and c:FindFirstChild(cn) then break end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            until os.clock() - _t2 > (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoCarpetWait) or 2)
            return cn
        end
        local function goToBrainrot(petPos, slot)
            if not petPos then return end
            local char, hrp, hum
            local _t0 = os.clock()
            repeat
                char = LP.Character
                hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hrp and hum then break end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            until os.clock() - _t0 > 3
            if not hrp or not hum then return end
            pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
            local _equipped = false
            do

                for _, _cn in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
                    if char and char:FindFirstChild(_cn) then _equipped = true break end
                end
                local _gd = (petPos and hrp and hrp.Parent) and (petPos - hrp.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
                local _gmin = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoGrappleMin) or 100
                if not _equipped and _G.Skyr0TpGoGrapple ~= false and _gd > _gmin then
                    pcall(carpetEngageFast)
                    char = LP.Character
                    for _, _cn in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
                        if char and char:FindFirstChild(_cn) then _equipped = true break end
                    end
                end
                local _e0 = os.clock()
                while not _equipped and os.clock() - _e0 <= (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoCarpetWait) or 3) do
                    char = LP.Character
                    for _, _cn in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
                        if char and char:FindFirstChild(_cn) then _equipped = true break end
                    end
                    if _equipped then break end
                    equipCarpet()
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end
                if _equipped then task.wait(tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoCarpetSettle) or 0.02) end
            end
            char = LP.Character
            hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hrp then return end
            pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
            local _plotRad = (petPos.Y <= 8.9) and 26 or 25
            do
                local _t0b = os.clock()
                repeat
                    local p = hrp.Position
                    local inRad = false
                    local plotsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                    if plotsFolder then
                        for _, plot in ipairs(plotsFolder:GetChildren()) do
                            pcall(function()
                                local pp = plot:GetPivot().Position
                                if math.abs(p.X - pp.X) < _plotRad and math.abs(p.Z - pp.Z) < _plotRad then inRad = true end
                            end)
                            if inRad then break end
                        end
                    end
                    if inRad then break end
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                until os.clock() - _t0b > (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoPlotWait) or 1)
            end
            local h = petPos.Y
            pcall(function()
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end)
            local _wentUnder = false
            local _slot = tonumber(slot)
            local _under = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpUnderOffset) or 6
            local targetY = hrp.Position.Y
            if (_slot and _slot >= 19) or (not _slot and h > 23.15) then
                targetY = h - (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpUnderOffset3) or 4)
                _wentUnder = true
            elseif (_slot and _slot >= 11) or (not _slot and h >= 11 and h <= 23.15) then

                targetY = h - (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpUnderOffset2) or 3.5)
                _wentUnder = true
            elseif (_slot and _slot >= 1) or (not _slot and h >= -6.9 and h <= 8.9) then
                targetY = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpFloor1Y) or -4
                if _G.Skyr0TpFloor1Platform ~= false then _wentUnder = true end
            else
                targetY = h - _under
                _wentUnder = true
            end
            local _to = Vector3.new(petPos.X, targetY, petPos.Z)
            if hrp and hrp.Parent then

                if _G.Skyr0TpGoGlide ~= false then
                    local _goSpeed = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoSpeed) or 400
                    local _s1 = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoGlideSpeed1) or (_goSpeed * 0.375)
                    local _t1 = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoGlideTime1) or 0.03
                    local _s2 = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoGlideSpeed2) or _goSpeed
                    local _cap = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpGoGlideMax) or 2.5
                    local _g0 = os.clock()
                    local _lastJump = 0
                    while os.clock() - _g0 < _cap do
                        if not (hrp and hrp.Parent) then break end
                        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") or _G.Skyr0TPCancel then break end
                        local d = _to - hrp.Position
                        if d.Magnitude <= 3 then break end
                        equipCarpet()
                        if _G.Skyr0TpGoJump == true and d.Magnitude > 15 and (os.clock() - _lastJump) > 0.5 then
                            local _gh = hrp.Parent and hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if _gh then
                                pcall(function() _gh:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
                                pcall(function() _gh.Jump = true end)
                                _lastJump = os.clock()
                            end
                        end
                        if _G.Skyr0TpGoZeroEachFrame ~= false then
                            pcall(function()
                                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                            end)
                        end
                        local _v = d.Unit * (((os.clock() - _g0) < _t1) and _s1 or _s2)
                        if _G.Skyr0TpGoNoRise ~= false and hrp.Position.Y >= _to.Y - 0.5 and _v.Y > 0 then
                            _v = Vector3.new(_v.X, 0, _v.Z)
                        end
                        _setFlightVel(hrp, _v)
                        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    end
                end
                local _snapCF = CFrame.new(_to) * (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position)
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    hrp.CFrame = _snapCF
                end)

                for _ = 1, (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpSnapHoldFrames) or 8) do
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    if not (hrp and hrp.Parent) then break end
                    if (hrp.Position - _to).Magnitude > 4 then
                        pcall(function()
                            hrp.CFrame = _snapCF
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
            if _wentUnder and _G.Skyr0TpPlatform ~= false then
                local _platPos = (hrp and hrp.Parent and hrp.Position) or _to
                local _feetY = _platPos.Y - 3
                local _sz = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpPlatSize) or 10
                local _old = workspace:FindFirstChild("ANGVELSTempPlatform")
                if _old then pcall(function() _old:Destroy() end) end
                local _plat = Instance.new("Part")
                _plat.Name = "ANGVELSTempPlatform"; _plat.Size = Vector3.new(_sz, 1, _sz)
                _plat.Position = Vector3.new(petPos.X, _feetY - (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpPlatDrop) or 1.5), petPos.Z)
                _plat.Anchored = true; _plat.CanCollide = false; pcall(makeOneWay, _plat); _plat.Transparency = 1
                _plat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic; _plat.Parent = workspace
                task.spawn(function()
                    local _s = tick()
                    while tick() - _s < (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpPlatLife) or 20) do
                        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then break end
                        task.wait(0.1)
                    end
                    if _plat and _plat.Parent then _plat:Destroy() end
                end)
            end
        end
        local _Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
        local function _pingMs()
            local ok, p = pcall(function() return LP:GetNetworkPing() * 1000 end)
            if ok and type(p) == "number" and p > 0 then return p end
            local ok2, p2 = pcall(function()
                return _Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
            end)
            if ok2 and type(p2) == "number" and p2 > 0 then return p2 end
            return 0
        end
        _G.Skyr0TpPingMs = _pingMs

        do
            local _PA_SAMPLE  = 1.5
            local _PA_ALPHA   = 0.25
            local _PA_THRESH  = 120

            local _PA_TIERS = {
                {  60,  0.45,  0.8,   6,  650 },
                { 100,  0.55,  1.0,   8,  600 },
                { 150,  0.70,  1.2,  10,  550 },
                { 200,  0.90,  1.5,  14,  450 },
                { 280,  1.10,  1.8,  18,  380 },
                { 400,  1.40,  2.2,  24,  300 },
                { math.huge, 1.80, 2.8, 32, 220 },
            }

            local _paSmooth   = nil
            local _paLastTier = nil
            local _paActive   = true

            local function _paTier(ping)
                for i, t in ipairs(_PA_TIERS) do
                    if ping <= t[1] then return i, t end
                end
                return #_PA_TIERS, _PA_TIERS[#_PA_TIERS]
            end

            local function _paApply(idx, t, ping)
                if idx == _paLastTier then return end
                _paLastTier = idx
                _G.Skyr0TpCloneSettle    = t[2]
                _G.Skyr0TpGoPlotWait     = t[3]
                _G.Skyr0TpSnapHoldFrames = t[4]
                _G.Skyr0TpHighPingSpeed  = t[5]
                _G.Skyr0TpPingThresh     = _PA_THRESH
            end

            _G.Skyr0PingAdaptStop = function()
                _paActive = false
            end

            _G.Skyr0PingAdaptStatus = function()
                if not _paSmooth then
                    return
                end
                local idx, t = _paTier(_paSmooth)
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                while _paActive do
                    local raw = nil
                    local ok1, v1 = pcall(_pingMs)
                    if ok1 and type(v1) == "number" and v1 > 0 then
                        raw = v1
                    end
                    if raw then
                        if _paSmooth == nil then
                            _paSmooth = raw
                        else
                            _paSmooth = _PA_ALPHA * raw + (1 - _PA_ALPHA) * _paSmooth
                        end
                        local idx, tier = _paTier(_paSmooth)
                        _paApply(idx, tier, _paSmooth)
                    end
                    task.wait(_PA_SAMPLE)
                end
            end)
        end

        local function _pingAdjustSpeed(spd)
            local thresh = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpPingThresh) or 170
            local capped = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpHighPingSpeed) or 400
            if _pingMs() >= thresh and spd > capped then return capped end
            return spd
        end
        local function _isStraightRoute(fromPos, route)
            if not route or #route <= 1 then return true end
            local turns, lastDir, prev = 0, nil, fromPos
            for _, wp in ipairs(route) do
                local seg = wp - prev
                if seg.Magnitude > 1 then
                    local dir = seg.Unit
                    if lastDir and dir:Dot(lastDir) < 0.94 then turns = turns + 1 end
                    lastDir = dir
                end
                prev = wp
            end
            return turns <= (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpStraightMaxTurns) or 1)
        end
        local function _inVoid(hrp)
            if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return true end
            local voidY = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpVoidY) or -50
            return hrp.Position.Y < voidY
        end
        local function run(pet, forceGrapple)

            local char = LP.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hrp or not hum then isTeleporting = false; return end

            pcall(function()
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end)

            SkyrTP.aim = nil
            pcall(function()
                local sd = pickCloneSpot(pet.position, pet.name, hrp.Position)
                if typeof(sd) == "Vector3" then SkyrTP.aim = sd end
            end)

            if forceGrapple or _G.Skyr0TpAlwaysGrapple ~= false then
                pcall(fireGrapple)
            end

            if _inVoid(hrp) then
                if _G.Skyr0TPCancel then isTeleporting = false; return end
                char = LP.Character
                hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hrp or not hum then isTeleporting = false; return end
            end

            local petPos = pet.position
            local petName = pet.name
