            _G.Skyr0TpStealHold = true

            local _hasCarpet = false
            do
                local _c = LP.Character
                if _c then
                    for _, _n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
                        if _c:FindFirstChild(_n) then _hasCarpet = true break end
                    end
                end
            end
            if not _hasCarpet then
                pcall(fireGrapple)
            end

            local adjY = petPos.Y
            if TALL_PETS[petName] then adjY = petPos.Y - TALL_OFFSET end
            local coordTable = adjY > 23.15 and UPPER or LOWER

            if _G.Skyr0WalkInFirstFloor == true and petPos.Y <= 8.9 and isPlotUnlocked(pet.plot) then
                if not _hasCarpet then carpetEngage(forceGrapple) end
                vZero(hrp)
                local _to = Vector3.new(petPos.X, -4, petPos.Z)
                local route = computeRoute(hrp.Position, _to, nil)
                if not route or #route == 0 then route = { _to } end
                local _straight = _isStraightRoute(hrp.Position, route)
                local _obSpeed = math.clamp(_spd(), 60, 750)
                if _straight then
                    _obSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpStraightSpeed) or 300, 100, 500)
                end
                do
                    local _len, _prev = 0, hrp.Position
                    for _, wp in ipairs(route) do _len = _len + (wp - _prev).Magnitude; _prev = wp end
                    if _len < 100 then
                        _obSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpCloseSpeed) or 80, 20, 400)
                    end
                end
                _obSpeed = _pingAdjustSpeed(_obSpeed)
                velMoveThrough(hrp, route, _obSpeed, true, true)
                if hrp and hrp.Parent and not _G.Skyr0TPCancel
                   and (hrp.Position - _to).Magnitude > 12 then
                    local _rr2 = computeRoute(hrp.Position, _to, nil)
                    if not _rr2 or #_rr2 == 0 then _rr2 = { _to } end
                    velMoveThrough(hrp, _rr2, _obSpeed, true, true)
                end
                if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                    _vzL(hrp)
                    _vzA(hrp)
                end
                _G.Skyr0TpStealHold = false
                pcall(settleAfterTP)
                _tpAnchor = nil
                isTeleporting = false
                if _G.Skyr0TPCancel then return end
                return
            end

            if _G.Skyr0BadSpots then table.clear(_G.Skyr0BadSpots) end
            local _cands = MV.spots.candidates(petPos, petName, hrp.Position, coordTable)
            if #_cands == 0 then _G.Skyr0TpStealHold = false; _tpAnchor = nil; isTeleporting = false; return end

            local destPos = _cands[1].coord

            local _carpet = _hasCarpet and (function()
                local _c = LP.Character
                if _c then
                    for _, _n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
                        if _c:FindFirstChild(_n) then return _n end
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end)() or carpetEngage(forceGrapple)
            vZero(hrp)
            _cloneTP = true
            disarmSteal()

            local facingDir = _cands[1].face
            local _frontApproach = false
            local _spotCoord, _spotKind = _cands[1].coord, _cands[1].kind

            local function useSpot(c)
                _spotCoord, _spotKind = c.coord, c.kind
                destPos, facingDir = c.coord, c.face
                _frontApproach = (c.kind == "front")
                if facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                    local axis = facingDir.Unit
                    local toPlayer = hrp.Position - destPos
                    local sign = (axis:Dot(toPlayer) >= 0) and 1 or -1
                    destPos = destPos + axis * sign * (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpCloneBackoff) or 0.5)
                end
            end
            useSpot(_cands[1])

            if workspace.StreamingEnabled then
                local _sp = destPos
                task.spawn(function() pcall(function() LP:RequestStreamAroundAsync(_sp) end) end)
                local _plotsF = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                local _tplot = _plotsF and pet.plot and _plotsF:FindFirstChild(tostring(pet.plot))
                if _tplot and MV.plotHull then
                    local _w0 = os.clock()
                    local _cap = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpStreamWait) or 3
                    local _h = MV.plotHull(_tplot, true)
                    if not (_h and _h.ready) then
                        while os.clock() - _w0 < _cap and not _G.Skyr0TPCancel do
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            _h = MV.plotHull(_tplot, true)
                            if _h and _h.ready then break end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local _route = computeRoute(hrp.Position, destPos, facingDir, nil, _G.Skyr0TpCrestRoute == true)

            local ASCEND_STEP = 10
            local _stepped = {}
            do
                local prev = hrp.Position
                for _, wp in ipairs(_route) do
                    local dy = wp.Y - prev.Y
                    if dy > ASCEND_STEP * 1.5 then
                        local n = math.ceil(dy / ASCEND_STEP)
                        for s = 1, n - 1 do
                            local t = s / n
                            _stepped[#_stepped + 1] = Vector3.new(
                                prev.X + (wp.X - prev.X) * t,
                                prev.Y + dy * t,
                                prev.Z + (wp.Z - prev.Z) * t
                            )
                        end
                    end
                    _stepped[#_stepped + 1] = wp
                    prev = wp
                end
            end
            local _mainSpeed = math.clamp(_spd(), 60, 750)
            local _targetIdx = getClosestBaseIdx(petPos)
            local _targetIsWest = _targetIdx <= 4
            local _playerIsWest = hrp.Position.X < COLUMN_SPLIT_X
            local _crossRow = _targetIsWest ~= _playerIsWest
            local _isLower = coordTable == LOWER
            local _toTarget = (petPos - hrp.Position).Unit
            local _lookDir = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
            local _dot = _toTarget:Dot(_lookDir)
            local _isBehind = _dot < -0.3
            local _isProblemTP = _crossRow and _frontApproach and _isLower and _isBehind
            do
                local _len, _prev = 0, hrp.Position
                for _, wp in ipairs(_route) do _len = _len + (wp - _prev).Magnitude; _prev = wp end
                if _len < 100 then
                    _mainSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpCloseSpeed) or 80, 20, 400)
                end
                if _isProblemTP and _G.Skyr0TpSlowCrossRow == true then
                    _mainSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpProblemBaseSpeed) or 60, 20, 400)
                end
            end
            _mainSpeed = _pingAdjustSpeed(_mainSpeed)

            local function flatOffNow()
                return Vector3.new(destPos.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, destPos.Z - hrp.Position.Z).Magnitude
            end

            local function routeHere()
                local r = computeRoute(hrp.Position, destPos, facingDir, nil, _G.Skyr0TpCrestRoute == true)
                if not r or #r == 0 then r = { destPos } end
                local out, prev = {}, hrp.Position
                for _, wp in ipairs(r) do
                    local dy = wp.Y - prev.Y
                    if dy > ASCEND_STEP * 1.5 then
                        local n = math.ceil(dy / ASCEND_STEP)
                        for s = 1, n - 1 do
                            local t = s / n
                            out[#out + 1] = Vector3.new(
                                prev.X + (wp.X - prev.X) * t,
                                prev.Y + dy * t,
                                prev.Z + (wp.Z - prev.Z) * t)
                        end
                    end
                    out[#out + 1] = wp
                    prev = wp
                end
                return out
            end

            local function nextSpot()
                MV.spots.markBad(_spotCoord)
                for _, c in ipairs(MV.spots.candidates(petPos, petName, hrp.Position, coordTable)) do
                    if not MV.spots.isBad(c.coord) then useSpot(c); return true end
                end
                return false
            end
            local _legWhy = velMoveThrough(hrp, _stepped, _mainSpeed, true, true)
            if _G.Skyr0TPCancel then
                if hrp and hrp.Parent then vZero(hrp) end
                _G.Skyr0TpStealHold = false
                isTeleporting = false
                return
            end

            do
                local _sw, _lagTries = 0, 0
                local _maxLag = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpLagRetries) or 2
                while hrp and hrp.Parent and not _G.Skyr0TPCancel
                      and flatOffNow() > (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpStuckDist) or 22) do
                    if _legWhy == "lagback" and _lagTries < _maxLag then
                        _lagTries = _lagTries + 1
                        local _png = (_G.Skyr0Ping and _G.Skyr0Ping()) or 0.1
                        task.wait(math.clamp(_png * 3 + 0.3, 0.5, 2))
                    else
                        if _sw >= (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpAltSpots) or 2) then break end
                        if not nextSpot() then break end
                        _sw = _sw + 1
                    end
                    _legWhy = velMoveThrough(hrp, routeHere(), _mainSpeed, true, true)
                end
            end

            do
                local above = destPos + Vector3.new(0, 16, 0)
                local _t0 = os.clock()
                local _lastJump = 0
                while os.clock() - _t0 < 1.5 do
                    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then break end
                    if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") or _G.Skyr0TPCancel then break end
                    equipCarpet()
                    local d = above - hrp.Position
                    local flat = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Magnitude
                    if flat <= 3 and hrp.Position.Y >= destPos.Y then break end
                    if d.Y > 3 and (os.clock() - _lastJump) > 0.5 then
                        local _hum = hrp.Parent and hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if _hum then
                            local st = _hum:GetState()
                            if st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
                                pcall(function() _hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
                                pcall(function() _hum.Jump = true end)
                                _lastJump = os.clock()
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    _setFlightVel(hrp, d.Unit * math.min(math.max(d.Magnitude * 8, 55), 320))
                    _vzA(hrp)
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end
            end

            do
                local _runCap = _frontApproach and (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpFrontRunIn) or 130) or 400
                local _t0 = os.clock()
                local _bestMag, _bestT = math.huge, os.clock()
                local _lastJump = 0
                while os.clock() - _t0 < 4 do
                    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then break end
                    if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") or _G.Skyr0TPCancel then break end
                    equipCarpet()
                    local diff = destPos - hrp.Position
                    local mag = diff.Magnitude
                    if mag <= 3 then break end
                    if mag < _bestMag - 0.5 then _bestMag = mag; _bestT = os.clock()
                    elseif os.clock() - _bestT > 0.6 then break end
                    if diff.Y > 3 and mag > 15 and (os.clock() - _lastJump) > 0.5 then
                        local _hum = hrp.Parent and hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if _hum then
                            local st = _hum:GetState()
                            if st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
                                pcall(function() _hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
                                pcall(function() _hum.Jump = true end)
                                _lastJump = os.clock()
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    _setFlightVel(hrp, diff.Unit * math.min(math.max(mag * 8, 55), _runCap))
                    _vzA(hrp)
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end
            end

            if hrp and hrp.Parent and not _G.Skyr0TPCancel then
                local _flatOff = Vector3.new(destPos.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, destPos.Z - hrp.Position.Z).Magnitude
                if _flatOff > 10 then

                    local _rrWhy = velMoveThrough(hrp, routeHere(), _mainSpeed, true, true)
                    if hrp and hrp.Parent and not _G.Skyr0TPCancel and flatOffNow() > 10
                       and _rrWhy ~= "lagback" and nextSpot() then
                        velMoveThrough(hrp, routeHere(), _mainSpeed, true, true)
                    end
                    if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                        _vzL(hrp)
                        _vzA(hrp)
                    end
                end
            end

            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + facingDir)
            end
            vZero(hrp)

            local syncFrames = 3
            local syncConn
            syncConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
                if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then syncConn:Disconnect(); return end
                syncFrames = syncFrames - 1
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + facingDir)
                _vzL(hrp)
                _vzA(hrp)
                if syncFrames <= 0 then syncConn:Disconnect() end
            end))

            local _landTries = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpMaxLandTries) or 3
            local _landGap   = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpLandCheckGap) or 0.03
            for _ = 1, _landTries do
                task.wait(_landGap)
                if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then break end
            end

            do
                local stable, _st0 = 0, os.clock()
                local _cap = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpStableWait) or 0.3
                while os.clock() - _st0 < _cap do
                    if _G.Skyr0TPCancel then break end
                    local _hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not _hrp or not _hrp.Parent then break end
                    local flat = (Vector3.new(_hrp.Position.X, 0, _hrp.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(destPos.X, 0, destPos.Z)).Magnitude
                    if flat <= 3.5 and math.abs(_hrp.Position.Y - destPos.Y) <= 4 then
                        stable = stable + 1
                        if stable >= 2 then break end
                    else
                        stable = 0
                        pcall(function() _hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + facingDir) end)
                        _vzL(_hrp)
                        _vzA(_hrp)
                    end
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end
            end
            _cloneFired = true

            if not pet.campSlot then armSteal(pet); _commitTP(pet, true) end
            local _ahrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local _clonePos = (_ahrp and _ahrp.Parent and _ahrp.Position) or destPos

            local _clonePlat = Instance.new("Part")
            _clonePlat.Name = "Skyr0TPClonePlatform"
            _clonePlat.Size = Vector3.new(12, 1, 12)
            _clonePlat.Position = Vector3.new(_clonePos.X, _clonePos.Y - 3, _clonePos.Z)
            _clonePlat.Anchored = true
            _clonePlat.CanCollide = true
            _clonePlat.Transparency = 1
            _clonePlat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            _clonePlat.Parent = workspace

            if _ahrp and _ahrp.Parent then
                _vzL(_ahrp)
                _vzA(_ahrp)
            end

            local _preClonePos, _preCloneChar
            do
                _preCloneChar = LP.Character
                local _h = _preCloneChar and _preCloneChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                _preClonePos = _h and _h.Position or destPos
            end
            local _charAdded = false
            local _caConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() _charAdded = true end)

            _G.Skyr0TpStealHold = false

            task.wait(_cloneDelay())

            if facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                local _pinHum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if _pinHum then pcall(function() _pinHum.AutoRotate = false end) end
                for _ = 1, 2 do
                    local _h = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not _h or not _h.Parent then break end
                    pcall(function()
                        _h.CFrame = CFrame.new(_h.Position, _h.Position + facingDir)
                        _vzL(_h)
                        _vzA(_h)
                    end)
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end
            end

            local _cloneOk = doClone()
            if _clonePlat then pcall(function() _clonePlat:Destroy() end); _clonePlat = nil end
            do
                local _t0 = os.clock()
                repeat
                    if _charAdded then break end
                    if LP.Character ~= _preCloneChar then break end
                    local _h = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if _h then
                        local _dx = _h.Position.X - _preClonePos.X
                        local _dz = _h.Position.Z - _preClonePos.Z
                        local _dy = _h.Position.Y - _preClonePos.Y
                        if (_dx * _dx + _dz * _dz) > 4 then break end
                        if _dy * _dy > 1 then break end
                    end
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                until os.clock() - _t0 > (tonumber(_G.Skyr0TpCloneSettle) or 0.6)
            end
            if _caConn then _caConn:Disconnect() end

            pcall(function()
                local _rh = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if _rh then _rh.AutoRotate = true end
            end)

            task.wait(0.1)

            if _G.Skyr0Retarget ~= false and not pet.campSlot then
                pcall(function()
                    local fresh = scanAllPets(true)
                    if type(fresh) ~= "table" then return end
                    local still, cands = false, {}
                    for _, fp in ipairs(fresh) do
                        if not fp.conveyor and fp.plot == pet.plot then
                            cands[#cands + 1] = fp
                            if tostring(fp.slot) == tostring(pet.slot) and fp.index == pet.index then still = true end
                        end
                    end
                    if not still and #cands > 0 then
                        local _hr = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local np = _pickByMode(cands, _hr or hrp) or cands[1]
                        if np and np.position then
                            pet, petPos = np, np.position
                            armSteal(np); _commitTP(np, false)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            goToBrainrot(petPos, pet and pet.slot)
            isTeleporting = false
            if _G.Skyr0TPCancel then _tpAnchor = nil; _tpCommit.pet = nil; return end
            if pet.campSlot then

                pcall(function()
                    local _ch = LP.Character
                    if _ch and pet.position then
                        _ch:PivotTo(CFrame.new(pet.position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)))
                    end
                end)
                pcall(settleAfterTP)
                _tpAnchor = nil
                return
            end
            armSteal(pet); _commitTP(pet, true)
            pcall(settleAfterTP)
            _tpAnchor = nil
        end

        SkyrTP.runCarpet = function(pet)
            local char = LP.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hrp or not hum or not pet then return end
            local model = pet.model
            local function livePos()
                if not model or not model.Parent then return nil end
                if model:IsA("BasePart") then return model.Position end
                local p = model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true) or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                return p and p.Position or nil
            end
            local p0, t0 = livePos() or pet.position, os.clock()
            pcall(function()
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)

            SkyrTP.aim = p0
            pcall(fireGrapple)
            local _hasCarpet = false
            for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do if char:FindFirstChild(n) then _hasCarpet = true; break end end
            if not _hasCarpet then pcall(carpetEngage, true) else equipCarpet() end
            if _G.Skyr0TPCancel then return end

            local p1 = livePos()
            if not p1 then  return end
            local dt = math.max(os.clock() - t0, 0.05)
            local vel = (p1 - p0) / dt
            vel = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
            if vel.Magnitude > 40 then vel = vel.Unit * 40 end
            local spd = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.TPVelocity) or tonumber(Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed) or 450, 200, 1200)
            local eta = (p1 - hrp.Position).Magnitude / spd
            local lead = p1 + vel * eta + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

            if (lead - hrp.Position).Magnitude > 25 then
                local route = computeRoute(hrp.Position, lead, nil)
                if not route or #route == 0 then route = { lead } end
                pcall(velMoveThrough, hrp, route, spd, false, true)
            end
            if _G.Skyr0TPCancel then return end

            local tc, stall, last = os.clock(), 0, math.huge
            while os.clock() - tc < 4 do
                if not hrp.Parent then break end
                local lp = livePos()
                if not lp then break end
                local diff = (lp + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) - hrp.Position
                local mag = diff.Magnitude
                if mag <= 5 then break end
                if mag > last - 0.05 then stall = stall + 1 else stall = 0 end
                last = mag
                if stall >= 30 then break end
                equipCarpet()
                local v = diff.Unit * math.min(spd, mag * 20)
                local cap = _climbCap()
                if v.Y > cap then v = Vector3.new(v.X, cap, v.Z) end
                _setFlightVel(hrp, v)
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
            vZero(hrp)
        end
        SkyrTP.run = run
        SkyrTP.carpetEngage = carpetEngage
        SkyrTP.fireGrapple = fireGrapple
        return SkyrTP
    end)()
    _G.Skyr0MK = MV.mk

    _G.Skyr0CarpetPick = function(allPets, hrp)
        if type(allPets) ~= "table" then
            local ok, r = pcall(scanAllPets, true)
            if not ok or type(r) ~= "table" then return nil end
            allPets = r
        end
        hrp = hrp or (LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
        local minGen = parseMinGen(Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.MinGenForTp) or 0
        local belt, seen = {}, 0
        for _, p in ipairs(allPets) do
            if p.conveyor and p.model and p.model.Parent then
                seen = seen + 1
                local prio = _G.Skyr0IsPriority and _G.Skyr0IsPriority(p.name, p.index, p.mutation)
                if prio or minGen <= 0 or (tonumber(p.mps) or 0) >= minGen then belt[#belt + 1] = p end
            end
        end
        _G.Skyr0CarpetCounts = { seen = seen, passed = #belt }
        if #belt == 0 then return nil end
        local okP, pick = pcall(_pickByMode, belt, hrp)
        if okP and type(pick) == "table" then return pick end
        pick = belt[1]
        for _, p in ipairs(belt) do if (p.mps or 0) > (pick.mps or 0) then pick = p end end
        return pick
    end

    _G.Skyr0CarpetBeats = function(c, b)
        if type(c) ~= "table" then return false end
        if type(b) ~= "table" then return true end

        local prioOn = Config.AutoTPPriority == true
            or (_G.Skyr0ResolveMode and _G.Skyr0ResolveMode() == "Priority")
        local rank = prioOn and _G.Skyr0PrioRank or nil
        local rc = rank and rank(c.name, c.index)
        local rb = rank and rank(b.name, b.index)
        if rc and not rb then return true end
        if rb and not rc then return false end
        if rc and rb then return rc < rb end

        return (tonumber(c.mps) or 0) > (tonumber(b.mps) or 0)
    end

    local function doVelocityTP(forcePet)
        if isTeleporting and (os.clock() - _tpStartedAt) < 30 then return end
        if _G.Skyr0CarpetOffForTP then pcall(_G.Skyr0CarpetOffForTP) end
        isTeleporting = true
        _tpStartedAt = os.clock()
        _cloneTP = false
        _cloneFired = false
        clearViz()
        if not NetModule then pcall(loadNet) end

        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum then isTeleporting = false; return end

        _G.isCloning = false
        pcall(function()
            hrp.Anchored = false
            hum.PlatformStand = false
            hum.Sit = false
            hum.Jump = false
            hum.AutoRotate = true
        end)
        for _, _pn in ipairs({ "Skyr0ClonePlatform", "Skyr0TempPlatform" }) do
            local _po = workspace:FindFirstChild(_pn)
            if _po then pcall(function() _po:Destroy() end) end
        end
        vZero(hrp)

        local _okScan, allPets = pcall(scanAllPetsFresh, 0.15)
        if not _okScan or type(allPets) ~= "table" then
            isTeleporting = false; return
        end
        local function _nBase(t)
            local n = 0
            for _, p in ipairs(t) do if not p.conveyor then n = n + 1 end end
            return n
        end

        if _nBase(allPets) == 0 and not forcePet
           and not (Config.TpSettings.CarpetBrainrotTP and #allPets > 0) then
            local _t0 = os.clock()
            while _nBase(allPets) == 0 and os.clock() - _t0 < 6 do
                task.wait(0.05)
                local ok2, r = pcall(scanAllPets, true)
                if ok2 and type(r) == "table" then allPets = r end
            end
        end

        if #allPets == 0 then isTeleporting = false; return end

        local pet = type(forcePet) == "table" and forcePet or nil
        if not pet and manuallySelectedUID then
            for _, p in ipairs(allPets) do
                if p.plot and p.slot and (p.plot .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot)) == manuallySelectedUID then pet = p; break end
            end

            if not pet then pet = _petPorUID(manuallySelectedUID) end

            if not pet then isTeleporting = false; return end
        end

        if not pet then
            local locked   = _G.Skyr0TPLockedPet
            local lockedAt = tonumber(_G.Skyr0TPLockedAt) or 0
            _G.Skyr0TPLockedPet, _G.Skyr0TPLockedAt = nil, nil
            if type(locked) == "table" and locked.plot and locked.slot
               and (os.clock() - lockedAt) < 20
               and not (_G.Skyr0IsMyPlotCached and _G.Skyr0IsMyPlotCached(locked.plot)) then
                local uid = tostring(locked.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(locked.slot)
                for _, p in ipairs(allPets) do
                    if not p.conveyor and p.plot and p.slot
                       and (p.plot .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot)) == uid then
                        pet = p; break
                    end
                end
                if not pet then pet = _petPorUID(uid) end
            end
        end

        local _armedRanked = (type(_stealTarget) == "table" and _G.Skyr0PrioRank)
            and _G.Skyr0PrioRank(_stealTarget.name, _stealTarget.index) or nil
        local _listInUse = (type(priorityList) == "table" and #priorityList > 0)
        if not pet and type(_stealTarget) == "table"
           and (_armedRanked or not _listInUse)
           and _stealTarget.plot and _stealTarget.slot and not _stealTarget.conveyor
           and not (_G.Skyr0IsMyPlotCached and _G.Skyr0IsMyPlotCached(_stealTarget.plot)) then
            local uid = tostring(_stealTarget.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(_stealTarget.slot)
            for _, p in ipairs(allPets) do
                if not p.conveyor and p.plot and p.slot
                   and (p.plot .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot)) == uid then
                    pet = p; break
                end
            end

            if not pet then pet = _petPorUID(uid) end
        end

        if not pet then

            local semEsteira = {}
            for _, p in ipairs(allPets) do
                if not p.conveyor then semEsteira[#semEsteira+1] = p end
            end

            if #semEsteira == 0 and not (Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.CarpetBrainrotTP) then
                isTeleporting = false; return
            end

            if #semEsteira > 0 then pet = _pickByMode(semEsteira, hrp) or semEsteira[1] end
        end

        if not forcePet and not manuallySelectedUID
           and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.CarpetBrainrotTP then
            local okC, belt = pcall(_G.Skyr0CarpetPick, allPets, hrp)
            if okC and belt and _G.Skyr0CarpetBeats(belt, pet) then pet = belt end
        end
        if not pet then isTeleporting = false; return end

        if pet and pet.plot and _G.Skyr0IsMyPlotCached and _G.Skyr0IsMyPlotCached(pet.plot) then
            local safe = {}
            for _, p in ipairs(allPets) do
                if not p.conveyor and p.plot and not _G.Skyr0IsMyPlotCached(p.plot) then
                    safe[#safe+1] = p
                end
            end
            if #safe == 0 then
                isTeleporting = false
                return
            end

            pet = _pickByMode(safe, hrp) or safe[1]
        end

        if pet then
            local _rank = _G.Skyr0PrioRank and _G.Skyr0PrioRank(pet.name, pet.index)
            local _onServer = 0
            for _, p in ipairs(allPets) do
                if not p.conveyor and _G.Skyr0PrioRank and _G.Skyr0PrioRank(p.name, p.index) then
                    _onServer = _onServer + 1
                end
            end
        end

        if not pet.conveyor and not pet.campSlot then armSteal(pet); _commitTP(pet, false) end

        _tpAnchor = pet.position

        local _tpSpd = (Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed) or 400
        local _cloneDelay = (Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal) or 0.35

        local petPos = pet.position
        local petName = pet.name

        local adjY = petPos.Y
        if TALL_PETS[petName] then adjY = petPos.Y - TALL_OFFSET end

        local _f2FromF1 = _G.Skyr0IsF2FromF1 and _G.Skyr0IsF2FromF1(petPos.Y) or false
        if _f2FromF1 then adjY = math.min(adjY, UPPER_Y_THRESHOLD) end
        local coordTable = adjY > UPPER_Y_THRESHOLD and UPPER or LOWER

        if pet.conveyor then
            local maxHP = hum.MaxHealth
            local healConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if hum and hum.Parent then hum.Health = maxHP end
            end)
            local okR, errR = pcall(MV.mk.runCarpet, pet)
            if not okR then  end
            healConn:Disconnect()

            if okR and not _G.Skyr0TPCancel and _G.Skyr0SetAutoBuy then
                pcall(_G.Skyr0SetAutoBuy, true)
            end
            _tpAnchor = nil
            isTeleporting = false
            return
        end

        local _okTp, _errTp = pcall(MV.mk.run, pet, true)
        if not _okTp then
            _tpCommit.pet = nil
            _cloneTP = false
            pcall(settleAfterTP)
            _tpAnchor = nil
            isTeleporting = false
        end
    end

    doGrabbleVelocityTP = doVelocityTP
    _G.Skyr0StartSideTP = doVelocityTP

    _G.Skyr0DoVelocityTP = doVelocityTP

    _G.Skyr0ForceIdleTP = function() isTeleporting = false end

    _G.Skyr0TPActive = function()
        return isTeleporting and (os.clock() - _tpStartedAt) < 30
    end

    _G.Skyr0_ExecuteManualTP = function()
        task.spawn(function() pcall(doVelocityTP) end)
    end

    _G.Skyr0TPCarpetBrainrot = function()
        if _G.Skyr0TPActive and _G.Skyr0TPActive() then return false end
        local hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return false end
        local okP, pick = pcall(_G.Skyr0CarpetPick, nil, hrp)
        if not okP or type(pick) ~= "table" then
            local c = _G.Skyr0CarpetCounts or {}
            if ShowNotification then pcall(ShowNotification, "CARPET TP", "Nothing on the carpet qualifies") end
            return false
        end
        task.spawn(function() pcall(doVelocityTP, pick) end)
        return true
    end

    task.spawn(function() pcall(loadModules) pcall(loadNet) end)
    task.spawn(function()
        local char = LP.Character or LP.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
        char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        pcall(loadModules); pcall(loadNet)
        local _t0 = os.clock()
        repeat
            local ok, pets = pcall(scanAllPets)
            if ok and pets and #pets > 0 then break end
            task.wait(0.3)
        until os.clock() - _t0 > 12
        _started = true
    end)
end

local function _fxTpDispatch()
    if _G.Skyr0CarpetOffForTP then pcall(_G.Skyr0CarpetOffForTP) end

    if _G.Skyr0IsStealing and _G.Skyr0IsStealing() then
        return false
    end

    if _G.Skyr0TPActive and _G.Skyr0TPActive() then return false end
    _G.TPVelocity        = math.max(tonumber(Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed) or 450, 450)
    _G.TPCloneDelay      = tonumber(Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal) or 0.1
    _G.LandingDelay      = _G.LandingDelay or 0.4
    _G.Skyr0StealModeStr = (tostring(Config.StealMode or "")):lower()
    _G.Skyr0StealTargetUID = manuallySelectedUID and ((tostring(manuallySelectedUID):gsub("_", "|"))) or nil

    _G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS = Config.PriorityList or _G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS
    _G.Skyr0TPCancel = false

    if _G.Skyr0DoVelocityTP then return _G.Skyr0DoVelocityTP() end
end
_G.Skyr0StartSideTP      = _fxTpDispatch
doGrabbleVelocityTP    = _fxTpDispatch
_G.Skyr0_ExecuteManualTP = function() task.spawn(function() pcall(_fxTpDispatch) end) end

_G.Skyr0ManualTP = function()

    if _G.Skyr0TPActive and _G.Skyr0TPActive() then return end
    _G.Skyr0TPCancel = false

    if _G.Skyr0RunTPOnLoad then task.spawn(_G.Skyr0RunTPOnLoad, true); return end
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, err = pcall(_fxTpDispatch)
        if not ok then  end
    end)
end

do
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer

    local resetCooldown          = false
    local resetThread            = nil
    local resetSuccessful        = false
    local stopResetSequence      = false
    local cameraLocked           = false
    local lockedCameraCFrame     = nil
    local _lastInstaResetRequest = 0
    local prevAntiDie            = nil

    local function restoreAntiDie()
        if prevAntiDie == nil then return end
        local was = prevAntiDie
        prevAntiDie = nil
        _G.AntiDieDisabled = was
        if not was and _G.setupAntiDie then
            task.delay(0.6, function() pcall(_G.setupAntiDie) end)
        end
    end

    local function instaResetFast()
        if resetCooldown then return end
        resetCooldown     = true
        resetSuccessful   = false
        stopResetSequence = false
        cameraLocked      = false

        local character = LP.Character
        if not character then resetCooldown = false return end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then resetCooldown = false return end

        _G.__Skyr0Resetting = true
        if prevAntiDie == nil then prevAntiDie = _G.AntiDieDisabled == true end
        _G.AntiDieDisabled = true
        if _G.AntiDieConnection then
            pcall(function() _G.AntiDieConnection:Disconnect() end)
            _G.AntiDieConnection = nil
        end
        if _G.invisibleStealEnabled and _G._forceInvisToggle then pcall(_G._forceInvisToggle) end

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
            lockedCameraCFrame = cam.CFrame
            cameraLocked       = true
            cam.CFrame         = lockedCameraCFrame
        end

        local isRespawning = false

        resetThread = task.spawn(function()
            local attempts          = 0
            local maxAttempts       = 40
            local originalHipHeight = humanoid.HipHeight

            while character and character.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0
                  and not isRespawning and not stopResetSequence do
                if LP.Character ~= character then
                    isRespawning = true
                    break
                end
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid.HipHeight  = 1e30
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                    local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if rootPart then rootPart.CanCollide = false end
                    for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                            part.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                end)

                if not character or not character.Parent or not humanoid
                   or humanoid.Health <= 0 or LP.Character ~= character then
                    resetSuccessful = true
                    break
                end

                attempts = attempts + 1
                if attempts >= maxAttempts then break end
                task.wait(0.05)
            end

            if not resetSuccessful then
                if character and character.Parent and humanoid
                   and humanoid.Health > 0 and not isRespawning then
                    pcall(function() humanoid.Health = 0 end)
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    if not character.Parent or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                        resetSuccessful = true
                    end
                end
            end

            if not resetSuccessful and character and character.Parent and humanoid then
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid.HipHeight = originalHipHeight
                    local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if rootPart then rootPart.CanCollide = true end
                    for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                            part.CanCollide = true
                        end
                    end
                end)

                cameraLocked = false
                restoreAntiDie()
                _G.__Skyr0Resetting = nil
            end

            resetCooldown     = false
            resetThread       = nil
            stopResetSequence = false
        end)
    end

    local function instaReset()
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - _lastInstaResetRequest < 0.75 then return false end
        _lastInstaResetRequest = now
        instaResetFast()
        return true
    end

    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        stopResetSequence = true
        if resetThread then pcall(task.cancel, resetThread); resetThread = nil end
        resetCooldown = false
        cameraLocked  = false
        restoreAntiDie()
        _G.__Skyr0Resetting = nil
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.016)
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cameraLocked and lockedCameraCFrame and cam then
                cam.CFrame = lockedCameraCFrame
            end
        end
    end)

    _G.Skyr0InstaReset = instaReset
end

do
    local LPx = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local POTION = "Body Swap Potion"
    local GRACE  = 3
    local lastDeath = -math.huge
    local lastSwap  = -math.huge
    local loadedAt  = os.clock()
    local ARM_DELAY = 8

    local knownChar = LPx.Character

    _G.Skyr0AntiBodySwap = (Config and Config.AntiBodySwap) or false

    local function watchDeath(char)
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        hum.Died:Connect(function() lastDeath = os.clock() end)
    end
    watchDeath(LPx.Character)

    local function swapBack()
        local char = LPx.Character
        local bp = LPx:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not (char and hum) then return false end

        local tool = (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(POTION)) or char:FindFirstChild(POTION)
        if not tool then
            ShowNotification("BODY SWAP", "swapped -- no potion to swap back")
            return false
        end
        pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end)
        task.wait(0.1)
        pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
        ShowNotification("BODY SWAP", "swapped back")
        return true
    end

    LPx.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        watchDeath(char)
        local prev = knownChar
        knownChar = char
        if not _G.Skyr0AntiBodySwap then return end

        local now = os.clock()

        if prev == nil then return end
        if (now - loadedAt) < ARM_DELAY then return end

        if (now - lastDeath) <= GRACE then return end

        if _G.__meerkoResetBusy or _G.__Skyr0Resetting then return end

        if (now - lastSwap) < 5 then return end
        lastSwap = now

        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.35)
            swapBack()
        end)
    end)

    function _G.Skyr0SetAntiBodySwap(on)
        _G.Skyr0AntiBodySwap = on and true or false
        Config.AntiBodySwap = _G.Skyr0AntiBodySwap
        pcall(saveConfig)
    end
end

do
    local LPx = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local RSx = game:GetService("RunService")

    local JUMP_BACK   = 40
    local DRIFT_BACK  = 60
    local DRIFT_WIN   = 0.75
    local COOLDOWN    = 6
    local MAX_RETRIES = 3

    local lastPos, origin, retries, lastRetry = nil, nil, 0, -math.huge
    local wasPending = false

    local drift, driftAt = 0, 0

    local function hrp()
        local c = LPx.Character
        return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    RSx.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not _G.AutoRecoverLagback then lastPos = nil; return end

        local pending = _G.__Skyr0TPPending ~= nil
        if not pending then

            if wasPending then wasPending, retries, origin = false, 0, nil end
            lastPos = nil
            return
        end

        local root = hrp()
        if not root then lastPos = nil; return end
        local pos = root.Position

        if not wasPending then
            wasPending = true
            origin = pos
            lastPos = pos
            return
        end
        if not lastPos or not origin then lastPos = pos; return end

        local step = (pos - lastPos).Magnitude
        local wasAway = (lastPos - origin).Magnitude
        local nowAway = (pos - origin).Magnitude
        lastPos = pos

        local now = os.clock()
        local backwards = nowAway < wasAway

        if (now - driftAt) > DRIFT_WIN then drift, driftAt = 0, now end
        if backwards then drift = drift + (wasAway - nowAway) end

        local snapped = (step >= JUMP_BACK) and backwards
        local drifted = drift >= DRIFT_BACK
        if not (snapped or drifted) then return end
        drift, driftAt = 0, now

        if _G.Skyr0TPActive and _G.Skyr0TPActive() then return end

        if _G.Skyr0LagbackRetry ~= true then return end
        if retries >= MAX_RETRIES or (now - lastRetry) < COOLDOWN then return end
        retries = retries + 1
        lastRetry = now

        if ShowNotification then
            ShowNotification("LAGBACK", "teleport rejected -- retrying (" .. retries .. ")")
        end

        task.spawn(function()

            local png = (_G.Skyr0Ping and _G.Skyr0Ping()) or 0.1
            task.wait(math.clamp(png * 3 + 0.3, 0.6, 2.5))
            if _G.Skyr0TPActive and _G.Skyr0TPActive() then return end
            if _G.Skyr0LaunchTP then pcall(_G.Skyr0LaunchTP, false) end
        end)
    end)
end

;(function()
    local Players    = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
    if _G.Skyr0NoCollidePlayers == nil then _G.Skyr0NoCollidePlayers = true end
    local function on() return _G.Skyr0NoCollidePlayers ~= false end

    local pinned  = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local hooked  = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local ncBag   = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local THEIRS  = { "HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso", "Head" }

    local function pin(p)
        if not p:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        if p.CanCollide then p.CanCollide = false end
        if pinned[p] then return end
        pinned[p] = true
        pcall(function()
            p:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanCollide"):Connect(function()
                if p.CanCollide and on() then p.CanCollide = false end
            end)
        end)
    end
    local function pinModel(m)
        if not m or not on() then return end
        for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do pin(d) end
        if not hooked[m] then
            hooked[m] = true
            m.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d) task.defer(pin, d) end)
        end
    end
    local function constrain(m)
        if not on() or _G.Skyr0NoCollideConstraints == false or not m then return end
        local c = LP.Character
        local mine = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not mine then return end
        local bag = ncBag[m]
        if not bag or bag.root ~= mine then bag = { root = mine }; ncBag[m] = bag end
        for _, n in ipairs(THEIRS) do
            local part = m:FindFirstChild(n)
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                local k = bag[n]
                if not (k and k.Parent) then
                    pcall(function()
                        local nc = Instance.new("NoCollisionConstraint")
                        nc.Part0, nc.Part1 = mine, part
                        nc.Parent = mine
                        bag[n] = nc
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function handle(m)
        pinModel(m)
        constrain(m)
    end

    local function hookPlayer(pl)
        if pl == LP then return end
        if pl.Character then handle(pl.Character) end
        pl.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            task.wait(0.05)
            handle(ch)
            task.wait(0.15)
            handle(ch)
        end)
    end
    for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do hookPlayer(pl) end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hookPlayer)

    local function isClone(inst)
        local f = _G.Skyr0IsOtherClone
        if f then return f(inst) end
        return inst.Name:match("^%d+_Clone$") ~= nil and inst.Name ~= tostring(LP.UserId) .. "_Clone"
    end
    local function scanClones()
        for _, c in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if isClone(c) then handle(c) end
        end
    end
    workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
        task.defer(function()
            if c.Parent == workspace and isClone(c) then handle(c) end
        end)
        task.delay(0.2, function()
            if c.Parent == workspace and isClone(c) then handle(c) end
        end)
    end)
    scanClones()

    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.3)
        for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if pl ~= LP and pl.Character then constrain(pl.Character) end
        end
        for _, c in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if isClone(c) then constrain(c) end
        end
    end)

    local acc = 0
    RunService.Stepped:Connect(function(_, dt)
        if not on() or _G.isCloning == true then return end
        acc = acc + (dt or 0.016)
        if acc < 0.1 then return end
        acc = 0
        local c = LP.Character
        local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end
        local mp = root.Position
        local rng = tonumber(_G.Skyr0NoCollideRange) or 28
        local function sweep(m)
            local h = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if h and (h.Position - mp).Magnitude <= rng then
                for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide then d.CanCollide = false end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if pl ~= LP and pl.Character then sweep(pl.Character) end
        end
        for _, cl in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if isClone(cl) then sweep(cl) end
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(1)
            if on() and _G.isCloning ~= true then
                for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if pl ~= LP and pl.Character then handle(pl.Character) end
                end
                scanClones()
            end
        end
    end)
end)()

;(function()
    if type(_G.Skyr0PlotCleanerRestore) == "function" then pcall(_G.Skyr0PlotCleanerRestore) end
    if type(_G.Skyr0LoaderStop) == "function" then pcall(_G.Skyr0LoaderStop) end
    _G.Skyr0PlotCleaner = false
    local on = false

    local keepSet, keepAt = {}, 0
    _G.Skyr0PlotCleanerKeep = function(plotName)
        if os.clock() - keepAt > 0.5 then
            keepAt = os.clock()
            local k = {}
            local camp = _G.Skyr0Camp
            if camp and camp.active and camp.plotName then k[camp.plotName] = true end
            local st = _G.Skyr0_StealStatus
            if st and st.target and st.target.plot then k[st.target.plot] = true end
            local sel = SharedState and SharedState.SelectedPetData
            if sel and sel.plot then k[sel.plot] = true end
            local cache = SharedState and SharedState.AllAnimalsCache
            if type(cache) == "table" and _G.Skyr0IsPriority then
                for _, a in ipairs(cache) do
                    if a and a.plot and not k[a.plot] then
                        local ok, isP = pcall(_G.Skyr0IsPriority, a.name, a.index, a.mutation)
                        if ok and isP then k[a.plot] = true end
                    end
                end
            end
            keepSet = k
        end
        return keepSet[plotName] == true
    end

    local function runLoader()

        local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer or Players.PlayerAdded:Wait()
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local THRESHOLD = tonumber(_G.Skyr0LoaderThreshold) or 1000000

        local EVAL_EVERY = tonumber(_G.Skyr0LoaderScanEvery) or 0.08
        local VERIFY_AFTER_RESTORE = tonumber(_G.Skyr0LoaderVerifyAfter) or 3.5
        if _G.Skyr0LoaderStop then pcall(_G.Skyr0LoaderStop) end

        local running, connections = true, {}

        local stash = Instance.new("Folder")
        stash.Name = "Skyr0Stash"
        local state = {}

        local function plotBox(plot, s)
            if not s.pivot then
                local ok, pv = pcall(function() return plot:GetPivot() end)
                if ok and pv then s.pivot = pv.Position end
            end
            if not s.boxCF then
                local ok, cf, size = pcall(function() return plot:GetBoundingBox() end)
                if ok and cf and size and size.X > 1 and size.Z > 1 then s.boxCF, s.boxSize = cf, size end
            end
            return s.boxCF, s.boxSize
        end

        local function bodyPoints()
            local pts = {}
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if char then
                for _, n in ipairs({ "HumanoidRootPart", "Head", "UpperTorso", "Torso", "LowerTorso" }) do
                    local p = char:FindFirstChild(n)
                    if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then pts[#pts + 1] = p.Position end
                end
                local ok, pv = pcall(function() return char:GetPivot() end)
                if ok and pv then pts[#pts + 1] = pv.Position end
            end
            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            local subj = cam and cam.CameraSubject
            if subj then
                if subj:IsA("Humanoid") then subj = subj.RootPart end
                if subj and subj:IsA("BasePart") then pts[#pts + 1] = subj.Position end
            end
            return pts
        end

        local function playerInPlot(s)
            if not (s.boxCF or s.pivot) then return false end
            local m = tonumber(_G.Skyr0LoaderInsideMargin) or 6
            local r = tonumber(_G.Skyr0LoaderInsideRadius) or 26
            for _, pos in ipairs(bodyPoints()) do
                if s.boxCF then
                    local lp = s.boxCF:PointToObjectSpace(pos)
                    if math.abs(lp.X) <= s.boxSize.X / 2 + m and math.abs(lp.Z) <= s.boxSize.Z / 2 + m
                        and lp.Y >= -s.boxSize.Y / 2 - 15 and lp.Y <= s.boxSize.Y / 2 + 30 then
                        return true
                    end
                end
                if s.pivot and math.abs(pos.X - s.pivot.X) <= r and math.abs(pos.Z - s.pivot.Z) <= r
                    and pos.Y >= s.pivot.Y - 20 and pos.Y <= s.pivot.Y + 90 then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function removeOutline(s)
            if s.outline then pcall(function() s.outline:Destroy() end); s.outline = nil end
        end
        local function addOutline(s)
            removeOutline(s)
            if _G.Skyr0LoaderOutline == false or not (s.boxCF and s.boxSize) then return end
            local p = Instance.new("Part")
            p.Name = "Skyr0StashOutline"
            p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = false; p.CanQuery = false; p.CanTouch = false
            p.CastShadow = false; p.Transparency = 1
            p.CFrame, p.Size = s.boxCF, s.boxSize
            local box = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
            box.Adornee = p
            box.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(167, 110, 247)
            box.LineThickness = tonumber(_G.Skyr0LoaderOutlineThickness) or 0.2
            box.SurfaceTransparency = 1
            box.Parent = p
            p.Parent = Workspace
            s.outline = p
        end
        local function restorePlot(plot, s)
            s.stashed = false
            removeOutline(s)
            plot.Parent = s.parent or Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        end
        _G.Skyr0LoaderStop = function()
            running = false
            for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
            for plot, s in pairs(state) do
                if s.stashed then pcall(restorePlot, plot, s) end
                removeOutline(s)
            end
            pcall(function() stash:Destroy() end)
        end

        _G.Skyr0StashedPlots = function()
            local out = {}
            for plot, s in pairs(state) do
                if s.stashed and plot.Parent == stash then out[#out + 1] = plot end
            end
            return out
        end

        if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
        if not running then return end
        LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 60)
        do
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while running and not LocalPlayer.Character and os.clock() - t0 < 30 do task.wait(0.1) end
        end
        if not running then return end

        local plots = Workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 90)
        if not plots or not running then return end
        do
            local last, since = -1, os.clock()
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while running and os.clock() - t0 < 60 do
                local n = #plots:GetChildren()
                if n ~= last then last, since = n, os.clock() end
                if n > 0 and os.clock() - since >= (tonumber(_G.Skyr0LoaderSettle) or 0.25) then break end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end

        local datas = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Datas", 90)
        if not datas then return end
        local okAnimals, animalsData = pcall(require, datas:WaitForChild("Animals", 90))
        local okMutations, mutations = pcall(require, datas:WaitForChild("Mutations", 90))
        local okTraits, traitsData = pcall(require, datas:WaitForChild("Traits", 90))
        local okGame, gameData = pcall(require, datas:WaitForChild("Game", 90))
        if not (okAnimals and type(animalsData) == "table" and okMutations and okTraits and okGame) then
            return
        end
        local function generation(index, mutation, traits)
            local animal = animalsData[index]
            if not animal then return nil end
            local base = animal.Generation
            if not base then
                local modifier = gameData.Game and gameData.Game.AnimalGanerationModifier
                if type(modifier) ~= "number" then return nil end
                base = (animal.Price or 0) * modifier
            end
            local mult = 1
            local mutationData = mutation and mutations[mutation]
            if mutationData then mult = mult + (mutationData.Modifier or 0) end
            local sleepy = false
            for _, trait in pairs(traits or {}) do
                if trait == "Sleepy" then sleepy = true
                else
                    local traitData = traitsData[trait]
                    if traitData then mult = mult + (traitData.MultiplierModifier or 0) end
                end
            end
            local result = base * mult
            if sleepy then result = result * 0.5 end
            return math.round(result)
        end
        local packages = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages", 90)
        local syncFolder = packages and packages:WaitForChild("Synchronizer", 90)
        local requestData = syncFolder and syncFolder:WaitForChild("RequestData", 90)
        if not (requestData and requestData:IsA("RemoteFunction")) or not running then
            return
        end

        local SUF = { K = 1e3, M = 1e6, B = 1e9, T = 1e12, Qa = 1e15, Qi = 1e18,
                      Sx = 1e21, Sp = 1e24, Oc = 1e27, No = 1e30, Dc = 1e33 }
        local function parseMps(text)
            if type(text) ~= "string" then return nil end
            local num, suf = text:match("%$%s*([%d%.]+)%s*(%a*)%s*/s")
            local v = tonumber(num)
            if not v then return nil end
            if suf and suf ~= "" then
                local m = SUF[suf] or SUF[suf:sub(1, 1):upper() .. suf:sub(2):lower()]
                if not m then return nil end
                v = v * m
            end
            return v
        end
        local function readOverheads()
            local out = {}
            local debris = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")
            if not debris then return out end
            local terra = Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
            for _, d in ipairs(debris:GetDescendants()) do
                if (d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("SurfaceGui")) and d:FindFirstChild("Traits") then
                    local part = d.Parent
                    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local pos = part.Position
                        local m6 = part:FindFirstChild("__foh_transform")
                        if m6 and m6:IsA("Motor6D") and m6.Part0 and m6.Part0 ~= terra and m6.Part0:IsA("BasePart") then
                            pos = m6.Part0.Position
                        end
                        if pos.Y < 9000 then
                            local mps
                            for _, t in ipairs(d:GetDescendants()) do
                                if t:IsA("TextLabel") then
                                    mps = parseMps(t.Text)
                                    if mps then break end
                                end
                            end
                            if mps then out[#out + 1] = { pos = pos, mps = mps } end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return out
        end
        local podCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        local function podiumList(plot)
            local c = podCache[plot]
            if c and os.clock() - c.at < 10 and #c.list > 0 then return c.list end
            local list = {}
            local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if pods then
                for _, p in ipairs(pods:GetChildren()) do
                    local ok, cf = pcall(p.GetPivot, p)
                    if ok then list[#list + 1] = { slot = p.Name, pos = cf.Position } end
                end
            end
            podCache[plot] = { at = os.clock(), list = list }
            return list
        end

        local function buildDisplayed(list)
            local disp, pods = {}, {}
            for _, plot in ipairs(list) do
                for _, pd in ipairs(podiumList(plot)) do
                    pods[#pods + 1] = { plot = plot.Name, slot = pd.slot, pos = pd.pos }
                end
            end
            for _, oh in ipairs(readOverheads()) do
                local best, bestD = nil, 9
                for _, pd in ipairs(pods) do
                    local dx, dz = oh.pos.X - pd.pos.X, oh.pos.Z - pd.pos.Z
                    local d = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)
                    if d <= bestD then best, bestD = pd, d end
                end
                if best then
                    disp[best.plot] = disp[best.plot] or {}
                    local cur = disp[best.plot][best.slot]
                    if not cur or oh.mps > cur then disp[best.plot][best.slot] = oh.mps end
                end
            end
            return disp
        end

        local function signText(plot)
            local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            local surfaceGui = sign and sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
            local label = surfaceGui and surfaceGui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
            return label and label.Text or nil
        end
        local function findMyPlot()
            for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                if sign then
                    local marker = sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
                    if marker and marker:IsA("BillboardGui") and marker.Enabled then
                        return plot
                    end
                    local text = signText(plot)
                    if text then
                        text = text:lower()
                        if text:find(LocalPlayer.Name:lower(), 1, true)
                            or text:find(LocalPlayer.DisplayName:lower(), 1, true) then
                            return plot
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function readPlot(plot, disp)
            local ok, result = pcall(function() return requestData:InvokeServer(plot.Name) end)
            local cache = ok and type(result) == "table" and result or nil
            if type(cache) ~= "table" then return nil end
            local animals = cache.AnimalList or cache.Animals
            if type(animals) ~= "table" then

                if cache.Owner == nil and next(cache) ~= nil then
                    local text = signText(plot)
                    if text and text ~= "" and text ~= "Empty Base" then return nil end
                    animals = {}
                else
                    return nil
                end
            end
            local info, parts = { top = 0, uncertain = false }, {}
            for slot, animal in pairs(animals) do
                if type(animal) == "table" and animal.Index then
                    local computed = generation(animal.Index, animal.Mutation, animal.Traits)
                    if type(computed) ~= "number" then return nil end
                    local shown = disp and disp[plot.Name] and disp[plot.Name][tostring(slot)]
                    if shown == nil then info.uncertain = true end
                    local v = math.max(computed, shown or 0)
                    if v > info.top then info.top = v end
                    parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(slot) .. "=" .. tostring(animal.Index) .. "/" .. tostring(animal.Mutation)
                end
            end
            table.sort(parts)
            info.sig = table.concat(parts, ",")
            return info, cache.Owner
        end

        local myPlotName, cycles = nil, 0
        local function stateOf(plot)
            local s = state[plot]
            if not s then
                s = { stashed = false, notKeep = 0, verifyUntil = 0 }
                state[plot] = s
                connections[#connections + 1] = plot.Destroying:Connect(function()
                    removeOutline(s)
                    state[plot] = nil
                end)
            end
            return s
        end
        local function stashPlot(plot, s, sig)
            plotBox(plot, s)

            if playerInPlot(s) then return end
            s.stashed, s.sig, s.parent = true, sig, plot.Parent
            plot.Parent = stash
            addOutline(s)
        end

        connections[#connections + 1] = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not running then return end
            for plot, s in pairs(state) do
                if s.stashed and plot.Parent == stash and (s.boxCF or plotBox(plot, s) or s.pivot)
                    and playerInPlot(s) then
                    pcall(restorePlot, plot, s)
                    s.verifyUntil, s.notKeep, s.lastSig = 0, 0, nil
                    s.proxRestored, s.proxSig = true, s.sig
                elseif s.proxRestored and not s.stashed and plot.Parent ~= stash and not playerInPlot(s) then
                    s.proxRestored = false
                    if s.proxSig and plot.Name ~= myPlotName
                        and not (_G.Skyr0PlotCleanerKeep and _G.Skyr0PlotCleanerKeep(plot.Name)) then
                        pcall(stashPlot, plot, s, s.proxSig)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        local lastDisp, lastDispAt = nil, 0
        local function cycle()
            local visible = plots:GetChildren()
            local all = {}
            for _, plot in ipairs(visible) do all[#all + 1] = plot end
            for plot, s in pairs(state) do
                if s.stashed then
                    if plot.Parent == stash then all[#all + 1] = plot
                    elseif plot.Parent ~= nil then s.stashed = false; removeOutline(s) end
                end
            end
            myPlotName = (findMyPlot() or {}).Name or myPlotName

            if not lastDisp or os.clock() - lastDispAt > 0.5 then
                lastDisp, lastDispAt = buildDisplayed(visible), os.clock()
            end
            local disp = lastDisp
            local results, pending = {}, #all
            for _, plot in ipairs(all) do
                task.spawn(function()
                    local ok, info, owner = pcall(readPlot, plot, disp)
                    results[plot] = ok and info or nil
                    if ok and info and not myPlotName and owner ~= nil then
                        if owner == LocalPlayer or owner == LocalPlayer.Name or owner == LocalPlayer.UserId
                            or (type(owner) == "table" and
                                (owner.UserId == LocalPlayer.UserId or owner.Name == LocalPlayer.Name)) then
                            myPlotName = plot.Name
                        end
                    end
                    pending = pending - 1
                end)
            end
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while pending > 0 and running and os.clock() - t0 < (tonumber(_G.Skyr0LoaderReadWait) or 0.9) do RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
            if not running then return end
            cycles = cycles + 1

            local ready = myPlotName ~= nil and cycles >= 2
            local now = os.clock()
            for _, plot in ipairs(all) do
                local s = stateOf(plot)
                local info = results[plot]
                if s.stashed then

                    if (info and info.sig ~= s.sig) or (_G.Skyr0PlotCleanerKeep and _G.Skyr0PlotCleanerKeep(plot.Name)) then
                        restorePlot(plot, s)
                        s.verifyUntil, s.notKeep, s.lastSig = now + VERIFY_AFTER_RESTORE, 0, nil
                    end
                else
                    plotBox(plot, s)

                    if s.proxRestored and info and (info.sig ~= s.proxSig
                        or (not info.uncertain and info.top >= THRESHOLD)) then
                        s.proxRestored = false
                    end
                    local keep = plot.Name == myPlotName or info == nil or info.top >= THRESHOLD or info.uncertain
                        or (_G.Skyr0PlotCleanerKeep and _G.Skyr0PlotCleanerKeep(plot.Name))
                        or playerInPlot(s)
                    if keep then
                        s.notKeep, s.lastSig = 0, info and info.sig
                    else
                        if s.lastSig == info.sig then s.notKeep = s.notKeep + 1 else s.notKeep = 1 end
                        s.lastSig = info.sig
                        if ready and s.notKeep >= 2 and now >= s.verifyUntil then
                            stashPlot(plot, s, info.sig)
                            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while running do
                pcall(cycle)
                task.wait(EVAL_EVERY)
            end
        end)

    end

    _G.Skyr0PlotCleanerRestore = function()
        if _G.Skyr0LoaderStop then pcall(_G.Skyr0LoaderStop) end
    end

    function _G.Skyr0SetPlotCleaner(want)
        want = (want == true)
        if on == want then return end
        on = want
        _G.Skyr0PlotCleaner = want
        Config.PlotCleaner = want
        pcall(saveConfig)
        if want then
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, err = pcall(runLoader)
                if not ok then  end
            end)
        else
            if _G.Skyr0LoaderStop then pcall(_G.Skyr0LoaderStop) end
        end
        pcall(setToggle, "Plot Cleaner", want)
    end
    if Config.PlotCleaner == true then
        task.defer(function() _G.Skyr0SetPlotCleaner(true) end)
    end
end)()

;(function()
    local Players    = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local UIS        = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer

    local RC = { active = false, target = nil, plotName = nil, status = "idle",
                 standSlot = nil, grabSlot = nil, grabEntry = nil }
    _G.Skyr0Camp = RC

    if _G.Skyr0CampDebug == nil then _G.Skyr0CampDebug = true end
    local logAt = {}
    _G.Skyr0CampLog = function(key, msg, every)
        if _G.Skyr0CampDebug ~= true then return end
        if key then
            local now = os.clock()
            if logAt[key] and now - logAt[key] < (every or 1.5) then return end
            logAt[key] = now
        end
    end
    local clog = _G.Skyr0CampLog

    local function col(key, fallback)
        local t = Theme
        return (t and t[key]) or fallback
    end
    local C_BG     = col("MainBackground", Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 20))
    local C_ROW    = col("Background", Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 32))
    local C_ACCENT = col("AccentLight", Color3.fromRGB(170, 85, 255))
    local C_ON     = col("Green", Color3.fromRGB(215, 215, 215))
    local C_TEXT   = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
    local C_DIM    = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
    local C_STAND  = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 170)
    local C_GRAB   = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    local C_STEAL  = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 190)
    local C_STEALBG = Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 58)

    local function notify(text)
        pcall(function()
            game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "Base Camp", Text = text, Duration = 4 })
        end)
    end

    local animalsData
    local function animalsTbl()
        if animalsData == nil then
            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Datas", 5):WaitForChild("Animals", 5))
            end)
            animalsData = (ok and type(res) == "table") and res or false
        end
        return animalsData or nil
    end
    local function displayNameOf(index)
        local ad = animalsTbl()
        local info = ad and index and ad[index]
        return (type(info) == "table" and info.DisplayName) or (index and tostring(index)) or nil
    end
    local function stealingWhat(plr)
        if not plr or plr:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then return nil end
        local idx = plr:GetAttribute("StealingIndex")
        if idx ~= nil and tostring(idx) ~= "" then return displayNameOf(tostring(idx)) end
        local ad = animalsTbl()
        local ch = plr.Character
        if ad and ch then
            for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("Model") and ad[d.Name] then return displayNameOf(d.Name) end
            end
        end
        return "Brainrot"
    end

    local stealInfo = {}
    RC.stealInfo = stealInfo

    local function plotOwnerName(plot)
        local sign = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        local gui  = sign and sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
        local lbl  = gui and gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
        if not (lbl and lbl.Text) then return nil end
        local nick = lbl.Text:match("^(.-)'") or lbl.Text
        nick = nick and nick:gsub("%s+$", "")
        if nick == "" then return nil end
        return nick
    end

    local function findPlotFor(plr)
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not (plots and plr) then return nil end
        local n, dn = plr.Name:lower(), (plr.DisplayName or ""):lower()
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local owner = plotOwnerName(plot)
            if owner then
                local ol = owner:lower()
                if ol == n or ol == dn then return plot end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function slotPrompt(slot)
        local base  = slot:FindFirstChild("Base")
        local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
        local att   = spawn and spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
        if att then
            for _, p in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do
                if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and p.Enabled and p.ActionText == "Steal" then return p end
            end
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(slot:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and d.Enabled and d.ActionText == "Steal" then return d end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function sortedSlots(plot)
        local pods = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        local slots = {}
        if not pods then return slots end
        for _, s in ipairs(pods:GetChildren()) do
            local base  = s:FindFirstChild("Base")
            local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
            if spawn and spawn:IsA("BasePart") then slots[#slots + 1] = s end
        end
        table.sort(slots, function(a, b)
            local na, nb = tonumber(tostring(a.Name):match("%d+")), tonumber(tostring(b.Name):match("%d+"))
            if na and nb then return na < nb end
            return tostring(a.Name) < tostring(b.Name)
        end)
        return slots
    end

    local function petInSlot(plotName, slotName)
        local cache = SharedState and SharedState.AllAnimalsCache
        if type(cache) ~= "table" then return nil end
        for _, a in ipairs(cache) do
            if a and a.plot == plotName and tostring(a.slot) == tostring(slotName) then return a end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function scanCamp(plot)
        local slots = sortedSlots(plot)
        local stand, grab, grabD = nil, nil, math.huge
        local full = #slots > 0
        local c = LP.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        for _, slot in ipairs(slots) do
            if slotPrompt(slot) then
                local d = hrp and (slot.Base.Spawn.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude or 0
                if d < grabD then grab, grabD = slot, d end
            else
                full = false
                stand = stand or slot
            end
        end
        return stand, grab, full, grabD
    end

    local tpGen = 0
    local function tpToCampSlot(plot)
        if not plot then clog(nil, "TP: no base to go to"); return false, "base not found" end
        if _G.Skyr0IsStealing and _G.Skyr0IsStealing() then clog(nil, "TP: refused, you are carrying a brainrot"); return false, "you are stealing" end
        local char = LP.Character
        local root = char and (char.PrimaryPart or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not (root and hum and hum.Health > 0) then return false, "no character" end
        if not _G.Skyr0DoVelocityTP then clog(nil, "TP: engine not loaded"); return false, "TP engine not loaded" end

        local stand = scanCamp(plot)
        local slot = stand or sortedSlots(plot)[1]
        local spawn = slot and slot.Base.Spawn
        if not spawn then return false, "no slots in that base" end
        local dest = spawn.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
