        clog(nil, ("TP: open slot %s at %.0f, %.0f, %.0f (%.0f studs away, %s)"):format(
            tostring(slot.Name), dest.X, dest.Y, dest.Z, (root.Position - dest).Magnitude,
            stand and "first empty slot" or "base FULL, using first slot"))

        tpGen = tpGen + 1
        local myGen = tpGen
        RC.arrived = false
        local entry = {
            name = "Base Camp", index = "Base Camp", mps = 0, mutation = "None",
            position = spawn.Position, plot = plot.Name, slot = tostring(slot.Name),
            campSlot = true,
        }
        task.spawn(function()

            if _G.Skyr0TPActive and _G.Skyr0TPActive() then
                clog(nil, "TP: cancelling the running flight for the new target")
                _G.Skyr0TPCancel = true
                local c0 = os.clock()
                while _G.Skyr0TPActive() and os.clock() - c0 < 2 do task.wait(0.05) end
                if _G.Skyr0TPActive() and _G.Skyr0ForceIdleTP then pcall(_G.Skyr0ForceIdleTP) end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
            if myGen ~= tpGen then return end
            local s = Config and Config.TpSettings or {}
            _G.TPVelocity   = math.max(tonumber(s.GrabbleTPSpeed) or 400, 400)
            _G.TPCloneDelay = tonumber(s.CloneDelayVal) or 0.1
            _G.LandingDelay = _G.LandingDelay or 0.4
            _G.Skyr0TPCancel = false

            clog(nil, "TP: grapple -> pathfinding flight to the slot")
            task.spawn(function() pcall(_G.Skyr0DoVelocityTP, entry) end)

            local t0 = os.clock()
            while myGen == tpGen and not _G.Skyr0TPActive() and os.clock() - t0 < 3 do task.wait(0.05) end
            local t1 = os.clock()
            while myGen == tpGen and _G.Skyr0TPActive() and os.clock() - t1 < 15 do task.wait(0.1) end
            if myGen ~= tpGen then return end
            RC.arrivedAt = os.clock()
            RC.arrived = true
            clog(nil, "TP: arrived -> grab armed")
        end)
        return true, "slot " .. (tostring(slot.Name):match("%d+") or slot.Name)
    end
    RC.tp = function()
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        local plot = RC.plotName and plots and plots:FindFirstChild(RC.plotName)
        if not plot and RC.target then
            local plr = Players:FindFirstChild(RC.target)
            plot = plr and findPlotFor(plr)
        end
        return tpToCampSlot(plot)
    end

    task.defer(function()
    if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
    local guiParent = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
    pcall(function() local o = guiParent:FindFirstChild("Skyr0_BaseCamp"); if o then o:Destroy() end end)

    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name = "Skyr0_BaseCamp"
    sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
    sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    sg.DisplayOrder = 997
    sg.Parent = guiParent

    local W, H = 250, 240
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -W - 20, 0.5, -H / 2)
    do

        local sp = Config and Config.BaseCampPos
        if type(sp) == "table" and tonumber(sp[1]) and tonumber(sp[2]) and tonumber(sp[3]) and tonumber(sp[4]) then
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(tonumber(sp[1]), tonumber(sp[2]), tonumber(sp[3]), tonumber(sp[4]))
        end
    end
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = C_BG
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Active = true
    frame.Parent = sg
    frame.ClipsDescendants = true
    do
        local _fc = Instance.new("UICorner", frame); _fc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
        local _fs = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
        _fs.Color = C_ACCENT; _fs.Thickness = 1.5; _fs.Transparency = 0; _fs.Enabled = true
        _fs.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    end

    local _fAccent = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
    _fAccent.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, 0)
    _fAccent.BackgroundColor3 = C_ACCENT
    _fAccent.BackgroundTransparency = 1; _fAccent.Visible = false
    _fAccent.BorderSizePixel = 0

    local bar = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
    bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
    bar.BackgroundColor3 = C_ROW
    bar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    bar.BorderSizePixel = 0

    Instance.new("UICorner", bar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

    local _hAccent = Instance.new("Frame", bar)
    _hAccent.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, 0)
    _hAccent.BackgroundColor3 = C_ACCENT
    _hAccent.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Instance.new("UICorner", _hAccent).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", bar)
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
    title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    title.TextSize = 12
    title.TextColor3 = C_ACCENT
    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    title.Text = "BASE CAMP"

    local minBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", bar)
    minBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 22)
    minBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0.5, -11)
    minBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C_BG
    minBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    minBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    minBtn.TextSize = 16
    minBtn.TextColor3 = C_TEXT
    minBtn.Text = "-"
    minBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    Instance.new("UICorner", minBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local _hDiv = Instance.new("Frame", bar)
    _hDiv.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
    _hDiv.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1)
    _hDiv.BackgroundColor3 = C_ACCENT
    _hDiv.BorderSizePixel = 0

    local body = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
    body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -36)
    body.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 36)
    body.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    local status = Instance.new("TextLabel", body)
    status.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 28)
    status.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 4)
    status.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    status.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    status.TextSize = 10
    status.TextColor3 = C_DIM
    status.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    status.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    status.TextWrapped = true
    status.RichText = true

    local _sDivider = Instance.new("Frame", body)
    _sDivider.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
    _sDivider.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 34)
    _sDivider.BackgroundColor3 = C_ROW
    _sDivider.BackgroundTransparency = 1; _sDivider.Visible = false
    _sDivider.BorderSizePixel = 0

    local listLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", body)
    listLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 18)
    listLbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 36)
    listLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    listLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    listLbl.TextSize = 10
    listLbl.TextColor3 = C_DIM
    listLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    listLbl.Text = "PLAYERS"

    local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", body)
    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -58)
    list.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 56)
    list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    list.BorderSizePixel = 0
    list.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    list.ScrollBarImageColor3 = C_ACCENT
    list.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", list)
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)

    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    do
        local dragging, dStart, fStart
        bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging, dStart, fStart = true, i.Position, frame.Position
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local d = i.Position - dStart
                frame.Position = UDim2.new(fStart.X.Scale, fStart.X.Offset + d.X, fStart.Y.Scale, fStart.Y.Offset + d.Y)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                if dragging and _G.__Skyr0CampSavePos then _G.__Skyr0CampSavePos() end
                dragging = false
            end
        end)
    end

    local minimized = false
    local function savePos()
        if not Config then return end
        local p = frame.Position
        Config.BaseCampPos = { p.X.Scale, math.floor(p.X.Offset + 0.5), p.Y.Scale, math.floor(p.Y.Offset + 0.5) }
        Config.BaseCampMin = minimized == true
        if saveConfig then pcall(saveConfig) end
    end
    _G.__Skyr0CampSavePos = savePos

    local function applyMin()
        body.Visible = not minimized
        frame.Size = minimized and UDim2.fromOffset(W, 36) or UDim2.fromOffset(W, H)
        minBtn.Text = minimized and "+" or "-"
    end
    _G.Skyr0OnTap(minBtn, function()
        minimized = not minimized
        applyMin()
        savePos()
    end)
    if Config and Config.BaseCampMin == true then minimized = true; applyMin() end

    task.defer(function()
        task.wait()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize
        if not vp or vp.X < 50 then return end
        local ap, as = frame.AbsolutePosition, frame.AbsoluteSize
        local nx = math.clamp(ap.X, 0, math.max(0, vp.X - as.X))
        local ny = math.clamp(ap.Y, 0, math.max(0, vp.Y - 28))
        if nx ~= ap.X or ny ~= ap.Y then
            frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(nx, ny)
        end
    end)

    local function makeMarker(color)
        local box = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
        box.LineThickness = 0.08
        box.Color3 = color
        box.SurfaceColor3 = color
        box.SurfaceTransparency = 0.75
        box.Visible = false
        box.Parent = sg
        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170, 34)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.Enabled = false
        bb.Parent = sg
        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
        t.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        t.TextSize = 13
        t.TextColor3 = color
        t.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
        t.TextWrapped = true
        return { box = box, bb = bb, label = t }
    end
    local standMk = makeMarker(C_STAND)
    local grabMk  = makeMarker(C_GRAB)

    local function showMarker(mk, part, text)
        if part then
            mk.box.Adornee = part; mk.box.Visible = true
            mk.bb.Adornee = part;  mk.bb.Enabled = true
            mk.label.Text = text
        else
            mk.box.Visible = false; mk.box.Adornee = nil
            mk.bb.Enabled = false;  mk.bb.Adornee = nil
        end
    end

    local selected = nil
    local rows = {}

    local function refreshToggle() end

    local function rowLabel(plr, on)
        return plr and ("  " .. plr.DisplayName .. "  <font size='8' color='" .. (on and "#2a2d36" or "#9aa0ad") .. "'>@" .. plr.Name .. "</font>") or ""
    end

    local function refreshRows()
        local names = {}
        for name in pairs(rows) do names[#names + 1] = name end
        table.sort(names, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
        for i, name in ipairs(names) do
            local wrap = rows[name]
            if not (wrap and wrap.Parent) then continue end
            local b = wrap:FindFirstChild(name:lower())
            if not b then continue end
            local on = (name == selected)
            local what = stealInfo[name]
            local plr = Players:FindFirstChild(name)
            local rowH = what and 48 or 24

            wrap.LayoutOrder = what and (i - 10000) or i
            wrap.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, rowH)

            b.BackgroundColor3 = on and C_ACCENT or (what and C_STEALBG or C_ROW)
            b.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12) or C_TEXT
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
            b.TextWrapped = what ~= nil
            if what then
                local _sc = string.format("#%02x%02x%02x", math.floor(C_STEAL.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(C_STEAL.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(C_STEAL.B * 255 + 0.5))
                local _w = tostring(what):gsub("&", "&amp;"):gsub("<", "&lt;"):gsub(">", "&gt;")
                b.Text = rowLabel(plr, on) .. "\n  <font size='13'><b><font color='" .. (on and "#7a0010" or _sc)
                    .. "'>STEALING</font> <font color='" .. (on and "#000000" or "#ffffff") .. "'>" .. _w .. "</font></b></font>"
            else
                b.Text = rowLabel(plr, on)
            end
            local st = b:FindFirstChild("StealStroke")
            if st then st.Enabled = false end
        end
    end

    local heads = {}
    local function headerFor(plr)
        local bb = heads[plr.Name]
        if bb and bb.Parent then return bb end
        bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name = "Skyr0StealHeader"
        bb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(280, 30)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.4, 0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.Enabled = false
        bb.Parent = sg
        local pill = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
        pill.Name = "Pill"
        pill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        pill.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        pill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        pill.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
        pill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 8, 10)
        pill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
        pill.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        pill.TextSize = 14
        pill.TextScaled = true
        do local _tc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", pill); _tc.MaxTextSize = 15; _tc.MinTextSize = 9 end
        pill.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        pill.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
        pill.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        Instance.new("UICorner", pill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", pill)
        s.Color = C_STEAL
        s.Thickness = 0; s.Enabled = false
        s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        heads[plr.Name] = bb
        return bb
    end
    local function dropHeader(name)
        local bb = heads[name]
        if bb then pcall(function() bb:Destroy() end); heads[name] = nil end
    end

    RC.start = function(name)
        local plr = name and Players:FindFirstChild(name)
        if not plr or plr == LP then RC.status = "player not found"; return false, RC.status end
        RC.target, RC.active = plr.Name, true
        RC.arrived = false
        local plot = findPlotFor(plr)
        RC.plotName = plot and plot.Name or nil
        RC.status = plot and ("camping " .. plr.Name) or "looking for base..."
        clog(nil, ("START camp on %s -> base %s"):format(plr.Name, tostring(RC.plotName or "not found yet")))
        refreshToggle()

        local want = plr.Name
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while RC.active and RC.target == want and not RC.plotName and os.clock() - t0 < 5 do
                task.wait(0.2)
                local p = findPlotFor(plr)
                if p then RC.plotName = p.Name end
            end
            if not (RC.active and RC.target == want) then return end
            local ok, why = RC.tp()

            if not ok then RC.arrivedAt = os.clock(); RC.arrived = true end
            notify(ok and ("TP to " .. want .. "'s " .. tostring(why)) or ("TP failed: " .. tostring(why)))
        end)
        return true, RC.status
    end

    RC.stop = function()
        if RC.active then clog(nil, "STOP camp on " .. tostring(RC.target)) end
        RC.active, RC.plotName, RC.arrived = false, nil, false
        RC.standSlot, RC.grabSlot, RC.grabEntry = nil, nil, nil
        RC.status = "stopped"
        showMarker(standMk, nil); showMarker(grabMk, nil)
        refreshToggle()
    end

    local campBtns = campBtns or {}
    local function refreshCampBtns()
        for name, cb in pairs(campBtns) do
            if cb and cb.Parent then
                local isThisCamp = RC.active and RC.target == name
                cb.BackgroundColor3 = isThisCamp and C_ON or C_ROW
                cb.TextColor3 = isThisCamp and Color3.fromRGB(10,10,12) or C_ACCENT
                cb.Text = isThisCamp and "CAMP\nON" or "CAMP"
            end
        end
    end

    local function addRow(plr)
        if plr == LP or rows[plr.Name] then return end

        local wrap = Instance.new("Frame")
        wrap.Name = plr.Name:lower() .. "_wrap"
        wrap.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 24)
        wrap.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        wrap.BorderSizePixel = 0
        wrap.Parent = list

        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Name = plr.Name:lower()
        b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
        b.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        b.TextSize = 11
        b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.RichText = true
        b.Text = rowLabel(plr)
        b.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        b.Parent = wrap
        Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", b)
        st.Name = "StealStroke"
        st.Color = C_STEAL
        st.Thickness = 0
        st.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        st.Enabled = false

        local cb = Instance.new("TextButton")
        cb.Name = plr.Name:lower() .. "_camp"
        cb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 1, 0)
        cb.Position = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 0)
        cb.BorderSizePixel = 0
        cb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        cb.TextSize = 8
        cb.AutoButtonColor = false
        cb.RichText = false
        cb.Text = "CAMP"
        cb.BackgroundColor3 = C_ROW
        cb.TextColor3 = C_ACCENT
        cb.Parent = wrap
        Instance.new("UICorner", cb).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        campBtns[plr.Name] = cb
        _G.Skyr0OnTap(cb, function()
            if RC.active and RC.target == plr.Name then
                RC.stop(); notify("camp off")
            else
                selected = plr.Name
                RC.start(plr.Name)
                notify("camping " .. plr.Name)
            end
            refreshRows(); refreshCampBtns()
        end)

        rows[plr.Name] = wrap
        _G.Skyr0OnTap(b, function()
            selected = plr.Name
            refreshRows()

            if RC.active and RC.target ~= plr.Name then RC.start(plr.Name) end
        end)
        refreshRows(); refreshCampBtns()
    end

    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do addRow(p) end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(addRow)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
        local wrap = rows[p.Name]
        if wrap then pcall(function() wrap:Destroy() end); rows[p.Name] = nil end
        local cb = campBtns and campBtns[p.Name]
        if cb then pcall(function() cb:Destroy() end); campBtns[p.Name] = nil end
        stealInfo[p.Name] = nil
        dropHeader(p.Name)
        if selected == p.Name then selected = nil end
        if RC.active and RC.target == p.Name then
            notify(p.Name .. " left the game - camp off")
            RC.stop()
        end
    end)
    refreshToggle()

    local function updateSteal(plr)
        if plr == LP or not rows[plr.Name] then return false end
        local what = stealingWhat(plr)
        local changed = stealInfo[plr.Name] ~= what
        stealInfo[plr.Name] = what
        local ch = plr.Character
        local adornee = ch and (ch:FindFirstChild("Head") or ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
        if what and adornee then
            local bb = headerFor(plr)
            bb.Adornee = adornee
            bb.Pill.Text = "STEALING: " .. what
            bb.Enabled = true
        elseif heads[plr.Name] then
            heads[plr.Name].Enabled = false
        end
        return changed
    end
    local function hookSteal(plr)
        if plr == LP then return end
        local function bump() if updateSteal(plr) then refreshRows() end end
        plr:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(bump)
        plr:GetAttributeChangedSignal("StealingIndex"):Connect(bump)
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do hookSteal(p) end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hookSteal)
    LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        if RC.active then
            clog(nil, LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true and "YOU are stealing now (grab worked)"
                or "you stopped carrying (delivered or dropped)")
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while sg.Parent do
            local changed = false
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                local ok, c = pcall(updateSteal, p)
                if ok and c then changed = true end
            end
            if changed then refreshRows() end
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end)

    local function slotNum(slot) return slot and (tostring(slot.Name):match("%d+") or slot.Name) or "-" end

    task.spawn(function()
        while sg.Parent do
            task.wait(0.15)

            local thieves = {}
            for n, what in pairs(stealInfo) do
                if what then thieves[#thieves + 1] = n .. ": " .. what end
            end
            table.sort(thieves)
            local stealLine = (#thieves > 0)
                and ("\n<font color='#c8c8c8'><b>STEALING</b> " .. table.concat(thieves, ", ") .. "</font>")
                or ""

            if not RC.active then
                status.Text = (selected and ("Selected: <b>" .. selected .. "</b>\nPress CAMP to start camping their base.")
                    or "Pick a player below to start camping.") .. stealLine
                continue
            end
            local plr = Players:FindFirstChild(RC.target or "")
            if not plr then RC.stop(); continue end

            local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            local plot = RC.plotName and plots and plots:FindFirstChild(RC.plotName)
            if not plot then
                plot = findPlotFor(plr)
                RC.plotName = plot and plot.Name or nil
            end
            if not plot then
                clog("nobase", "looking for " .. tostring(RC.target) .. "'s base (PlotSign not found yet)", 3)
                RC.status = "looking for base..."
                showMarker(standMk, nil); showMarker(grabMk, nil)
                status.Text = "Target: <b>" .. RC.target .. "</b>\nlooking for their base..." .. stealLine
                continue
            end

            local stand, grab, full, grabD = scanCamp(plot)
            RC.standSlot, RC.grabSlot = stand and stand.Name or nil, grab and grab.Name or nil
            do
                local k = plot.Name .. "|" .. tostring(RC.standSlot) .. "|" .. tostring(RC.grabSlot)
                if k ~= RC._dbgKey then
                    RC._dbgKey = k
                    clog(nil, ("base %s: open slot %s, nearest brainrot slot %s%s%s"):format(plot.Name,
                        tostring(RC.standSlot), tostring(RC.grabSlot),
                        (grabD and grabD < math.huge) and (" (" .. math.floor(grabD) .. " studs)") or "",
                        full and ", base FULL" or ""))
                end
            end

            local pet = grab and petInSlot(plot.Name, grab.Name)
            RC.grabEntry = grab and {
                name = (pet and pet.name) or "Brainrot",
                mpsValue = pet and pet.genValue or 0,
                plot = plot.Name, slot = grab.Name,
            } or nil

            showMarker(standMk, stand and stand.Base.Spawn,
                "STAND  |  Slot " .. slotNum(stand))
            showMarker(grabMk, grab and grab.Base.Spawn,
                "GRAB  |  Slot " .. slotNum(grab) .. "\n" .. ((pet and pet.name) or "Brainrot")
                .. ((grabD < math.huge) and ("  " .. math.floor(grabD) .. "m") or ""))

            RC.status = full and (RC.target .. "'s base is FULL") or ("camping " .. RC.target)
            status.Text = "Target: <b>" .. RC.target .. "</b>"
                .. (full and "  <font color='#c8c8c8'>(FULL)</font>" or "")
                .. "\nStand: <font color='#b4b4b4'>Slot " .. slotNum(stand) .. "</font>"
                .. "   Grab: <font color='#dcdcdc'>Slot " .. slotNum(grab) .. "</font>"
                .. (grab and ("\n" .. ((pet and pet.name) or "Brainrot")) or "\nbase is empty")
                .. stealLine
        end
    end)
    end)

    do
        local Workspace = workspace
        local LocalPlayer = LP
        local Config = { StealRadius = nil, StealDuration = nil }
        if _G.JAF_M2 == nil then
            _G.JAF_M2 = function(ax, az, bx, bz)
                local dx, dz = ax - bx, az - bz
                return math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)
            end
        end
        local function getChar()
            local c = LocalPlayer.Character
            return c, c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end

        local isAutoStealing = false
        local StealData      = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
        local PromptCache    = {}
        local _stealStart    = 0
        local _holdEpoch     = 0
        local function _ragUntil()
            return math.max(tonumber(LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")) or 0, tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollUntil) or 0)
        end
        local function _ragBlockedNow()
            return (_ragUntil() - workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollLatePad) or 0.1)) > 0
                or (tick() - (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollPhysLastT) or 0)) < (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollPhysWindow) or 0.4)
        end
        local function _podOf(uid)
            if not uid then return nil end
            local pn, sl = tostring(uid):match("^(.*)_([^_]+)$")
            if not (pn and sl) then return nil end
            local pl = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            pl = pl and pl:FindFirstChild(pn)
            pl = pl and pl:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            return pl and pl:FindFirstChild(sl)
        end
        local function _grabCB(p)
            local d = { hold = {}, trigger = {} }
            if type(getconnections) ~= "function" then return d end
            local ok, cs = pcall(getconnections, p.PromptButtonHoldBegan)
            if ok and type(cs) == "table" then
                for _, c in ipairs(cs) do if type(c.Function) == "function" then d.hold[#d.hold + 1] = c.Function end end
            end
            ok, cs = pcall(getconnections, p.Triggered)
            if ok and type(cs) == "table" then
                for _, c in ipairs(cs) do if type(c.Function) == "function" then d.trigger[#d.trigger + 1] = c.Function end end
            end
            return d
        end
        local function _fireTrigger(p, d)
            if fireproximityprompt and _G.JAF_StealUseFPP ~= false then pcall(fireproximityprompt, p); return end
            if d then for _, f in ipairs(d.trigger) do task.spawn(f) end
                if #d.trigger == 0 and fireproximityprompt then pcall(fireproximityprompt, p) end end
        end
        local _apCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
        local function _apFromHold(p, d)
            local ap = _apCache[p]
            if ap ~= nil then return ap or nil end
            if debug and debug.getupvalues and d then
                for _, f in ipairs(d.hold) do
                    local ok, ups = pcall(debug.getupvalues, f)
                    if ok then for _, v in pairs(ups) do
                        if type(v) == "table" and rawget(v, "State") ~= nil and rawget(v, "ProximityPrompt") ~= nil then
                            _apCache[p] = v
                            return v
                        end
                    end end
                end
            end
            _apCache[p] = false
            return nil
        end
        local function _fireHoldForced(p, d)
            if not d or #d.hold == 0 then return false end
            local ap = _apFromHold(p, d)
            if ap and ap.State ~= "Steal" then
                local _os = ap.State
                ap.State = "Steal"
                for _, f in ipairs(d.hold) do pcall(f) end
                ap.State = _os
                return true
            end
            if not ap and p and p.Enabled == false then return false end
            for _, f in ipairs(d.hold) do task.spawn(f) end
            return true
        end
        local function isMyPlot(plotName)
            local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            local plot  = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
            local sign  = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            local yb    = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
            return (yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") and yb.Enabled == true) or false
        end
        local function findPrompt(a)
            if not a then return nil end
            local cp = PromptCache[a.uid]
            if cp and cp.Parent and cp.Enabled and cp.ActionText == "Steal" then return cp end
            local pod = _podOf(a.uid)
            local base = pod and pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
            local sp = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
            if not sp then return nil end
            local att = sp:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
            if att then
                for _, p in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do
                    if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and p.Enabled and p.ActionText == "Steal" then PromptCache[a.uid] = p; return p end
                end end
            return nil
        end
        local _ownerPresent = {}
        for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do _ownerPresent[pl.Name] = true end
        Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(pl) _ownerPresent[pl.Name] = true end)
        Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(pl) _ownerPresent[pl.Name] = nil end)
        local function _stealable(a, me)
            if not a or a.owner == me then return false end
            if _G.JAF_SkipLeftOwners == false then return true end
            return a.owner ~= nil and _ownerPresent[a.owner] == true
        end
        local function findNearestPrompt(priorityTarget)
            local _, hrp = getChar()
            if not hrp then return end
            local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            if not plots then return end
            if not priorityTarget then return end
            local _hrpPos = hrp.Position
            local _radius = Config.StealRadius or 95
            local plot = plots:FindFirstChild(priorityTarget.plot)
            if not plot or isMyPlot(plot.Name) then return nil end
            local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            local pod  = pods and pods:FindFirstChild(priorityTarget.slot)
            local base = pod and pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
            local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
            if not spawn then return nil end
            local sp = spawn.Position
            if _G.JAF_M2(sp.X, sp.Z, _hrpPos.X, _hrpPos.Z) > _radius then return nil end
            local att = spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
            if not att then return nil end
            local nearest
            for _, p in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and p.Enabled and p.ActionText:find("Steal") then nearest = p end end
            return nearest
        end
        local function promptDistXZ(prompt)
            local c = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then return nil end
            local par = prompt and prompt.Parent
            local pos
            if par then
                if par:IsA("Attachment") then pos = par.WorldPosition
                elseif par:IsA("BasePart") then pos = par.Position
                else
                    local bp = par:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")
                    if bp then pos = bp.Position end
                end end
            if not pos then return nil end
            local hp = hrp.Position
            return _G.JAF_M2(pos.X, pos.Z, hp.X, hp.Z), (pos - hp).Magnitude
        end
        local function _pivotPos(m) return m:GetPivot().Position end
        local function _cache() return (SharedState and SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) or {} end

        local function _animalSlotEntry(a, memo)
            local al = memo and memo[a.plot]
            if al == nil then
                local ok, list = pcall(function()
                    return _G.Skyr0_GetPlotAnimalList and _G.Skyr0_GetPlotAnimalList(tostring(a.plot))
                end)
                al = (ok and type(list) == "table") and list or false
                if memo then memo[a.plot] = al end
            end
            if al == false then return nil, "noChan" end
            local e = al[a.slot]
            if e == nil then local n = tonumber(a.slot); if n ~= nil then e = al[n] end end
            return e
        end

        local _stealSpecies, _stealSpeciesAt = {}, 0
        local function _stealSpeciesSet()
            local ttl = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSetTTL) or 0.25
            if (tick() - _stealSpeciesAt) < ttl then return _stealSpecies end
            _stealSpeciesAt = tick()
            for k in pairs(_stealSpecies) do _stealSpecies[k] = nil end
            for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if pl ~= LocalPlayer then
                    local idx = pl:GetAttribute("StealingIndex")
                    if idx ~= nil and idx ~= "" then
                        local s2 = tostring(idx):lower()
                        _stealSpecies[s2] = true
                        _stealSpecies[(s2:gsub("%s+", ""))] = true
                        local dn = displayNameOf and displayNameOf(tostring(idx))
                        if dn then local d = tostring(dn):lower() _stealSpecies[d] = true _stealSpecies[(d:gsub("%s+", ""))] = true end
                    end end end
            return _stealSpecies
        end

        local function _isBeingStolen(a, memo)
            if _G.JAF_StolenDetect == false then return false end
            if type(a) ~= "table" or a.isWalking then return false end
            if type(a.plot) ~= "string" or a.slot == nil then return false end
            local e, why = _animalSlotEntry(a, memo)
            if why then return false end
            if type(e) == "table" then
                local st = e.Steal
                if type(st) ~= "number" then return false end
                if st == LocalPlayer.UserId then return false end
                for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if pl.UserId == st then return pl:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true end end
                return false
            end
            local nm = a.nameLower or (a.name and tostring(a.name):lower())
            if not nm then return false end
            local set = _stealSpeciesSet()
            if set[nm] or set[(nm:gsub("%s+", ""))] then return true end
            return false
        end

        local _barGen = 0
        local function _bar(active, start, dur, tgt)
            _G.Skyr0_StealStatus = _G.Skyr0_StealStatus or {}
            local st = _G.Skyr0_StealStatus
            if start then st.start = start end
            if dur then st.duration = dur end
            if tgt ~= nil then st.target = tgt or nil end
            if active == true then
                _barGen = _barGen + 1
                st.active = true
            elseif active == false then
                _barGen = _barGen + 1
                local g = _barGen
                task.delay(tonumber(_G.JAF_StealReadyDebounce) or 0.12, function()
                    if g == _barGen and not isAutoStealing then st.active = false end
                end)
            end
        end

        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if isAutoStealing and RC.active then
                local st = _G.Skyr0_StealStatus
                if st and st.start ~= _stealStart then st.start = _stealStart end
            end
        end)

        local function executeSteal(prompt, heldUid, preHold)
            if isAutoStealing then return end
            local function _tpBanking() return false end
            local _d = promptDistXZ(prompt)
            if _d and _d > (Config.StealRadius or 95) and not _tpBanking() then return end
            local data = StealData[prompt]
            if not data then data = _grabCB(prompt); data.ready = true if #data.hold > 0 and (#data.trigger > 0 or _G.JAF_StealUseFPP ~= false) then StealData[prompt] = data end end
            if not data.ready then return end
            data.ready = false
            isAutoStealing = true
            _stealStart = tick()
            task.spawn(function()
                local oldDuration = prompt.HoldDuration
                prompt.HoldDuration = 0
                local _beginT, _inherited = 0, false
                local _hAge = _holdEpoch > 0 and (tick() - _holdEpoch) or math.huge
                if _G.JAF_StealHoldCarry ~= false and prompt.Enabled and _hAge <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCarryMax) or 1.25) then
                    _beginT, _inherited = _holdEpoch, true
                else
                    for _, f in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(f) end
                    _beginT = prompt.Enabled and tick() or 0
                    if _beginT > 0 and #data.hold > 0 then _holdEpoch = _beginT end
                end
                local _beganOk, _recapN = (_inherited or #data.hold > 0), 0
                local _dur = Config.StealDuration
                if not _dur then
                    local _srv = (_G.JAF_StealUsePromptHold == true) and tonumber(oldDuration) or nil
                    _dur = ((_srv and _srv > 0.1 and _srv) or 1.3) + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealHoldPad) or 0)
                end
                local _maxHold = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealMaxHold) or (_dur + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealExtendWindow) or 1.3))
                local _t0 = _inherited and _beginT or tick(); local _holdAbsStart = tick()
                if _inherited then _stealStart = _beginT end
                local _ragRet0 = _ragUntil()
                local _postGrace = (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPostGrace) or 0) + ((_ragRet0 > 0) and (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollPostGrace) or 0) or 0)
                local _abort, _grabbed, _recycle, _didEnter = false, false, false, false
                local _clearFireUntil, _lastCommitT, _commitN = 0, 0, 0
                local _eligSince, _commitGraceUntil = 0, 0
                local _wasDis, _disAt = false, nil
                local _bankT, _campChkT = 0, 0
                local _carryChkT, _carryFlag, _carryLastT = 0, false, 0
                local _promptGone, _goneAt, _goneConn = false, nil, nil
                local activePrompt, activeData = prompt, data
                local _podiumForDist = _podOf(heldUid)
                local function _petDistNow()
                    local _, _h = getChar()
                    if not _h then return nil end
                    if _podiumForDist and _podiumForDist.Parent then
                        local ok, piv = pcall(_pivotPos, _podiumForDist)
                        if ok and piv then return (piv - _h.Position).Magnitude end
                    end
                    local _, d3 = promptDistXZ(activePrompt); return d3
                end
                _bar(true, _stealStart, _dur)
                if _G.JAF_StealReacquire ~= false and heldUid and _podiumForDist then
                    _goneConn = _podiumForDist.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
                        if _promptGone and desc:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and tostring(desc.ActionText or ""):find("Steal") then
                            activePrompt, activeData = desc, _grabCB(desc)
                            pcall(function() desc.HoldDuration = 0 end)
                            for _, f in ipairs(activeData.hold) do task.spawn(f) end
                            _beginT = desc.Enabled and tick() or 0
                            if _beginT > 0 and #activeData.hold > 0 then _holdEpoch = _beginT end
                            _beganOk = #activeData.hold > 0
                            _wasDis, _disAt, _bankT = false, nil, 0
                            _promptGone, _goneAt = false, nil
                            _stealStart = tick()
                            _t0, _commitN, _lastCommitT, _recycle = tick(), 0, 0, false
                        end end) end
                repeat
                    local _mt = RC._mt
                    if _mt ~= nil and heldUid ~= nil and _mt.uid ~= heldUid then _abort = true; break end
                    if not RC.active then _abort = true; break end
                    if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then _grabbed = true; break end
                    if _goneConn and not activePrompt.Parent and not _promptGone then _promptGone = true; _goneAt = tick() end
                    if _promptGone then
                        if _bankT == 0 or (tick() - _bankT) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealBankRefresh) or 2.5) then
                            if _fireHoldForced(activePrompt, activeData) then _bankT = tick() _holdEpoch = _bankT end
                        end
                        if (tick() - _goneAt) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPromptGoneWait) or 6.5) then break end
                        _t0 = _t0 + (_G.JAF_StealFireInterval or 0.12)
                        task.wait(_G.JAF_StealFireInterval or 0.12)
                        continue
                    end
                    if activePrompt.Parent and not activePrompt.Enabled then
                        if not _disAt then _disAt = tick() end
                        if (tick() - _carryChkT) > 0.25 then
                            _carryChkT = tick()
                            if #activeData.hold == 0 then activeData = _grabCB(activePrompt) end
                            local _cnow = false
                            for _, _sa in ipairs(_cache()) do if _sa and _sa.uid == heldUid then
                                local _cok, _cv = pcall(_isBeingStolen, _sa)
                                _cnow = _cok and _cv == true
                                break
                            end end
                            if _cnow then _carryLastT = tick() end
                            _carryFlag = _cnow or (tick() - _carryLastT) < 1.0
                        end
                        if _bankT > 0 and (tick() - _bankT) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealBankRefresh) or 2.5) then _bankT = 0 end
                        if _bankT == 0 and _G.JAF_StealPreBank ~= false then
                            if _fireHoldForced(activePrompt, activeData) then _bankT = tick() _holdEpoch = _bankT end
                        end
                        if (tick() - _campChkT) > 0.25 then
                            _campChkT = tick()
                            if _bankT == 0 and _G.JAF_StealPreBank ~= false then
                                for _, _sa in ipairs(_cache()) do
                                    if _sa and _sa.uid ~= heldUid and _stealable(_sa, LocalPlayer.Name) then
                                        local _bp = findPrompt(_sa)
                                        if _bp and _bp.Enabled and _bp:GetAttribute("State") == "Steal" then
                                            local _bd = _grabCB(_bp)
                                            if #_bd.hold > 0 then for _, f in ipairs(_bd.hold) do task.spawn(f) end _bankT = tick() _holdEpoch = _bankT break end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if (not _carryFlag) and (tick() - math.max(_disAt, _carryLastT)) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealDisabledGrace) or 3) then break end
                        _wasDis = true
                        local _dt = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPoll) or 0.03
                        _stealStart = _bankT > 0 and _bankT or tick()
                        _t0, _commitN, _lastCommitT, _recycle = tick(), 0, 0, false
                        _bar(true, _stealStart, _dur)
                        _holdAbsStart = _holdAbsStart + (task.wait(_dt) or _dt)
                        continue
                    end
                    if _wasDis then
                        _wasDis, _disAt = false, nil
                        activeData = _grabCB(activePrompt)
                        local _bAge = _bankT > 0 and (tick() - _bankT) or math.huge
                        if _bAge <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealBankUseMax) or 2.3) then
                            _beginT = _bankT
                            _t0 = _bankT
                            _stealStart = _bankT
                            _beganOk = true
                        else
                            for _, f in ipairs(activeData.hold) do task.spawn(f) end
                            _beginT = tick()
                            _beganOk = #activeData.hold > 0
                            if _beganOk then _holdEpoch = _beginT end
                            _t0 = tick()
                            _stealStart = tick()
                        end
                        _bankT = 0
                        _commitN, _lastCommitT, _recycle = 0, 0, false
                        _eligSince = tick() - (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealEntryDelay) or 0.3)
                        _clearFireUntil = math.max(_clearFireUntil, tick() + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealDropSettle) or 0.5))
                        _bar(true, _stealStart, _dur)
                    end
                    if not _beganOk then _stealStart = tick() end
                    local _needTrig = _G.JAF_StealUseFPP == false
                    if (#activeData.hold == 0 or (_needTrig and #activeData.trigger == 0)) and _recapN < (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealRecapMax) or 40) then
                        _recapN = _recapN + 1
                        local _rd = _grabCB(activePrompt)
                        if #_rd.hold > 0 and (not _needTrig or #_rd.trigger > 0) then
                            activeData = _rd
                            if not _beganOk then
                                for _, f in ipairs(_rd.hold) do task.spawn(f) end
                                if activePrompt.Enabled then _beginT = tick() _holdEpoch = _beginT end
                                _beganOk = true
                                _stealStart = tick()
                                _t0, _commitN, _lastCommitT, _recycle = tick(), 0, 0, false
                                _bar(true, _stealStart, _dur)
                            end end end
                    local _hrDist = promptDistXZ(activePrompt)
                    if _hrDist and _hrDist > (Config.StealRadius or 95) then _abort = true; break end
                    local _ragSync = _G.JAF_RagdollStealSync ~= false
                    local _now = tick()
                    if _ragSync then
                        local _nr = _ragUntil(); local _remNow = _nr - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                        if _nr > _ragRet0 + 0.3 and _remNow > 0.1 then
                            _ragRet0 = _nr
                            _t0 = math.max(_t0, _now + _remNow - _dur + (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollLatePad) or 0.1))
                            _stealStart = math.max(_stealStart, _t0)
                            _postGrace = (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPostGrace) or 0) + (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollPostGrace) or 0)
                        end end
                    local _holdDone = (_now - _t0) >= (_dur + _postGrace)
                    local _ragBlocked = _ragSync and _ragBlockedNow()
                    if _ragSync and _G.JAF_RagdollHoldUntilClear ~= false and _holdDone and _ragBlocked then
                        _clearFireUntil = _now + (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollClearWindow) or 0.35)
                    end
                    if _commitN == 0 and not _ragBlocked and (_beginT == 0 or (_now - _beginT) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHoldMax) or 2.5)) and activePrompt.Parent and activePrompt.Enabled then
                        local _rhOk = _fireHoldForced(activePrompt, activeData)
                        _beginT = tick()
                        _holdEpoch = _rhOk and _beginT or 0
                        _beganOk = #activeData.hold > 0
                        _stealStart = tick()
                        _t0 = math.max(_t0, tick())
                        _bar(true, _stealStart, _dur)
                    end
                    local _petDist = _petDistNow()
                    local _petInRange = _petDist and _petDist <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitRange) or 10)
                    if _petInRange then if _eligSince == 0 then _eligSince = _now end else _eligSince = 0 end
                    local _settleAnchor = _eligSince
                    local _effDelay = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealEntryDelay) or 0.3
                    local _settled = (_eligSince > 0) and ((_now - _settleAnchor) >= _effDelay)
                    local _inCommit = _holdDone and not _grabbed and not _ragBlocked and _petInRange and _settled
                                      and _beginT > 0 and (_now - _beginT) >= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHold) or _dur)
                                      and (_now - _beginT) <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHoldMax) or 2.5)
                    if _inCommit and _commitN == 0 then
                        _lastCommitT, _commitN = _now, 1
                        _fireTrigger(activePrompt, activeData)
                        clog(nil, ("rose steal: commit on slot %s (%.1f studs, %.2fs hold)")
                            :format(tostring(heldUid and heldUid:match("_([^_]+)$")), _petDist or -1, _now - _beginT))
                    end
                    if _inCommit and not _didEnter then
                        _didEnter = true
                        if _G.JAF_StealCommitSettle == true then
                            local _, _shrp = getChar()
                            local _v = _shrp and _shrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                            local _minSpd = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitSettleSpeed) or 30
                            if _v and (_v.X * _v.X + _v.Z * _v.Z) > (_minSpd * _minSpd) then
                                pcall(function() _shrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, _v.Y, 0) end)
                            end end end
                    if _commitN > 0 and not _grabbed and (_now - _lastCommitT) >= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitWait) or 0.4) then _recycle = true end
                    _commitGraceUntil = 0
                    if _G.JAF_StealHoldForSettle ~= false and not _grabbed and (_eligSince > 0 or _ragBlocked) then
                        local _fm = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitFireMargin) or 0.06
                        local _due = 0
                        if _commitN == 0 then
                            if _eligSince > 0 and not _settled then _due = _settleAnchor + _effDelay + _fm end
                            if _beginT > 0 then
                                local _srvDue = _beginT + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHold) or _dur) + _fm
                                if _srvDue > _due then _due = _srvDue end
                            end
                            local _hdDue = _t0 + _dur + _postGrace + _fm
                            if _hdDue > _due then _due = _hdDue end
                        else _due = _lastCommitT + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitWait) or 0.4) end
                        if _due > 0 then _commitGraceUntil = _due end
                    end
                    task.wait(tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPoll) or 0.03)
                until (not _promptGone and not _disAt and ((tick() - _t0) >= _maxHold or _recycle) and tick() >= _clearFireUntil and tick() >= _commitGraceUntil)
                      or (not _goneConn and not activePrompt.Parent)
                      or (tick() - _holdAbsStart > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealHoldMaxTotal) or 20))
                if _commitN > 0 or not _abort then _holdEpoch = 0 end
                if _goneConn then pcall(function() _goneConn:Disconnect() end) end
                if activePrompt.Parent and not _abort and not _grabbed and _commitN == 0 then
                    local _fd = _petDistNow()
                    if _fd and _fd <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitRange) or 10) and activePrompt.Enabled and _beginT > 0 and (tick() - _beginT) >= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHold) or _dur) and (tick() - _beginT) <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHoldMax) or 2.5) then _fireTrigger(activePrompt, activeData) end
                end
                if activePrompt.Parent then activePrompt.HoldDuration = oldDuration end
                local _why = _grabbed and "grab" or (_abort and "abort") or (_disAt and "carry") or (_promptGone and "gone") or (_commitN > 0 and "fired") or "expire"
                clog(nil, ("rose steal: hold ended (%s)"):format(_why))
                _bar(false)
                data.ready = true
                isAutoStealing = false
            end)
        end

        local function campTarget()
            local cache = _cache()
            local me = LocalPlayer.Name
            local _, hrp = getChar()
            local myPos = hrp and hrp.Position
            local best, bestD, firstAny = nil, math.huge, nil
            for _, a in ipairs(cache) do
                if a and a.plot == RC.plotName and not a.isWalking and a.owner ~= me then
                    firstAny = firstAny or a
                    if myPos and type(findAdorneeGlobal) == "function" then
                        local ok, part = pcall(findAdorneeGlobal, a)
                        local pos = ok and part and part.Position
                        if pos then
                            local d = (pos - myPos).Magnitude
                            if d < bestD then bestD = d; best = a end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return best or firstAny
        end

        local _campMoveAt = 0
        local function campHold()
            if not RC.standSlot or not RC.plotName then return end
            if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") or isAutoStealing then return end
            if _G.Skyr0TPActive and _G.Skyr0TPActive() then return end
            if os.clock() - _campMoveAt < (tonumber(_G.Skyr0CampMoveCooldown) or 0.6) then return end
            local _, hrp = getChar()
            if not hrp then return end
            local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(RC.plotName)
            local pods = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            local pod = pods and pods:FindFirstChild(tostring(RC.standSlot))
            local base = pod and pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
            local sp = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
            if not sp then return end
            local stand = sp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
            if (stand - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= 5 then return end

            local range = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitRange) or 10
            local tgt = RC._mt
            if tgt and tgt.uid then
                local tp = _podOf(tgt.uid)
                local okP, piv = tp and pcall(_pivotPos, tp)
                if okP and piv and (piv - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= range then return end
            end
            _campMoveAt = os.clock()
            pcall(function()
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(stand)
            end)
            clog("campmove", ("move -> next open slot %s"):format(tostring(RC.standSlot)), 1)
        end

        local function campSteer()
            if not RC.arrived then return end

            if _G.Skyr0CampMove == true then campHold() end
            local tgt = campTarget()
            if tgt then
                if RC._mt ~= tgt then
                    if not (RC._mt and RC._mt.uid == tgt.uid) then
                        clog(nil, ("steer -> %s [slot %s]"):format(tostring(tgt.name), tostring(tgt.slot)))
                    end
                    RC._mt = tgt
                    _bar(nil, nil, nil, { name = tgt.name, mps = tgt.genValue, plot = tgt.plot, slot = tgt.slot })
                end
            elseif RC._mt ~= nil then
                RC._mt = nil
                _bar(nil, nil, nil, false)
            end
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            while sg.Parent do
                task.wait(0.03)
                if RC.active then
                    pcall(campSteer)
                elseif RC._mt ~= nil then
                    RC._mt = nil
                end
            end
        end)

        local _asAcc, _asResolveDt = 1, 0.03
        local _asP, _asUid = nil, nil
        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if _G.SKYR0_GRAB_OWNS_STEAL == true then return end
            if not RC.active then _asP, _asUid = nil, nil; return end
            if RC._mt == nil then return end
            if isAutoStealing then return end
            if _G.JAF_StealCarryGate ~= false and LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then return end
            _asAcc = _asAcc + dt
            if _asAcc >= _asResolveDt or (_asP and not _asP.Parent) then
                _asAcc = 0
                local tgt = RC._mt
                _asP = tgt and findNearestPrompt(tgt) or nil
                if not _asP and tgt and tgt.uid and _G.JAF_CampCarried ~= false then
                    local _cok, _cv = pcall(_isBeingStolen, tgt)
                    if _cok and _cv == true then
                        local _pod = _podOf(tgt.uid)
                        local _sp = _pod and _pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
                        _sp = _sp and _sp:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                        local _att = _sp and _sp:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                        if _att then for _, _pp in ipairs(_att:GetChildren()) do
                            if _pp:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and tostring(_pp.ActionText or ""):find("Steal") then
                                local _cd = promptDistXZ(_pp)
                                if _cd and _cd <= (Config.StealRadius or 95) then _asP = _pp end
                                break
                            end
                        end end
                    end
                end
                _asUid = tgt and tgt.uid or nil
            end
            if _asP then
                if _G.JAF_RagdollStealSync ~= false then
                    local _ret = _ragUntil()
                    if _ret > 0 then
                        local _win = tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollPreArm) or ((Config.StealDuration or 1.3) - (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollLatePad) or 0.1))
                        if (_ret - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) > _win then return end
                    end end
                local ok, err = pcall(executeSteal, _asP, _asUid, false)
                if not ok then clog("rserr", "rose steal error: " .. tostring(err), 2) end
            end
        end)
    end
end)()

;(function()
    if type(_G.SKYR0_GRAB_SHUTDOWN) == "function" then pcall(_G.SKYR0_GRAB_SHUTDOWN) end
    local controllerAlive = true
    local grabStarted = false
    local grabStarting = false
    local capturedSetNearest = nil
    local capturedSetInstant = nil

    local function grabWanted()

        local camp = _G.Skyr0Camp
        return camp ~= nil and camp.active == true
    end

    local function startGrab()
        if grabStarted or grabStarting then return end
        grabStarting = true
        _G.SKYR0_GRAB_ENABLED = true
        task.spawn(function()

            getgenv().SKYR0_STEAL_MODE = "Instant"

            pcall(function()
                if setthreadidentity then
                    setthreadidentity(8)
                end
            end)
            local P = game:GetService("Players")
            local C = P.LocalPlayer
            if not game:IsLoaded() then
                game.Loaded:Wait()
            end
            local P = {
                SelectedPetData = nil,
                AllAnimalsCache = nil,
                DisableStealSpeed = nil,
                ListNeedsRedraw = true,
                StealSpeedToggleFunc = nil,
                _ssUpdateBtn = nil,
                BalloonedPlayers = {},
                MobileScaleObjects = {},
                RefreshMobileScale = nil,
                MobileActionButtons = {},
                RefreshMobileActionButtons = nil,
            }
            do
                local C = { AUTO_STEAL = false, RADIUS = 12 }
                local r =
                    {
                        {
                            min = Vector3.new(-337.448303, -3.898971, -122.397758),
                            max = Vector3.new(-328.004578, -3.898971, 242.625626),
                        },
                        {
                            min = Vector3.new(-327.25766, -3.899109, -122.228622),
                            max = Vector3.new(-320.600891, -3.899109, 242.612259),
                        },
                        {
                            min = Vector3.new(-319.783386, -3.89897, -122.227089),
                            max = Vector3.new(-312.908325, -3.89897, 242.585617),
                        },
                        {
                            min = Vector3.new(-312.445648, -3.899108, -122.389832),
                            max = Vector3.new(-305.489899, -3.899108, 242.456818),
                        },
                        {
                            min = Vector3.new(-305.037048, -3.89897, -122.230743),
                            max = Vector3.new(-293.957489, -3.89897, 242.606873),
                        },
                        {
                            min = Vector3.new(-491.448608, -3.898972, -122.253258),
                            max = Vector3.new(-481.811737, -3.898972, 242.615005),
                        },
                        {
                            min = Vector3.new(-498.971069, -3.89897, -122.382767),
                            max = Vector3.new(-491.74884, -3.89897, 242.612061),
                        },
                        {
                            min = Vector3.new(-506.436737, -3.898972, -122.411476),
                            max = Vector3.new(-499.318542, -3.898972, 242.615982),
                        },
                        {
                            min = Vector3.new(-513.783569, -3.898972, -122.223297),
                            max = Vector3.new(-506.801849, -3.898972, 242.62709),
                        },
                        {
                            min = Vector3.new(-525.236938, -3.898972, -122.409813),
                            max = Vector3.new(-514.265015, -3.898972, 242.608932),
                        },
                    }
                local R = game:GetService("Players")
                local d = game:GetService("RunService")
                local n = R.LocalPlayer
                do
                    local R

                        n.DevEnableMouseLock = true
                        n.DevCameraOcclusionMode = Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam

                            R:Disconnect()
                        end

                            local R = workspace.CurrentCamera
                            if R and R.CameraSubject and R.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Custom then
                                R.CFrame = R.CFrame
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    f()
                    n.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        f()
                    end)
                end
                local R = {}
                local d = {}
                local f = 0
                function _G.Skyr0GrabSafePollRate()
                    if os.clock() < f then
                        return 0.27
                    end
                    return 0.1
                end
                function _G.Skyr0GrabSafePollBoost()
                    f = os.clock() + 3
                end
                local f = {}
                local function J()
                    local a = n.Character
                    return a and a:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                end
                local function n(a)
                    for e, x in ipairs(r) do
                        if
                            a.X >= math.min(x.min.X, x.max.X)
                            and a.X <= math.max(x.min.X, x.max.X)
                            and a.Z >= math.min(x.min.Z, x.max.Z)
                            and a.Z <= math.max(x.min.Z, x.max.Z)
                        then
                            return e
                        end
                    end
                end
                local function r(a)
                    local e = a.Parent
                    if not e then
                        return
                    end
                    if e:IsA("Attachment") and e.Parent then
                        e = e.Parent
                    end
                    if e:IsA("BasePart") then
                        return e.Position
                    elseif e:IsA("Model") then
                        return e:GetPivot().Position
                    end
                end
                local function a(e)
                    if not P then
                        return false
                    end

                    local x = (_G.Skyr0_StealStatus and _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.target)
                        or (SharedState and SharedState.SelectedPetData)
                        or P.SelectedPetData
                    if not x then
                        return false
                    end
                    local Q = e:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                    if not Q then
                        return false
                    end
                    if x.plot then
                        local V = e:FindFirstAncestor(x.plot)
                        if not V then
                            return false
                        end
                    end
                    if x.slot ~= nil then

                        local V = e:FindFirstAncestor(tostring(x.slot))
                        return V ~= nil and V.Parent ~= nil and V.Parent.Name == "AnimalPodiums"
                    end
                    if x.name then
                        local e = string.lower(x.name)
                        local x = Q
                        while x do
                            if x.Name and string.lower(x.Name) == e then
                                return true
                            end
                            x = x.Parent
                        end
                    end
                    return false
                end
                local function e(x, Q)
                    if _G.SKYR0_GRAB_ENABLED ~= true then return false end
                    local camp = _G.Skyr0Camp
                    if camp and camp.active == true then
                        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                        local plot = x
                        while plot and plots and plot.Parent ~= plots do plot = plot.Parent end
                        if not plots or not plot or plot.Parent ~= plots or plot.Name ~= camp.plotName then return false end
                    end
                    if not x or not x.Parent then
                        return false
                    end
                    if not x.Enabled then
                        return false
                    end
                    local V = r(x)
                    if not V then
                        return false
                    end
                    local r = x:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                    if r then
                        local r = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                        if r then
                            local i = x:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
                            while i and i.Parent ~= r do
                                i = i.Parent
                            end
                            if i then
                                local r = i:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                                if r then
                                    local i = r:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
                                    local r = i and i:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                                    if r then
                                        local i = r.Text:lower()
                                        if
                                            i:find(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Name:lower(), 1, true)
                                            or i:find(game.Players.LocalPlayer.DisplayName:lower(), 1, true)
                                        then
                                            return false
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    local reach = tonumber(_G.Skyr0K7Radius) or C.RADIUS
                    local _camping = camp and camp.active == true
                    local _log = _camping and _G.Skyr0CampLog or nil
                    local _slot = "?"
                    if _log then
                        local nd = x.Parent
                        while nd and nd.Parent and nd.Parent.Name ~= "AnimalPodiums" do nd = nd.Parent end
                        if nd and nd.Parent then _slot = "slot " .. tostring(nd.Name) end
                    end

                    local _armed = _G.Skyr0_StealStatus and _G.Skyr0_StealStatus.target
                    if _camping then

                        if _G.SKYR0_NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE == true then
                            local r = n(Q)
                            local i = n(V)
                            if not r or r ~= i then
                                return false
                            end
                        end

                        local md = typeof(x.MaxActivationDistance) == "number"
                                and x.MaxActivationDistance > 0
                                and x.MaxActivationDistance
                            or C.RADIUS
                        reach = math.min(tonumber(_G.Skyr0CampGrabRadius) or C.RADIUS, md)
                    elseif _armed and _armed.plot and _armed.slot ~= nil then
                        if not a(x) then
                            return false
                        end
                    else
                        if _G.SKYR0_NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE == true then
                            local r = n(Q)
                            local i = n(V)
                            if not r or r ~= i then
                                return false
                            end
                        end
                        if _G.SKYR0_NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE ~= true then
                            if not a(x) then
                                return false
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    local _d = (V - Q).Magnitude
                    if _d > reach then
                        if _log then
                            _log("far:" .. tostring(x), ("skip %s: %.0f studs away (radius %s)"):format(_slot, _d, tostring(reach)), 2)
                        end
                        return false
                    end
                    if _log then
                        _log("ok:" .. tostring(x), ("IN RANGE %s: %.0f studs -> firing"):format(_slot, _d), 1)
                    end
                    return true
                end
                local function r(n, a)
                    local x = os.clock()
                    local Q = d[n]
                    if Q and x - Q < a then
                        return false
                    end
                    d[n] = x
                    return true
                end
                local function n(a, x, Q)
                    if not a or not a.Parent then
                        return
                    end
                    if not a.Enabled then
                        return
                    end
                    if not r(a, Q) then
                        return
                    end
                    if _G.Skyr0CampLog and _G.Skyr0Camp and _G.Skyr0Camp.active == true then
                        if type(fireproximityprompt) ~= "function" then
                            _G.Skyr0CampLog("nofpp", "your executor has NO fireproximityprompt -- nothing can be grabbed", 5)
                        else
                            _G.Skyr0CampLog("fire:" .. tostring(a), ("fire x%d on %s"):format(x, a:GetFullName()), 1)
                        end
                    end
                    for r = 1, x do
                        local ok, err = pcall(function()
                            fireproximityprompt(a, 0)
                        end)
                        if not ok and _G.Skyr0CampLog then
                            _G.Skyr0CampLog("ferr", "fireproximityprompt error: " .. tostring(err), 3)
                        end
                    end
                end
                local function r(a)
                    if R[a] then
                        return
                    end
                    R[a] = true
                    local function x()
                        local Q = J()
                        if not Q then
                            return
                        end
                        local V = Q.Position
                        if e(a, V) then
                            C.AUTO_STEAL = true
                            local Q = os.clock()
                            local V = f[a]
                            if not V or Q - V >= 0.08 then
                                f[a] = Q
                                n(a, 25, 0)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    task.defer(function()
                        x()
                    end)
                    pcall(function()
                        a:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
                            if a.Enabled then
                                x()
                            end
                        end)
                    end)
                    a.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
                        if not a:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                            R[a] = nil
                            d[a] = nil
                            f[a] = nil
                        end
                    end)
                end
                local function d()
                    local f = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                    if not f then
                        return
                    end
                    for a, a in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
                        local f = a:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
                        if f then
                            for a, a in ipairs(f:GetDescendants()) do
                                if a:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                                    r(a)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                d()
                workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                    if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and d:FindFirstAncestor("AnimalPodiums") then
                        r(d)
                    end
                end)
                task.spawn(function()
                    while task.wait(_G.Skyr0GrabSafePollRate()) do
                        local r = J()
                        if not r then
                            C.AUTO_STEAL = false
                            continue
                        end
                        local d = r.Position
                        local camp = _G.Skyr0Camp
                        if _G.Skyr0CampLog and camp and camp.active == true then
                            local total, inCamp, enabled = 0, 0, 0
                            local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                            local cp = plots and camp.plotName and plots:FindFirstChild(camp.plotName)
                            for f in pairs(R) do
                                total = total + 1
                                if cp and f:IsDescendantOf(cp) then
                                    inCamp = inCamp + 1
                                    if f.Enabled then enabled = enabled + 1 end
                                end
                            end
                            _G.Skyr0CampLog("poll", ("K7 poll: grabEnabled=%s, %d prompts tracked, %d in camped base %s (%d enabled), you at %.0f, %.0f, %.0f")
                                :format(tostring(_G.SKYR0_GRAB_ENABLED), total, inCamp, tostring(camp.plotName), enabled, d.X, d.Y, d.Z), 3)
                        end
                        local r = false
                        for f in pairs(R) do
                            if e(f, d) then
                                r = true
                                if C.AUTO_STEAL then
                                    n(f, 3, 0.12)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        C.AUTO_STEAL = r
                    end
                end)
            end

                    eG = eG or 1

                    local QG = math.max(1, xG)
                    local xG = math.floor(math.log(QG, 1000))
                    local QG = CG[xG + 1] or "e+" .. xG
                    local CG = aG * (10 ^ eG / 1000 ^ xG)
                    local aG = math.floor(CG) / 10 ^ eG
                    return (("%." .. eG .. "f"):format(aG)):gsub("%.?0+$", "") .. QG
                end
                local function CG(aG, eG, xG)
                    local QG = rG[aG]

                    local QG = 1

                        if VG and VG.Modifier then
                            QG = QG + VG.Modifier
                        end

                    local nG = false

                                    QG = QG + xG.MultiplierModifier

                            end
                        end
                    end
                    local fG = math.round(aG * QG)
                    if nG then
                        fG = math.round(fG * 0.5)
