                local function TG(IG)
                    if not IG or IG == "None" then
                        return ""
                    end
                    local pG = ""
                    if IG == "Cursed" then
                        pG = "<font color='rgb(200,0,0)'>Cur</font><font color='rgb(0,0,0)'>sed</font>"
                    elseif IG == "Gold" then
                        pG = "<font color='rgb(255,215,0)'>Gold</font>"
                    elseif IG == "Diamond" then
                        pG = "<font color='rgb(0,255,255)'>Diamond</font>"
                    elseif IG == "YinYang" then
                        pG = "<font color='rgb(255,255,255)'>Yin</font><font color='rgb(0,0,0)'>Yang</font>"
                    elseif IG == "Candy" then
                        pG = "<font color='rgb(255,105,180)'>Candy</font>"
                    elseif IG == "Divine" then
                        pG = "<font color='rgb(255,255,255)'>Divine</font>"
                    elseif IG == "Rainbow" then
                        local jG = {
                            "rgb(255,0,0)",
                            "rgb(255,127,0)",
                            "rgb(255,255,0)",
                            "rgb(0,255,0)",
                            "rgb(0,0,255)",
                            "rgb(75,0,130)",
                            "rgb(148,0,211)",
                        }
                        for lG = 1, #IG do
                            pG = pG .. "<font color='" .. jG[(lG - 1) % #jG + 1] .. "'>" .. IG:sub(lG, lG) .. "</font>"
                        end
                    elseif IG == "Radioactive" then
                        pG = "<font color='rgb(132,255,0)'>Radioactive</font>"
                    elseif IG == "Galaxy" then
                        pG = "<font color='rgb(170,85,255)'>Galaxy</font>"
                    else
                        pG = IG
                    end
                    return "<font weight='800'>" .. pG .. " </font>"
                end
                local function IG(pG)

                grabSecLbl.BackgroundColor3 = _campC
                grabSecLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
                grabSecLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                grabSecLbl.TextSize = math.floor(10 * _ms)
                grabSecLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5, 15, 20)
                grabSecLbl.Text = "NEAREST"
                Instance.new("UICorner", grabSecLbl).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
                do
                    local p = Instance.new("UIPadding", grabSecLbl)
                    p.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, math.floor(7 * _ms)); p.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, math.floor(7 * _ms))
                end

                local grabGenLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", hud)
                grabGenLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.floor(110 * _ms), 0, math.floor(16 * _ms))
                grabGenLbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -PAD - math.floor(110 * _ms), 0, math.floor(6 * _ms))
                grabGenLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                grabGenLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                grabGenLbl.TextSize = math.floor(12 * _ms)
                grabGenLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 160)
                grabGenLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
                grabGenLbl.Text = ""

                local MG = Instance.new("TextLabel", hud)
                MG.Size = UDim2.new(1, -PAD * 2 - math.floor(70 * _ms), 0, math.floor(18 * _ms))
                MG.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(PAD, math.floor(25 * _ms))
                MG.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                MG.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                MG.TextSize = math.floor(15 * _ms)
                MG.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 255, 255)
                MG.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                MG.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                MG.Text = "Searching..."

                local FG = Instance.new("TextLabel", hud)
                FG.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.floor(70 * _ms), 0, math.floor(18 * _ms))
                FG.Position = UDim2.new(1, -PAD - math.floor(70 * _ms), 0, math.floor(25 * _ms))
                FG.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                FG.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                FG.TextSize = math.floor(14 * _ms)
                FG.TextColor3 = _campCL
                FG.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
                FG.Text = "0%"

                local track = Instance.new("Frame", hud)
                track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -PAD * 2, 0, math.max(4, math.floor(6 * _ms)))
                track.Position = UDim2.new(0, PAD, 1, -math.floor(11 * _ms))
                track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 35, 40)
                track.BorderSizePixel = 0
                track.ClipsDescendants = true
                Instance.new("UICorner", track).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

                local bG = Instance.new("Frame", track)
                bG.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                bG.BackgroundColor3 = _campC
                bG.BorderSizePixel = 0
                Instance.new("UICorner", bG).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
                do
                    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient", bG)
                    g.Color = ColorSequence.new{
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, _campCL),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, _campC),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, _campCG)
                    }
                end
                local wG = bG
                local stealBarWasVisible = nil
                _G.SKYR0_GRAB_RESTORE_NORMAL_BAR = function()
                    hudGui.Enabled = false
                    if normalStealBar and normalStealBar.Parent and stealBarWasVisible ~= nil then
                        normalStealBar.Visible = stealBarWasVisible
                    end
                    stealBarWasVisible = nil
                end
                task.spawn(function()
                    while bG.Parent do
                        local pct = math.clamp(math.floor(bG.Size.X.Scale * 100 + 0.5), 0, 100)
                        FG.Text = tostring(pct) .. "%"
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                end)

                local function sG() end
                P.UpdateAutoStealUI = function() end
                _G.Skyr0GrabSetStealNearest = function(grabV)
                    fG = grabV == true
                    if fG then
                        aG = false
                        eG = false
                        UG = nil
                    end
                    i.StealNearest = fG
                    i.StealHighest = false
                    i.StealPriority = false
                    _G.SKYR0_NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = (fG and xG)
                    U()
                end
                _G.Skyr0GrabSetInstantSteal = function(grabV)
                    xG = grabV == true
                    QG = false
                    VG = false
                    i.InstantSteal = xG
                    _G.SKYR0_INSTANT_DISPLAY = xG
                    _G.SKYR0_NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = (fG and xG)
                    U()
                end
                task.spawn(function()
                    while true do
                        task.wait(1.5)
                        if not xG then
                            continue
                        end
                        xG = false
                        QG = false
                        VG = false
                        i.InstantSteal = false
                        _G.SKYR0_NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = false
                        task.wait(0.05)
                        xG = true
                        i.InstantSteal = true
                        _G.SKYR0_NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = (fG and true)
                    end
                end)
                local function b(KG)
                    if not KG then
                        return nil
                    end
                    local oG = cG[KG.uid]
                    if oG and oG.Parent then
                        return oG
                    end
                    local oG = a.Plots:FindFirstChild(KG.plot)
                    if not oG then
                        return nil
                    end
                    local IG = oG:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
                    if not IG then
                        return nil
                    end
                    local pG = dG(oG.Name)
                    local jG = pG and pG.AnimalList
                    if not jG then
                        local pG = IG:FindFirstChild(KG.slot)
                        if pG then
                            local lG = pG:FindFirstChild("Base")
                            local pG = lG and lG:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                            if pG then
                                local lG = pG:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                                if lG then
                                    for pG, pG in ipairs(lG:GetChildren()) do
                                        if pG:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                                            cG[KG.uid] = pG
                                            return pG
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return nil
                    end
                    if not jG then
                        return nil
                    end
                    local pG = KG.name and KG.name:lower() or ""
                    local lG = KG.slot
                    local WG = nil
                    for XG, tG in pairs(jG) do
                        if type(tG) == "table" and tostring(XG) == lG then
                            local jG, lG = tG.Index, rG[tG.Index]
                            if lG and (lG.DisplayName or jG):lower() == pG then
                                WG = IG:FindFirstChild(tostring(XG))
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if not WG then
                        WG = IG:FindFirstChild(KG.slot)
                    end
                    if WG then
                        local IG = WG:FindFirstChild("Base")
                        local jG = IG and IG:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                        if jG then
                            local IG = jG:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                            if IG then
                                for lG, lG in ipairs(IG:GetChildren()) do
                                    if lG:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and lG.Enabled and lG.ActionText == "Steal" then
                                        cG[KG.uid] = lG
                                        return lG
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            local IG = jG.Position
                            local jG, lG = IG.X, IG.Z
                            local WG = nil
                            local XG = math.huge
                            for tG, tG in pairs(oG:GetDescendants()) do
                                if tG:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and tG.Enabled and tG.ActionText == "Steal" then
                                    local oG = tG.Parent
                                    local GG = nil
                                    if oG and oG:IsA("BasePart") then
                                        GG = oG.Position
                                    elseif oG and oG:IsA("Attachment") and oG.Parent and oG.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                                        GG = oG.Parent.Position
                                    end
                                    if GG then
                                        local oG = IG.Y
                                        if pG:find("la secret combinasion") then
                                            oG = IG.Y - 5
                                        end
                                        local IG = math.sqrt((GG.X - jG) ^ 2 + (GG.Z - lG) ^ 2)
                                        if IG < 5 and GG.Y > oG then
                                            local IG = GG.Y - oG
                                            if IG < XG then
                                                XG = IG
                                                WG = tG
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if WG then
                                cG[KG.uid] = WG
                                return WG
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    return nil
                end
                local function KG(oG)
                    if qG[oG] then
                        return
                    end
                    local IG = { holdCallbacks = {}, triggerCallbacks = {}, holdEndCallbacks = {}, ready = true }
                    local pG, jG = pcall(getconnections, oG.PromptButtonHoldBegan)
                    if pG and type(jG) == "table" then
                        for pG, pG in ipairs(jG) do
                            if type(pG.Function) == "function" then
                                table.insert(IG.holdCallbacks, pG.Function)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    local pG, jG = pcall(getconnections, oG.Triggered)
                    if pG and type(jG) == "table" then
                        for pG, pG in ipairs(jG) do
                            if type(pG.Function) == "function" then
                                table.insert(IG.triggerCallbacks, pG.Function)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    local pG, jG = pcall(getconnections, oG.PromptButtonHoldEnded)
                    if pG and type(jG) == "table" then
                        for pG, pG in ipairs(jG) do
                            if type(pG.Function) == "function" then
                                table.insert(IG.holdEndCallbacks, pG.Function)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if #IG.holdCallbacks > 0 or #IG.triggerCallbacks > 0 or #IG.holdEndCallbacks > 0 then
                        qG[oG] = IG
                    end
                end
                local function oG(IG)
                    for pG, pG in ipairs(IG) do
                        task.spawn(pG)
                    end
                end
                local function IG(pG)
                    local jG = dG(pG)
                    if jG then
                        return S(jG.Owner)
                    end
                    return false
                end
                local function pG()
                    local jG = C.Character and C.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not jG then
                        return nil, math.huge, nil
                    end
                    local lG = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                    if not lG then
                        return nil, math.huge, nil
                    end
                    local WG, XG, tG = nil, math.huge, nil
                    for GG, GG in ipairs(lG:GetChildren()) do
                        if IG(GG.Name) then
                            continue
                        end
                        local IG = math.huge
                        pcall(function()
                            IG = (GG:GetPivot().Position - jG.Position).Magnitude
                        end)
                        if IG > 100 then
                            continue
                        end
                        local IG = GG:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
                        if not IG then
                            continue
                        end
                        for lG, lG in ipairs(IG:GetChildren()) do
                            local IG = lG:FindFirstChild("Base")
                            local GG = IG and IG:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                            if not GG then
                                continue
                            end
                            local IG = (GG.Position - jG.Position).Magnitude
                            if IG > 60 or IG >= XG then
                                continue
                            end
                            local jG = GG:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                            if not jG then
                                continue
                            end
                            local GG = jG:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                            if GG and GG.Parent and GG.Enabled then
                                WG = GG
                                XG = IG
                                tG = lG.Name
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    return WG, XG, tG
                end
                local function IG(jG, lG)
                    if _G.SKYR0_GRAB_ENABLED ~= true then return false end
                    local WG = qG[jG]
                    if not WG or not WG.ready then
                        return false
                    end
                    WG.ready = false
                    task.spawn(function()
                        if _G.SKYR0_GRAB_ENABLED ~= true then WG.ready = true return end
                        if BG ~= lG then
                            if zG then
                                zG:Cancel()
                            end
                            wG.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                            BG = lG
                        end
                        if _G.SKYR0_GRAB_ENABLED == true and #WG.holdCallbacks > 0 then
                            oG(WG.holdCallbacks)
                        end

                        wG.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                        wG.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                        zG = f:Create(wG, TweenInfo.new(1.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) })
                        zG:Play()
                        zG.Completed:Wait()
                        if _G.SKYR0_GRAB_ENABLED == true and BG == lG and #WG.triggerCallbacks > 0 then
                            oG(WG.triggerCallbacks)
                        end
                        WG.ready = true
                    end)
                    return true
                end
                local function oG(jG, lG)
                    if _G.SKYR0_GRAB_ENABLED ~= true then return false end
                    if not jG or not jG.Parent then
                        return false
                    end
                    KG(jG)
                    if not qG[jG] then
                        return false
                    end
                    if BG ~= lG then
                        if zG then
                            zG:Cancel()
                            zG = nil
                        end
                        wG.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                    end
                    return IG(jG, lG)
                end
                local function qG()
                    for BG, BG in pairs(cG) do
                        if BG and BG.Parent then
                            KG(BG)
                        end
                    end
                end
                task.spawn(function()
                    while task.wait(2) do
                        if nG then
                            qG()
                        end
                    end
                end)
                local KG = {}
                local function qG(BG)
                    if not BG then
                        return ""
                    end
                    local IG = ""
                    for jG, lG in pairs(BG) do
                        if type(lG) == "table" then
                            IG = IG .. tostring(jG) .. tostring(lG.Index) .. tostring(lG.Mutation)
                        end
                    end
                    return IG
                end
                local function BG(IG)
                    local jG = false
                    pcall(function()
                        local lG = dG(IG.Name)
                        if not lG then
                            return
                        end
                        local WG = lG.AnimalList
                        local XG = lG.Owner
                        if
                            not XG
                            or S(XG)
                            or typeof(XG) == "Instance" and not r:FindFirstChild(XG.Name)
                            or type(XG) == "string" and not r:FindFirstChild(XG)
                        then
                            KG[IG.Name] = nil
                            for S = #yG, 1, -1.0 do
                                if yG[S].plot == IG.Name then
                                    table.remove(yG, S)
                                    jG = true
                                end
                            end
                            return
                        end
                        if not WG then
                            KG[IG.Name] = nil
                            for S = #yG, 1, -1.0 do
                                if yG[S].plot == IG.Name then
                                    table.remove(yG, S)
                                    jG = true
                                end
                            end
                            return
                        end
                        local S = typeof(XG) == "Instance" and XG.Name or tostring(XG)
                        local lG = qG(WG, S)
                        if KG[IG.Name] == lG then
                            return
                        end
                        for qG = #yG, 1, -1.0 do
                            if yG[qG].plot == IG.Name then
                                table.remove(yG, qG)
                            end
                        end
                        for qG, XG in pairs(WG) do
                            if type(XG) == "table" then
                                local WG, tG = XG.Index, rG[XG.Index]
                                if tG then
                                    local rG = XG.Mutation or "None"
                                    if rG == "Yin Yang" then
                                        rG = "YinYang"
                                    end
                                    local GG = XG.Traits and #XG.Traits > 0 and table.concat(XG.Traits, ", ") or "None"
                                    local vG = CG(WG, XG.Mutation, XG.Traits)
                                    local CG = "$" .. JG(vG) .. "/s"
                                    table.insert(
                                        yG,
                                        {
                                            name = tG.DisplayName or WG,
                                            genText = CG,
                                            genValue = vG,
                                            mutation = rG,
                                            traits = GG,
                                            owner = S,
                                            plot = IG.Name,
                                            slot = tostring(qG),
                                            uid = IG.Name .. "_" .. tostring(qG),
                                        }
                                    )
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        KG[IG.Name] = lG
                        jG = true
                    end)
                    if jG then
                        table.sort(yG, function(S, CG)
                            return S.genValue > CG.genValue
                        end)
                        P.AllAnimalsCache = yG
                        P.ListNeedsRedraw = true
                        if P.UpdateAutoStealUI then
                            P.UpdateAutoStealUI()
                        end
                    end
                end
                local function S(CG)
                    local rG, JG = nil, 0
                    while not rG and JG < 40 do
                        rG = dG(CG.Name)
                        if not rG then
                            JG = JG + 1
                            task.wait(0.07)
                        end
                    end
                    if not rG then
                        return
                    end
                    BG(CG)
                    local function rG(JG)
                        if not JG then
                            return
                        end
                        JG.ChildAdded:Connect(function()
                            task.wait(0.15)
                            BG(CG)
                        end)
                        JG.ChildRemoved:Connect(function()
                            for JG = #yG, 1, -1.0 do
                                if yG[JG].plot == CG.Name then
                                    table.remove(yG, JG)
                                end
                            end
                            KG[CG.Name] = nil
                            _getPetsCache = nil
                            for JG in pairs(cG) do
                                if JG:sub(1, #CG.Name) == CG.Name then
                                    cG[JG] = nil
                                end
                            end
                            P.ListNeedsRedraw = true
                            if P.UpdateAutoStealUI then
                                P.UpdateAutoStealUI()
                            end
                            task.wait(0.15)
                            BG(CG)
                        end)
                    end
                    local JG = CG:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
                    rG(JG)
                    CG.ChildAdded:Connect(function(JG)
                        if JG.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then
                            rG(JG)
                            BG(CG)
                        end
                    end)
                    CG.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(rG)
                        if rG.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then
                            for rG = #yG, 1, -1.0 do
                                if yG[rG].plot == CG.Name then
                                    table.remove(yG, rG)
                                end
                            end
                            KG[CG.Name] = nil
                            _getPetsCache = nil
                            P.ListNeedsRedraw = true
                            if P.UpdateAutoStealUI then
                                P.UpdateAutoStealUI()
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                    task.spawn(function()
                        while CG.Parent do
                            task.wait(10)
                            BG(CG)
                        end
                    end)
                end
                local CG = a:WaitForChild("Plots", 8)
                if CG then
                    for rG, rG in ipairs(CG:GetChildren()) do
                        S(rG)
                    end
                    CG.ChildAdded:Connect(function(rG)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        S(rG)
                    end)
                    CG.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(S)
                        KG[S.Name] = nil
                        _getPetsCache = nil
                        for CG = #yG, 1, -1.0 do
                            if yG[CG].plot == S.Name then
                                table.remove(yG, CG)
                            end
                        end
                        for CG in pairs(cG) do
                            if CG:sub(1, #S.Name) == S.Name then
                                cG[CG] = nil
                            end
                        end
                        P.ListNeedsRedraw = true
                        if P.UpdateAutoStealUI then
                            P.UpdateAutoStealUI()
                        end
                    end)
                end
                local function S(CG)
                    if #CG == 0 then
                        return
                    end
                    if UG then
                        for rG, dG in ipairs(CG) do
                            if dG.uid == UG then
                                if iG ~= rG then
                                    iG = rG
                                    OG = dG.uid
                                end
                                MG.Text = dG.petName or "Unknown"
                                grabGenLbl.Text = dG.mpsText or ""
                                return
                            end
                        end
                        UG = nil
                    end
                    if eG then
                        for rG, rG in ipairs(X) do
                            local dG = rG:lower()
                            for rG, JG in ipairs(CG) do
                                if JG.petName and JG.petName:lower() == dG then
                                    if iG ~= rG then
                                        iG = rG
                                        OG = JG.uid
                                    end
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if iG ~= 1 then
                            iG = 1
                            OG = CG[1] and CG[1].uid
                        end
                    elseif fG then
                        local rG = C.Character
                        local dG = rG and rG:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if dG then
                            local rG, JG = 1, math.huge
                            for eG, KG in ipairs(CG) do
                                local qG = KG.animalData and M(KG.animalData)
                                if qG and qG:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    local KG = (dG.Position - qG.Position).Magnitude
                                    if KG < JG then
                                        JG = KG
                                        rG = eG
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if CG[rG] then
                                local dG = CG[rG]
                                MG.Text = dG.petName or "Unknown"
                                grabGenLbl.Text = dG.mpsText or ""
                            end
                            if not xG and iG ~= rG then
                                iG = rG
                                OG = CG[rG] and CG[rG].uid
                            end
                        end
                    elseif aG then
                        if iG ~= 1 then
                            iG = 1
                            OG = CG[1] and CG[1].uid
                        end
                    end
                end
                local grabPickAt = 0
                R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not nG then
                        return
                    end
                    local now = os.clock()
                    if now - grabPickAt < 0.1 then return end
                    grabPickAt = now
                    S(TG())
                end)
                task.spawn(function()
                    while true do
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        if nG then
                            local S = TG()
                            if #S > 0 then
                                P.ListNeedsRedraw = false
                                sG(nG, S)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    local grabVisible = _G.SKYR0_GRAB_ENABLED == true
                    hudGui.Enabled = grabVisible

                    local orig = _G.Skyr0OrigStealHud
                    if orig and orig.Parent then
                        local op, os_ = orig.Position, orig.Size
                        local top = op.Y.Offset - os_.Y.Offset * orig.AnchorPoint.Y
                        local cx = op.X.Offset + os_.X.Offset * (0.5 - orig.AnchorPoint.X)
                        hud.Position = UDim2.new(op.X.Scale, cx, op.Y.Scale, top - 8)
                    end
                    grabSecLbl.Text = (_G.Skyr0Camp and _G.Skyr0Camp.active == true) and "BASE CAMP" or "NEAREST"
                    if not grabVisible then
                        if zG then zG:Cancel(); zG = nil end
                        wG.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                        return
                    end
                    if not nG then
                        return
                    end
                    if xG then
                        if zG then
                            zG:Cancel()
                            zG = nil
                        end
                        wG.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                        wG.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                        if not VG then
                            VG = true
                            task.spawn(function()
                                if not game:IsLoaded() then
                                    game.Loaded:Wait()
                                end
                                task.wait(0.5)
                                QG = true
                            end)
                        end
                        if QG then
                            if fG and not UG then
                                local S, CG, rG = pG()
                            else
                                local S = TG()
                                if #S > 0 then
                                    if iG > #S then
                                        iG = #S
                                    end
                                    if iG < 1 then
                                        iG = 1
                                    end
                                    local CG = S[iG]
                                    if CG and not YG(CG.animalData) then
                                        local S = cG[CG.uid]
                                        if not S or not S.Parent then
                                            S = b(CG.animalData)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return
                    end
                    local S = TG()
                    if #S == 0 then
                        return
                    end
                    if iG > #S then
                        iG = #S
                    end
                    if iG < 1 then
                        iG = 1
                    end
                    local CG = S[iG]
                    if not CG or YG(CG.animalData) then
                        return
                    end
                    local S = cG[CG.uid]
                    if not S or not S.Parent then
                        S = b(CG.animalData)
                    end
                    if S then
                        oG(S, CG.uid)
                    end
                end)
                task.spawn(function()
                    while task.wait(0.5) do
                        sG(nG, TG())
                    end
                end)
                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(1)
                    P.ListNeedsRedraw = true
                    sG(nG, TG())
                end)
                task.spawn(function()
                    while true do
                        P.AllAnimalsCache = yG
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end)

            end)

            grabStarted = true
            grabStarting = false
        end)
    end

    _G.SKYR0_GRAB_SHUTDOWN = function()
        controllerAlive = false
        _G.SKYR0_GRAB_ENABLED = false
        if type(_G.SKYR0_GRAB_RESTORE_NORMAL_BAR) == "function" then
            pcall(_G.SKYR0_GRAB_RESTORE_NORMAL_BAR)
        end
        _G.SKYR0_GRAB_OWNS_STEAL = true
        if capturedSetNearest then pcall(capturedSetNearest, false) end
        if capturedSetInstant then pcall(capturedSetInstant, false) end
        _G.SKYR0_GRAB_OWNS_STEAL = false
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local applied = nil
        local wasReady = false
        local lastDbg = nil
        while controllerAlive do
            local wanted = grabWanted()
            if wanted and not grabStarted then startGrab() end
            local ready = grabStarted
                and type(_G.Skyr0GrabSetStealNearest) == "function"
                and type(_G.Skyr0GrabSetInstantSteal) == "function"
            if ready and not capturedSetNearest then
                capturedSetNearest = _G.Skyr0GrabSetStealNearest
                capturedSetInstant = _G.Skyr0GrabSetInstantSteal
            end

            _G.SKYR0_GRAB_ENABLED = wanted

            local camping = _G.Skyr0Camp and _G.Skyr0Camp.active == true
            local nearest = wanted and (camping or Config.StealNearest == true)
            local want = wanted and (camping and "camp" or (nearest and "nearest" or "selected")) or "off"

            if ready and (applied ~= want or not wasReady) then

                _G.SKYR0_GRAB_OWNS_STEAL = true
                if wanted then

                    pcall(capturedSetInstant, true)
                    pcall(capturedSetNearest, nearest)
                else

                    pcall(capturedSetNearest, false)
                    pcall(capturedSetInstant, false)
                    _G.SKYR0_GRAB_OWNS_STEAL = false
                end
                applied = want
            elseif not ready then

                _G.SKYR0_GRAB_OWNS_STEAL = wanted and (grabStarting or grabStarted)
            elseif wanted then
                _G.SKYR0_GRAB_OWNS_STEAL = true
            else
                _G.SKYR0_GRAB_OWNS_STEAL = false
            end

            if _G.Skyr0CampLog then
                local st = ("wanted=%s started=%s ready=%s mode=%s ownsSteal=%s nearestInstant=%s")
                    :format(tostring(wanted), tostring(grabStarted), tostring(ready), tostring(applied),
                        tostring(_G.SKYR0_GRAB_OWNS_STEAL), tostring(_G.SKYR0_NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE))
                if st ~= lastDbg then
                    lastDbg = st
                    _G.Skyr0CampLog(nil, "K7 grab: " .. st)
                end
            end

            wasReady = ready
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end)()

task.spawn(function()
    if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
    task.wait(1)

    local Players          = game:GetService("Players")
    local ReplicatedStorage= game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local RunService       = game:GetService("RunService")
    local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local LP               = Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerGui        = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

    local CFG = {
        BURST_SHOTS      = 5,
        SHOT_INTERVAL    = 0.005,
        EQUIP_DELAY      = 0.006,
        POST_FIRE_WAIT   = 0.005,
        SWAP_GAP         = 0.004,
        AIM_LEAD_TIME    = 0.18,
        AIM_TARGET_PARTS = {"HumanoidRootPart","UpperTorso","Torso","Head"},
    }

    local isFiring         = false
    local aimRemoteIndex   = {}
    local aimRemoteObjects = {}
    local _animalNames     = nil
    local _animalsData     = nil
    local _stealSessions   = {}
    local _morphCache      = {}
    local rowPool          = {}

    local function resolveUseItemRemote()
        local cached = _G._FH_UseItemRemote
        if typeof(cached) == "Instance" and cached.Parent then return cached end
        local nm = _G._FH_NET
        if nm and typeof(nm.UseItem) == "Instance" and nm.UseItem.Parent then
            _G._FH_UseItemRemote = nm.UseItem; return nm.UseItem
        end
        local _getconn  = getconnections
        local _getconst = (debug and debug.getconstants) or getconstants
        if type(_getconn) ~= "function" or type(_getconst) ~= "function" then return nil end
        local ok, netFolder = pcall(function()
            return ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages",15):WaitForChild("Net",15)
        end)
        if not ok or not netFolder then return nil end
        local found
        for _, r in ipairs(netFolder:GetChildren()) do
            if r:IsA("RemoteEvent") and not found then
                local okc, conns = pcall(_getconn, r.OnClientEvent)
                if okc and conns then
                    for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
                        if type(c.Function) == "function" then
                            local okk, consts = pcall(_getconst, c.Function)
                            if okk and consts then
                                for _, k in ipairs(consts) do
                                    if k == "PaintballHitted" then found = r; break end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if found then break end
                    end
                end
            end
            if found then break end
        end
        if found then _G._FH_UseItemRemote = found end
        return found
    end

    local function aimInitRemotes()
        local ok, children = pcall(function()
            return ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages"):WaitForChild("Net"):GetChildren()
        end)
        if not ok or not children then return end
        aimRemoteIndex, aimRemoteObjects = {}, {}
        for i, obj in ipairs(children) do
            if obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                local nxt = children[i+1]
                if nxt then
                    aimRemoteIndex[obj.Name]    = i+1
                    aimRemoteObjects[i+1]       = nxt
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function aimFireRemote(name, ...)
        if name == "RE/UseItem" or name == "UseItem" then
            local r = resolveUseItemRemote()
            if r then r:FireServer(...); return true end
        end
        local idx = aimRemoteIndex[name]
        if idx and aimRemoteObjects[idx] then
            aimRemoteObjects[idx]:FireServer(...); return true
        end
        return false
    end

    local function neutralizeTool(tool)
        if not tool then return end
        pcall(function() tool.Grip = CFrame.new(0,100,0) end)
        for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                pcall(function() d.Transparency=1; d.CanCollide=false; d.CanQuery=false end)
            elseif d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
                pcall(function() d.Transparency=1 end)
            elseif d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Trail") then
                pcall(function() d.Enabled=false end)
            elseif d:IsA("Sound") then
                pcall(function() d.Volume=0 end)
            elseif d:IsA("Light") then
                pcall(function() d.Enabled=false end)
            end
        end
    end

    local function killToolAnims()
        local char     = LP.Character
        local hum      = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        if not animator then return end
        for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            if track.Priority == Enum.AnimationPriority.Action then
                pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end)
            end
        end
    end

    local function killGrip()
        local char = LP.Character; if not char then return end
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                for _, w in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
                    if (w:IsA("Motor6D") or w:IsA("Weld")) and w.Name=="RightGrip" then
                        pcall(function() w:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function isAlive(char)
        if not char then return false end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        return hum ~= nil and hum.Health > 0
    end

    local function getBestPart(char)
        for _, n in ipairs(CFG.AIM_TARGET_PARTS) do
            local p = char:FindFirstChild(n); if p then return p end
        end
    end

    local function aimShootAt(targetPlayer)
        if not targetPlayer then return false end
        local char = targetPlayer.Character
        if not char or not isAlive(char) then return false end
        local part = getBestPart(char); if not part then return false end
        local vel  = Vector3.zero
        pcall(function()
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then vel = hrp.Velocity or Vector3.zero end
        end)
        local targetPos = part.Position + Vector3.new(0,0.5,0) + vel * CFG.AIM_LEAD_TIME
        if not (aimRemoteIndex["RE/UseItem"] or aimRemoteIndex["UseItem"]) then aimInitRemotes() end
        local ok = aimFireRemote("RE/UseItem", targetPos, part)
        if not ok then aimInitRemotes(); ok = aimFireRemote("RE/UseItem", targetPos, part) end
        return ok
    end

    local function fireBurstAt(targetPlayer, onDone)
        if isFiring then return end
        isFiring = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local char = LP.Character
            local bp   = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
            local cape = char and (char:FindFirstChild("Laser Cape") or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Laser Cape")))
            if not cape then isFiring = false; if onDone then onDone(false) end; return end
            local carpet = char and (char:FindFirstChild("Flying Carpet") or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Flying Carpet")))
            aimInitRemotes()

            if carpet then
                if carpet.Parent ~= char then carpet.Parent = char; task.wait(CFG.EQUIP_DELAY) end
                if bp and carpet.Parent == char then carpet.Parent = bp; task.wait(CFG.SWAP_GAP) end
            end

            if cape.Parent ~= char then
                neutralizeTool(cape)
                cape.Parent = char
                killToolAnims(); killGrip()
                task.wait(CFG.EQUIP_DELAY)
                killToolAnims()
            end

            for i = 1, CFG.BURST_SHOTS do
                pcall(aimShootAt, targetPlayer)
                if i < CFG.BURST_SHOTS then task.wait(CFG.SHOT_INTERVAL) end
            end

            task.wait(CFG.POST_FIRE_WAIT)
            if bp and cape.Parent == char then cape.Parent = bp end
            killToolAnims()
            if carpet and carpet.Parent ~= char then pcall(function() carpet.Parent = char end) end

            isFiring = false
            if onDone then onDone(true) end
        end)
    end

    local function loadAnimalNames()
        if _animalNames then return end
        pcall(function()
            if not _animalsData then
                local Datas = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Datas")
                _animalsData = Datas and require(Datas:FindFirstChild("Animals"))
            end
            if _animalsData then
                _animalNames = {}
                for idx, info in pairs(_animalsData) do
                    local disp = (type(info)=="table" and info.DisplayName) or tostring(idx)
                    _animalNames[tostring(idx):lower()] = disp
                    _animalNames[tostring(disp):lower()] = disp
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function getStealingWhat(plr)
        if not plr or not plr.Character then return nil end
        loadAnimalNames()
        if plr:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
            _stealSessions[plr.UserId] = nil; return nil
        end
        local attrIdx = plr:GetAttribute("StealingIndex")
        if attrIdx ~= nil and tostring(attrIdx) ~= "" then
            local nm = tostring(attrIdx)
            if _animalNames then nm = _animalNames[nm:lower()] or nm end
            _stealSessions[plr.UserId] = nm; return nm
        end
        local sess = _stealSessions[plr.UserId]
        if sess then return sess end
        if _animalNames then
            local morphNames = _morphCache[plr.UserId] and _morphCache[plr.UserId].names or {}
            local carried
            pcall(function()
                for _, m in ipairs(plr.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if m:IsA("Model") or m:IsA("MeshPart") then
                        local key = m.Name:lower()
                        local hit = _animalNames[key]
                        if hit and not morphNames[key] then carried = hit; return end
                    end
                end
            end)
            if carried then _stealSessions[plr.UserId] = carried; return carried end
        end
        return "Unknown"
    end

    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
        _stealSessions[plr.UserId] = nil
        _morphCache[plr.UserId]    = nil
    end)

    local oldGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("LCAimbotV2")
    if oldGui then oldGui:Destroy() end

    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name="LCAimbotV2"; sg.ResetOnSpawn=false
    sg.IgnoreGuiInset=true; sg.DisplayOrder=9999999; sg.Parent=playerGui

    local win = Instance.new("Frame")
    win.Name="Window"; win.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(220,284)
    do
        local sx = tonumber(_G.__LCAimbot_X) or 20
        local sy = tonumber(_G.__LCAimbot_Y) or 80
        win.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sx, sy)
    end
    win.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(18,22,38)
    win.BackgroundTransparency=0.15
    win.BorderSizePixel=0; win.Active=true; win.Parent=sg
    Instance.new("UICorner",win).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,10)
    local wStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",win)
    wStroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(50,60,90); wStroke.Thickness=1

    local tbar=Instance.new("Frame")
    tbar.Name="TBar"; tbar.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,28)
    tbar.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(18,22,38)
    tbar.BackgroundTransparency=0.1
    tbar.BorderSizePixel=0; tbar.Parent=win
    Instance.new("UICorner",tbar).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,10)
    local tbarFix=Instance.new("Frame")
    tbarFix.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,10); tbarFix.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,1,-10)
    tbarFix.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(18,22,38); tbarFix.BackgroundTransparency=0.1; tbarFix.BorderSizePixel=0; tbarFix.Parent=tbar

    local titleLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel")
    titleLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,1,0); titleLbl.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(12,0)
    titleLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; titleLbl.Text="Aimbot"
    titleLbl.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(180,230,80); titleLbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    titleLbl.TextSize=11; titleLbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; titleLbl.Parent=tbar

    do
        local dragging,dragStart,startPos=false,nil,nil
        tbar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging=true; dragStart=i.Position; startPos=win.Position
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if not dragging then return end
            if i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            local d=i.Position-dragStart
            local nx = startPos.X.Offset+d.X
            local ny = startPos.Y.Offset+d.Y
            win.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(nx, ny)
            _G.__LCAimbot_X = nx
            _G.__LCAimbot_Y = ny
        end)
        UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging=false end
        end)
    end

    local ownerPanel=Instance.new("Frame")
    ownerPanel.Name="OwnerPanel"; ownerPanel.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,36)
    ownerPanel.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,34)
    ownerPanel.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(24,30,50)
    ownerPanel.BackgroundTransparency=0.1
    ownerPanel.BorderSizePixel=0; ownerPanel.Parent=win
    Instance.new("UICorner",ownerPanel).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
    local oPanelStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",ownerPanel)
    oPanelStroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(50,60,90); oPanelStroke.Thickness=1

    local ownerHdrLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel")
    ownerHdrLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,14); ownerHdrLbl.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,2)
    ownerHdrLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; ownerHdrLbl.Text="BASE OWNER"
    ownerHdrLbl.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(170,180,200); ownerHdrLbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
    ownerHdrLbl.TextSize=9; ownerHdrLbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; ownerHdrLbl.Parent=ownerPanel

    local ownerValLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel")
    ownerValLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,16); ownerValLbl.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,16)
    ownerValLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; ownerValLbl.Text="—"
    ownerValLbl.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(180,230,80); ownerValLbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
    ownerValLbl.TextSize=11; ownerValLbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    ownerValLbl.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd; ownerValLbl.Parent=ownerPanel

    local ownerHitBtn=Instance.new("TextButton")
    ownerHitBtn.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); ownerHitBtn.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,0)
    ownerHitBtn.BackgroundTransparency=1; ownerHitBtn.Text=""
    ownerHitBtn.AutoButtonColor=false; ownerHitBtn.BorderSizePixel=0
    ownerHitBtn.ZIndex=ownerValLbl.ZIndex+1; ownerHitBtn.Parent=ownerPanel

    ownerHitBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if ownerValLbl.Text ~= "—" and ownerValLbl.Text ~= "" then
            ownerPanel.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(40,50,30)
            oPanelStroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,255,80)
        end
    end)
    ownerHitBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        ownerPanel.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(24,30,50)
        oPanelStroke.Color=(ownerValLbl.Text ~= "—" and ownerValLbl.Text ~= "")
            and Color3.fromRGB(180,230,80) or Color3.fromRGB(50,60,90)
    end)
    ownerHitBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local name = ownerValLbl.Text
        if name == "—" or name == "" then return end

        local target = nil
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr == LP then continue end
            if plr.DisplayName == name or plr.Name == name then
                target = plr; break
            end
        end
        if not target then return end
        if isFiring then return end
        fireBurstAt(target, nil)
    end)

    local secLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel")
    secLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-16,0,16); secLbl.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,76)
    secLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; secLbl.Text="PLAYERS - click to target"
    secLbl.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(170,180,200); secLbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
    secLbl.TextSize=10; secLbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; secLbl.Parent=win

    local div=Instance.new("Frame")
    div.Size=UDim2.new(1,-16,0,1); div.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,94)
    div.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(50,60,90); div.BorderSizePixel=0; div.Parent=win

    local scroll=Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    scroll.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,0,178); scroll.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,98)
    scroll.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(22,27,46)
    scroll.BackgroundTransparency=0.2
    scroll.BorderSizePixel=0; scroll.ScrollBarThickness=2
    scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3=Color3.fromRGB(50,60,90)
    scroll.CanvasSize=UDim2.fromOffset(0,0)
    scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y; scroll.Parent=win
    Instance.new("UICorner",scroll).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,7)
    local layout=Instance.new("UIListLayout",scroll)
    layout.Padding=UDim.new(0,3); layout.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.Name
    local pad=Instance.new("UIPadding",scroll)
    pad.PaddingTop=UDim.new(0,3); pad.PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,3)
    pad.PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,3); pad.PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0,3)

    local function makeRow(plr)
        local uid = plr.UserId
        if rowPool[uid] then return end

        local row=Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Name=plr.Name; row.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,30)
        row.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(24,30,50)
        row.BackgroundTransparency=0.1
        row.BorderSizePixel=0; row.Parent=scroll
        Instance.new("UICorner",row).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
        local rStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",row)
        rStroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(50,60,90); rStroke.Thickness=1

        local hitBtn=Instance.new("TextButton")
        hitBtn.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); hitBtn.BackgroundTransparency=1
        hitBtn.Text=""; hitBtn.AutoButtonColor=false; hitBtn.BorderSizePixel=0
        hitBtn.ZIndex=3; hitBtn.Parent=row

        local dot=Instance.new("Frame")
        dot.Name="Dot"; dot.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(6,6)
        dot.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(8,12)
        dot.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(50,60,90)
        dot.BorderSizePixel=0; dot.ZIndex=2; dot.Parent=row
        Instance.new("UICorner",dot).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)

        local nameLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLbl.Name="Name"; nameLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-50,0,16)
        nameLbl.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(20,7)
        nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1
        nameLbl.Text=plr.DisplayName
        nameLbl.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        nameLbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; nameLbl.TextSize=11
        nameLbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLbl.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        nameLbl.ZIndex=2; nameLbl.Parent=row

        local stealLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel")
        stealLbl.Name="StealTag"; stealLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-50,0,12)
        stealLbl.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(20,17)
        stealLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; stealLbl.Text=""
        stealLbl.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(180,230,80)
        stealLbl.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham; stealLbl.TextSize=9
        stealLbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        stealLbl.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        stealLbl.Visible=false; stealLbl.ZIndex=2; stealLbl.Parent=row

        local distLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel")
        distLbl.Name="Dist"; distLbl.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(44,30)
        distLbl.Position=UDim2.new(1,-48,0,0)
        distLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; distLbl.Text="--"
        distLbl.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(170,180,200)
        distLbl.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham; distLbl.TextSize=9
        distLbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        distLbl.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
        distLbl.ZIndex=2; distLbl.Parent=row

        hitBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            row.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(40,50,72)
            rStroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(180,230,80)
        end)
        hitBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            row.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(24,30,50)
            rStroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(50,60,90)
        end)

        hitBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if isFiring then return end
            fireBurstAt(plr, nil)
        end)

        rowPool[uid] = {
            row=row, dot=dot, nameLbl=nameLbl,
            stealLbl=stealLbl, distLbl=distLbl,
            rStroke=rStroke,
        }
    end

    local function removeRow(uid)
        local r = rowPool[uid]
        if r then r.row:Destroy(); rowPool[uid]=nil end
    end

    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LP then task.spawn(makeRow, plr) end
    end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
        if plr ~= LP then task.spawn(makeRow, plr) end
    end)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
        removeRow(plr.UserId)
    end)

    local BOD_FALLBACK_RADIUS = 120

    local function _bodStripSuffix(txt)
        if not txt or txt == "" then return nil end
        txt = txt:gsub("'s%s+Base$",""):gsub("%s+Base$",""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        return (txt ~= "") and txt or nil
    end

    local function _bodSignText(plot)
        local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        if not sign then return nil end
        local sg = sign:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceGui")
        if not sg then return nil end
        local fr = sg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame")
        if not fr then return nil end
        local tl = fr:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
        return tl and tl.Text or nil
    end

    local function _bodOwnerFromChannel(plot)
        local ch = _G.Skyr0_GetPlotChannel and _G.Skyr0_GetPlotChannel(plot.Name)
        if not ch then return nil end
        local owner = _G.Skyr0ChanGet and _G.Skyr0ChanGet(ch, "Owner")
        if not owner then return nil end
        if typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner:IsA("Player") then return owner.DisplayName end
        if type(owner) == "number" then
            local p = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(owner)
            if p then return p.DisplayName end
        end
        if type(owner) == "table" then return owner.DisplayName or owner.Name end
        return nil
    end

    local function _bodResolvePlayerByName(name)
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr.DisplayName == name or plr.Name == name then return plr end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function resolveBaseOwner()
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return nil end

        local myChar = LP.Character
        local myHRP  = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        local bestPlot, bestDist = nil, math.huge

        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr == LP then continue end
            if plr:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then continue end
            local sc  = plr.Character
            local hrp = sc and sc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then continue end
            for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                if not sign then continue end
                local d = (hrp.Position - sign.Position).Magnitude
                if d < bestDist then bestDist = d; bestPlot = plot end
            end
        end

        if not bestPlot and myHRP then
            for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                if not sign then continue end
                local d = (myHRP.Position - sign.Position).Magnitude
                if d < bestDist then bestDist = d; bestPlot = plot end
            end
        end

        if not bestPlot then return nil end

        local raw = _bodSignText(bestPlot)
        local fromSign = raw and _bodStripSuffix(raw)
        if fromSign then return fromSign end

        local fromChan = _bodOwnerFromChannel(bestPlot)
        if fromChan then return fromChan end

        local plotSign = bestPlot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        if plotSign then
            local closest, closestDist = nil, BOD_FALLBACK_RADIUS
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr == LP then continue end
                local ch = plr.Character
                local h  = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hm = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not h or not hm or hm.Health <= 0 then continue end
                local d = (h.Position - plotSign.Position).Magnitude
                if d < closestDist then closestDist = d; closest = plr end
            end
            if closest then return closest.DisplayName end
        end

        return nil
    end

    local lastUpdate = 0
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local now = tick()
        if (now - lastUpdate) < 0.15 then return end
        lastUpdate = now

        local ownerName = resolveBaseOwner()
        if ownerName then
            ownerValLbl.Text = ownerName
            ownerValLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180,230,80)
            oPanelStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(180,230,80)
        else
            ownerValLbl.Text = "—"
            ownerValLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170,180,200)
            oPanelStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50,60,90)
        end
        local myChar = LP.Character
        local myHRP  = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr == LP then continue end
            local uid = plr.UserId
            local r   = rowPool[uid]
            if not r then continue end
            local dist = "?"
            if myHRP and plr.Character then
                local h = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if h then dist = math.floor((myHRP.Position - h.Position).Magnitude) .. "m" end
            end
            r.distLbl.Text = dist
            local alive = false
            if plr.Character then
                local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                alive = hum ~= nil and hum.Health > 0
            end
            r.dot.BackgroundColor3 = alive and Color3.fromRGB(180,230,80) or Color3.fromRGB(200,70,70)
            local stealing = plr:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
            if stealing then
                local what = getStealingWhat(plr) or "Unknown"
                r.stealLbl.Text    = "🐾 Stealing: " .. what
                r.stealLbl.Visible = true
                r.rStroke.Color    = Color3.fromRGB(180,230,80)
                r.row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30,40,20)
            else
                r.stealLbl.Visible = false
                r.rStroke.Color    = Color3.fromRGB(50,60,90)
                r.row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24,30,50)
            end
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(2)
            local bp   = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
            local char = LP.Character
            local cape = (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Laser Cape"))
                      or (char and char:FindFirstChild("Laser Cape"))
            if cape then neutralizeTool(cape) end
        end
    end)
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(1.5)
        local bp   = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        local cape = bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Laser Cape")
        if cape then neutralizeTool(cape) end
    end)

    local function nearestTarget()
        local myChar = LP.Character
        local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not myHrp then return nil end
        local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LP and p.Character and isAlive(p.Character) then
                local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if h then
                    local d = (h.Position - myHrp.Position).Magnitude
                    if d < bestD then bestD, best = d, p end
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end

    local _capeHooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local _lastManualShot = 0
    local function hookCape(tool)
        if _capeHooked[tool] then return end
        _capeHooked[tool] = true
        tool.Activated:Connect(function()
            if isFiring or _G.Skyr0CapeAutoAim == false then return end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - _lastManualShot < 0.05 then return end
            _lastManualShot = now
            local target = nearestTarget()
            if target then pcall(aimShootAt, target) end
        end)
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            for _, holder in ipairs({ LP.Character, LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack") }) do
                local cape = holder and holder:FindFirstChild("Laser Cape")
                if cape then hookCape(cape) end
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(aimInitRemotes)
end)

pcall(function() local o = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera:FindFirstChild("Skyr0FaceAway"); if o then o:Destroy() end end)
_G.__Skyr0Stage = "complete"
_G.__Skyr0Ready = true
